### [1] ["The System Will Choose Security Over Humanity Every Time": Understanding Security and Privacy for U.S. Incarcerated Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01370)
*Yael Eiger,Nino Migineishvili,Emi Yoshikawa,Liza Nadtochiy,Kentrell Owens,Franziska Roesner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 美国监狱数字设备使用中的隐私安全风险与权力失衡问题研究


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监狱中数字设备（平板电脑、媒体播放器、信息亭）的普及为囚犯提供了教育、通信和再入社会技能培养的机会，但也带来了新的隐私和安全风险。囚犯对这些设备的使用和合同几乎没有自主权，且被排除在许多消费者保护措施之外，需要研究这些技术相关的安全隐私漏洞及影响使用的权力动态。

Method: 采用焦点小组和访谈方法，对17名系统影响人群（包括前囚犯及其亲属）进行调查，探究设备相关的安全隐私漏洞经历以及影响其使用的权力动态。

Result: 参与者描述了监狱技术中普遍存在的监控、审查和可用性问题，包括不断变化且看似武断的使用政策。这些政策不仅损害了监狱内外的人际关系，还对囚犯用户产生了负面下游影响。

Conclusion: 建议通过促进技术相关决策的问责制、提供数字采购和使用政策的公共监督、以及将实际最终用户（囚犯）纳入设计考虑，来更好地平衡监狱安全关切与系统影响个体的隐私需求。

Abstract: Digital devices like tablets, media players, and kiosks are increasingly deployed in U.S. prisons. These technologies can enable incarcerated people to access education, communicate with loved ones, and develop vital reentry skills. However, they can also introduce new privacy and security risks for incarcerated people who have little agency over their usage and contracts, and are currently carved out of many consumer protection safeguards. To investigate these issues, we conducted focus groups and interviews with system-impacted people (n=17), i.e., those formerly incarcerated, and their relatives, to investigate experiences with device-related security and privacy vulnerabilities and the power dynamics that affect their use. In our findings, participants describe pervasive surveillance, censorship, and usability problems with the technology available to them, including shifting and seemingly arbitrary usage policies. These policies strain relationships both inside and outside prisons and contribute to negative downstream effects for incarcerated users. We recommend ways to better balance prison security concerns with privacy-related needs of system-impacted individuals by promoting accountability for technology-related decisions, providing public oversight of digital purchasing and use policies, and designing digital tools with them -- the actual end-users -- in mind.

</details>


### [2] [Cooking Up Risks: Benchmarking and Reducing Food Safety Risks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01444)
*Weidi Luo,Xiaofei Wen,Tenghao Huang,Hongyi Wang,Zhen Xiang,Chaowei Xiao,Kristina Gligorić,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FoodGuardBench是首个基于FDA指南的全面食品安全基准测试，包含3339个查询，用于评估LLM在食品安全领域的安全性和鲁棒性，揭示了现有模型和防护措施的严重漏洞，并提出了专门的防护模型FoodGuard-4B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地应用于食品制备和健康指导等日常任务，但食品安全是一个高风险领域，不准确或误导性信息可能导致严重的现实危害。当前LLM和安全防护措施缺乏针对特定领域食品危害的严格对齐。

Method: 构建了基于FDA指南的FoodGuardBench基准测试，包含3339个查询，建立了食品安全原则分类体系，并采用代表性越狱攻击（如AutoDAN和PAP）系统评估现有LLM和防护措施。

Result: 评估揭示了三个关键漏洞：1) 当前LLM在食品相关领域的安全对齐稀疏，容易受到少数典型越狱策略攻击；2) 被攻破时，LLM经常生成可操作但有害的指令，无意中赋能恶意行为者；3) 现有基于LLM的防护措施系统性地忽略了这些领域特定威胁，未能检测大量恶意输入。

Conclusion: 为缓解这些漏洞，提出了FoodGuard-4B，这是一个在数据集上微调的专业防护模型，用于保护食品相关领域的LLM，填补了当前LLM安全防护在食品安全领域的空白。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for everyday tasks, including food preparation and health-related guidance. However, food safety remains a high-stakes domain where inaccurate or misleading information can cause severe real-world harm. Despite these risks, current LLMs and safety guardrails lack rigorous alignment tailored to domain-specific food hazards. To address this gap, we introduce FoodGuardBench, the first comprehensive benchmark comprising 3,339 queries grounded in FDA guidelines, designed to evaluate the safety and robustness of LLMs. By constructing a taxonomy of food safety principles and employing representative jailbreak attacks (e.g., AutoDAN and PAP), we systematically evaluate existing LLMs and guardrails. Our evaluation results reveal three critical vulnerabilities: First, current LLMs exhibit sparse safety alignment in the food-related domain, easily succumbing to a few canonical jailbreak strategies. Second, when compromised, LLMs frequently generate actionable yet harmful instructions, inadvertently empowering malicious actors and posing tangible risks. Third, existing LLM-based guardrails systematically overlook these domain-specific threats, failing to detect a substantial volume of malicious inputs. To mitigate these vulnerabilities, we introduce FoodGuard-4B, a specialized guardrail model fine-tuned on our datasets to safeguard LLMs within food-related domains.

</details>


### [3] [Preserving Target Distributions With Differentially Private Count Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01468)
*Nitin Kohli,Paul Laskowski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段差分隐私计数表发布框架，平衡分布准确性、计数准确性和运行时间，引入新的Cyclic Laplace机制和基于epsilon-scales的构造算法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准差分隐私机制在发布计数表时会产生统计偏差，需要新的设计标准来保证分布准确性，同时平衡计数准确性和运行时间

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用专门为计数分布设计的Cyclic Laplace机制生成分布估计；第二阶段使用基于epsilon-scales理论的构造算法生成转移矩阵，其固定点即为隐私化分布

Result: Cyclic Laplace机制优于现有通用差分隐私直方图机制；基于epsilon-scales的构造算法比标准优化算法更高效地生成具有良好性质的转移矩阵；实验显示固定点方法在某些情况下提供了有利的性能权衡

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段框架有效平衡了分布准确性、计数准确性和运行时间，Cyclic Laplace机制和epsilon-scales理论为差分隐私计数表发布提供了新的实用解决方案

Abstract: Differentially private mechanisms are increasingly used to publish tables of counts, where each entry represents the number of individuals belonging to a particular category. A distribution of counts summarizes the information in the count column, unlinking counts from categories. This object is useful for answering a class of research questions, but it is subject to statistical biases when counts are privatized with standard mechanisms. This motivates a novel design criterion we term accuracy of distribution.
  This study formalizes a two-stage framework for privatizing tables of counts that balances accuracy of distribution with two standard criteria of accuracy of counts and runtime. In the first stage, a distribution privatizer generates an estimate for the true distribution of counts. We introduce a new mechanism, called the cyclic Laplace, specifically tailored to distributions of counts, that outperforms existing general-purpose differentially private histogram mechanisms. In the second stage, a constructor algorithm generates a count mechanism, represented as a transition matrix, whose fixed-point is the privatized distribution of counts. We develop a mathematical theory that describes such transition matrices in terms of simple building blocks we call epsilon-scales. This theory informs the design of a new constructor algorithm that generates transition matrices with favorable properties more efficiently than standard optimization algorithms. We explore the practicality of our framework with a set of experiments, highlighting situations in which a fixed-point method provides a favorable tradeoff among performance criteria.

</details>


### [4] [SelfGrader: Stable Jailbreak Detection for Large Language Models using Token-Level Logits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01473)
*Zikai Zhang,Rui Hu,Olivera Kotevska,Jiahao Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SelfGrader：一种基于token级logits的轻量级护栏方法，将越狱检测转化为数值评分问题，通过双视角评分规则实现稳定可解释的安全评分


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有护栏方法依赖内部特征或文本响应检测恶意查询，要么引入显著延迟，要么受文本生成随机性影响。需要克服这些限制，开发更高效的越狱检测方法。

Method: SelfGrader将越狱检测转化为数值评分问题，使用token级logits。通过紧凑数值token集（0-9）评估查询安全性，将logit分布解释为内部安全信号。引入双视角评分规则，同时考虑查询的恶意性和良性，产生稳定可解释的评分。

Result: 在多样化越狱基准测试、多个LLM和SOTA护栏基线实验中，SelfGrader在LLaMA-3-8B上实现ASR降低高达22.66%，同时保持显著更低的内存开销（高达173倍）和延迟（高达26倍）。

Conclusion: SelfGrader作为一种轻量级护栏方法，通过token级logits和双视角评分规则，在保持高效性的同时有效降低越狱攻击成功率，为LLM安全防护提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful tools for answering user queries, yet they remain highly vulnerable to jailbreak attacks. Existing guardrail methods typically rely on internal features or textual responses to detect malicious queries, which either introduce substantial latency or suffer from the randomness in text generation. To overcome these limitations, we propose SelfGrader, a lightweight guardrail method that formulates jailbreak detection as a numerical grading problem using token-level logits. Specifically, SelfGrader evaluates the safety of a user query within a compact set of numerical tokens (NTs) (e.g., 0-9) and interprets their logit distribution as an internal safety signal. To align these signals with human intuition of maliciousness, SelfGrader introduces a dual-perspective scoring rule that considers both the maliciousness and benignness of the query, yielding a stable and interpretable score that reflects harmfulness and reduces the false positive rate simultaneously. Extensive experiments across diverse jailbreak benchmarks, multiple LLMs, and state-of-the-art guardrail baselines demonstrate that SelfGrader achieves up to a 22.66% reduction in ASR on LLaMA-3-8B, while maintaining significantly lower memory overhead (up to 173x) and latency (up to 26x).

</details>


### [5] [EXHIB: A Benchmark for Realistic and Diverse Evaluation of Function Similarity in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01554)
*Yiming Fan,Jun Yeon Won,Ding Zhu,Melih Sirlanci,Mahdi Khalili,Carter Yagemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EXHIB是一个用于二进制函数相似性检测的综合性基准测试，包含5个真实世界数据集，评估了9个代表性模型，揭示了现有方法在固件和语义数据集上存在高达30%的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制函数相似性检测（BFSD）是软件安全的核心问题，但该领域缺乏全面的通用基准测试。现有数据集范围有限，通常只关注狭窄的转换类型或二进制文件类型，未能反映真实世界应用的多样性，导致研究人员难以有效比较不同模型。

Method: 引入EXHIB基准测试，包含5个从真实世界收集的数据集，每个数据集突出BFSD问题空间的不同方面。在EXHIB上评估了9个涵盖多个BFSD范式的代表性模型。

Result: 评估结果显示，与标准设置相比，模型在固件和语义数据集上的性能下降高达30%，揭示了显著的泛化差距。结果表明，对低层和中层二进制变异的鲁棒性不能泛化到高层语义差异。

Conclusion: 当前BFSD评估实践存在关键盲点：对低层和中层二进制变异的鲁棒性不能泛化到高层语义差异。EXHIB基准测试揭示了现有模型的泛化局限性，强调了需要更全面的评估方法。

Abstract: Binary Function Similarity Detection (BFSD) is a core problem in software security, supporting tasks such as vulnerability analysis, malware classification, and patch provenance. In the past few decades, numerous models and tools have been developed for this application; however, due to the lack of a comprehensive universal benchmark in this field, researchers have struggled to compare different models effectively. Existing datasets are limited in scope, often focusing on a narrow set of transformations or types of binaries, and fail to reflect the full diversity of real-world applications.
  We introduce EXHIB, a benchmark comprising five realistic datasets collected from the wild, each highlighting a distinct aspect of the BFSD problem space. We evaluate 9 representative models spanning multiple BFSD paradigms on EXHIB and observe performance degradations of up to 30% on firmware and semantic datasets compared to standard settings, revealing substantial generalization gaps. Our results show that robustness to low- and mid-level binary variations does not generalize to high-level semantic differences, underscoring a critical blind spot in current BFSD evaluation practices.

</details>


### [6] [AI-Assisted Hardware Security Verification: A Survey and AI Accelerator Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01572)
*Khan Thamid Hasan,Md Ajoad Hasan,Nashmin Alam,Md. Touhidul Islam,Upoma Das,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该综述系统总结了AI/LLM在硬件安全验证各阶段的应用进展，包括资产识别、威胁建模、测试计划生成、仿真分析、形式验证和防护措施推理，并通过NVDLA案例展示实践应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着硬件系统复杂度增加，传统安全验证方法面临挑战。AI和LLM技术为自动化验证流程提供了新机遇，能够帮助工程师分析设计、推理潜在威胁并生成验证工件，从而提高验证效率和覆盖度。

Method: 采用文献综述方法，按照硬件安全验证工作流的关键阶段组织文献：资产识别、威胁建模、安全测试计划生成、仿真驱动分析、形式验证和防护措施推理。通过NVIDIA深度学习加速器（NVDLA）作为案例研究，展示这些技术的实际应用。

Result: AI/LLM技术能够显著加速硬件安全验证任务，但研究发现其输出必须基于仿真证据、形式推理和基准驱动评估，以确保可信的硬件安全保障。研究为AI辅助硬件安全验证提供了系统化的框架和实践指导。

Conclusion: AI/LLM自动化可以显著提升硬件安全验证效率，但必须与传统的仿真验证、形式化方法和基准测试相结合，确保验证结果的可信度。未来的研究方向包括提高AI模型在硬件安全领域的专业性和可靠性。

Abstract: As hardware systems grow in complexity, security verification must keep up with them. Recently, artificial intelligence (AI) and large language models (LLMs) have started to play an important role in automating several stages of the verification workflow by helping engineers analyze designs, reason about potential threats, and generate verification artifacts. This survey synthesizes recent advances in AI-assisted hardware security verification and organizes the literature along key stages of the workflow: asset identification, threat modeling, security test-plan generation, simulation-driven analysis, formal verification, and countermeasure reasoning. To illustrate how these techniques can be applied in practice, we present a case study using the open-source NVIDIA Deep Learning Accelerator (NVDLA), a representative modern hardware design. Throughout this study, we emphasize that while AI/LLM-based automation can significantly accelerate verification tasks, its outputs must remain grounded in simulation evidence, formal reasoning, and benchmark-driven evaluation to ensure trustworthy hardware security assurance.

</details>


### [7] [Assertain: Automated Security Assertion Generation Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01583)
*Shams Tarek,Dipayan Saha,Khan Thamid Hasan,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Assertain是一个自动化框架，通过集成RTL设计分析、CWE映射和威胁模型智能，自动生成安全属性和可执行的SystemVerilog断言，显著减少硬件安全验证中的人工工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代SoC设计复杂性增加放大了硬件安全风险，手动指定安全属性成为形式化属性验证的主要瓶颈，需要自动化解决方案来降低人工成本并提高验证效率。

Method: Assertain框架整合RTL设计分析、CWE映射和威胁模型智能，利用大型语言模型配合自反思精炼机制，确保生成的断言既语法正确又语义一致。

Result: 在11个代表性硬件设计上评估，Assertain在正确断言生成、独特CWE覆盖和架构缺陷检测方面分别比GPT-5高出61.22%、59.49%和67.92%，显著扩展了漏洞覆盖范围并提高了断言质量。

Conclusion: Assertain通过自动化安全属性生成，显著减少了硬件安全验证中的人工工作量，同时提高了漏洞覆盖范围和断言质量，为解决硬件安全验证瓶颈提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of modern system-on-chip designs amplifies hardware security risks and makes manual security property specification a major bottleneck in formal property verification. This paper presents Assertain, an automated framework that integrates RTL design analysis, Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) mapping, and threat model intelligence to automatically generate security properties and executable SystemVerilog Assertions. Assertain leverages large language models with a self-reflection refinement mechanism to ensure both syntactic correctness and semantic consistency. Evaluated on 11 representative hardware designs, Assertain outperforms GPT-5 by 61.22%, 59.49%, and 67.92% in correct assertion generation, unique CWE coverage, and architectural flaw detection, respectively. These results demonstrate that Assertain significantly expands vulnerability coverage, improves assertion quality, and reduces manual effort in hardware security verification.

</details>


### [8] [Diffusion-Guided Adversarial Perturbation Injection for Generalizable Defense Against Facial Manipulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01635)
*Yue Li,Linying Xue,Kaiqing Lin,Hanyu Quan,Dongdong Lin,Hui Tian,Hongxia Wang,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AEGIS：首个扩散引导的对抗性人脸图像生成范式，通过潜在空间扰动注入实现身份保护，突破传统像素级约束限制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动防御方法因次优的扰动注入策略和白盒假设限制，在实际场景中效果有限，无法有效应对GAN和扩散模型生成的高质量深度伪造威胁

Method: 提出AEGIS框架，在DDIM去噪轨迹的潜在空间中注入对抗性扰动，解耦扰动幅度与像素级约束；通过可扩展设计支持白盒和黑盒场景，黑盒场景采用梯度估计策略

Result: 在GAN和扩散基深度伪造生成器上的广泛实验表明，AEGIS在保持高感知质量的同时提供强大防御效果；白盒设置下实现稳健操纵破坏，黑盒设置下展示强跨模型可迁移性

Conclusion: AEGIS通过扩散引导的潜在空间扰动注入范式，突破了传统主动防御的峰值裁剪约束限制，为实际场景中的深度伪造防御提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Recent advances in GAN and diffusion models have significantly improved the realism and controllability of facial deepfake manipulation, raising serious concerns regarding privacy, security, and identity misuse. Proactive defenses attempt to counter this threat by injecting adversarial perturbations into images before manipulation takes place. However, existing approaches remain limited in effectiveness due to suboptimal perturbation injection strategies and are typically designed under white-box assumptions, targeting only simple GAN-based attribute editing. These constraints hinder their applicability in practical real-world scenarios. In this paper, we propose AEGIS, the first diffusion-guided paradigm in which the AdvErsarial facial images are Generated for Identity Shielding. We observe that the limited defense capability of existing approaches stems from the peak-clipping constraint, where perturbations are forcibly truncated due to a fixed $L_\infty$-bounded. To overcome this limitation, instead of directly modifying pixels, AEGIS injects adversarial perturbations into the latent space along the DDIM denoising trajectory, thereby decoupling the perturbation magnitude from pixel-level constraints and allowing perturbations to adaptively amplify where most effective. The extensible design of AEGIS allows the defense to be expanded from purely white-box use to also support black-box scenarios through a gradient-estimation strategy. Extensive experiments across GAN and diffusion-based deepfake generators show that AEGIS consistently delivers strong defense effectiveness while maintaining high perceptual quality. In white-box settings, it achieves robust manipulation disruption, whereas in black-box settings, it demonstrates strong cross-model transferability.

</details>


### [9] [Seclens: Role-specific Evaluation of LLM's for security vulnerablity detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01637)
*Subho Halder,Siddharth Saxena,Kashinath Kadaba Shrish,Thiyagarajan M*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecLens-R是一个多利益相关者评估框架，用于LLM漏洞检测，通过35个维度、7个测量类别和5个角色特定权重配置文件，提供比单一指标更全面的模型性能评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测基准将模型性能压缩为单一指标，无法反映不同利益相关者（如CISO、工程主管、AI负责人）的优先级差异。需要一种能够捕捉多利益相关者视角的评估框架。

Method: 提出SecLens-R框架，包含35个共享维度（分7个测量类别），定义5个角色特定权重配置文件（CISO、首席AI官、安全研究员、工程主管、AI-as-Actor）。每个配置文件选择12-16个维度，权重总和为80，产生0-100的决策分数。在406个任务（来自93个开源项目，涵盖10种编程语言和8个OWASP漏洞类别）上评估12个前沿模型，采用代码提示和工具使用两种设置。

Result: 不同利益相关者视角下模型表现差异显著，同一模型的决策分数差异高达31分。例如，Qwen3-Coder在工程主管配置下得A（76.3），但在CISO配置下得D（45.2）；GPT-5.4也显示类似差异。这表明漏洞检测本质上是多目标问题，单一聚合指标会掩盖重要信息。

Conclusion: 漏洞检测需要多利益相关者评估方法。SecLens-R框架通过角色特定权重配置文件，为不同利益相关者提供更有意义的模型性能评估，揭示单一指标无法捕捉的性能差异，有助于更明智的模型选择和部署决策。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for LLM-based vulnerability detection compress model performance into a single metric, which fails to reflect the distinct priorities of different stakeholders. For example, a CISO may emphasize high recall of critical vulnerabilities, an engineering leader may prioritize minimizing false positives, and an AI officer may balance capability against cost. To address this limitation, we introduce SecLens-R, a multi-stakeholder evaluation framework structured around 35 shared dimensions grouped into 7 measurement categories. The framework defines five role-specific weighting profiles: CISO, Chief AI Officer, Security Researcher, Head of Engineering, and AI-as-Actor. Each profile selects 12 to 16 dimensions with weights summing to 80, yielding a composite Decision Score between 0 and 100.
  We apply SecLens-R to evaluate 12 frontier models on a dataset of 406 tasks derived from 93 open-source projects, covering 10 programming languages and 8 OWASP-aligned vulnerability categories. Evaluations are conducted across two settings: Code-in-Prompt (CIP) and Tool-Use (TU). Results show substantial variation across stakeholder perspectives, with Decision Scores differing by as much as 31 points for the same model. For instance, Qwen3-Coder achieves an A (76.3) under the Head of Engineering profile but a D (45.2) under the CISO profile, while GPT-5.4 shows a similar disparity. These findings demonstrate that vulnerability detection is inherently a multi-objective problem and that stakeholder-aware evaluation provides insights that single aggregated metrics obscure.

</details>


### [10] [Contextualizing Sink Knowledge for Java Vulnerability Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01645)
*Fabian Fleischer,Cen Zhang,Joonun Jang,Jeongin Cho,Meng Xu,Taesoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GONDAR是一个基于sink API的Java漏洞模糊测试框架，通过静态分析识别可到达和可利用的sink调用点，然后使用探索和利用两个智能体与覆盖率引导的模糊测试器协同工作，显著提高了Java应用程序漏洞发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Java应用程序存在大量由安全敏感API不安全使用导致的漏洞，如路径遍历和反序列化漏洞。现有模糊测试工具大多忽略了这些漏洞特定的知识，限制了其发现漏洞的有效性。

Method: GONDAR首先通过CWE特定扫描结合LLM辅助的静态过滤识别可到达和可利用的sink调用点。然后部署两个专门智能体与覆盖率引导模糊测试器协同工作：探索智能体通过迭代解决路径约束生成到达目标调用点的输入；利用智能体通过推理和满足漏洞触发条件合成概念验证利用。智能体和模糊测试器持续交换种子和运行时反馈。

Result: 在真实世界Java基准测试中，GONDAR发现的漏洞数量是最先进的Java模糊测试器Jazzer的四倍。在DARPA AI Cyber Challenge中表现出色，并已集成到Linux基金会OpenSSF的OSS-CRS沙盒项目中。

Conclusion: GONDAR通过系统性地利用sink API语义进行针对性漏洞发现，显著提高了Java应用程序漏洞检测效果，为开源软件安全提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Java applications are prone to vulnerabilities stemming from the insecure use of security-sensitive APIs, such as file operations enabling path traversal or deserialization routines allowing remote code execution. These sink APIs encode critical information for vulnerability discovery: the program-specific constraints required to reach them and the exploitation conditions necessary to trigger security flaws. Despite this, existing fuzzers largely overlook such vulnerability-specific knowledge, limiting their effectiveness.
  We present GONDAR, a sink-centric fuzzing framework that systematically leverages sink API semantics for targeted vulnerability discovery. GONDAR first identifies reachable and exploitable sink call sites through CWE-specific scanning combined with LLM-assisted static filtering. It then deploys two specialized agents that work collaboratively with a coverage-guided fuzzer: an exploration agent generates inputs to reach target call sites by iteratively solving path constraints, while an exploitation agent synthesizes proof-of-concept exploits by reasoning about and satisfying vulnerability-triggering conditions. The agents and fuzzer continuously exchange seeds and runtime feedback, complementing each other. We evaluated GONDAR on real-world Java benchmarks, where it discovers four times more vulnerabilities than Jazzer, the state-of-the-art Java fuzzer. Notably, GONDAR also demonstrated strong performance in the DARPA AI Cyber Challenge, and is integrated into OSS-CRS, a sandbox project in The Linux Foundation's OpenSSF, to improve the security of open-source software.

</details>


### [11] [Spike-PTSD: A Bio-Plausible Adversarial Example Attack on Spiking Neural Networks via PTSD-Inspired Spike Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01750)
*Lingxin Jin,Wei Jiang,Maregu Assefa Habtie,Letian Chen,Jinyu Zhan,Xingzhi Zhou,Lin Zuo,Naoufel Werghi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Spike-PTSD攻击框架，模拟PTSD异常神经放电模式，针对SNN的生物学特性进行对抗攻击，在多种数据集和模型上达到99%以上成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SNN具有高能效和生物可塑性，适合嵌入式和安全关键系统，但其对抗鲁棒性研究不足。现有攻击方法往往忽略SNN的生物可塑性动态特性，需要开发更符合SNN生物学特性的对抗攻击框架。

Method: 提出Spike-PTSD攻击框架，模拟PTSD异常神经放电模式：1) 定位决策关键层；2) 通过超激活/低激活特征选择神经元；3) 使用双重目标优化对抗样本。该方法考虑了SNN的生物可塑性动态特性。

Result: 在六个数据集、三种编码类型和四种模型上进行实验，Spike-PTSD攻击成功率超过99%，系统性地破坏了SNN的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Spike-PTSD是一种有效的生物启发式对抗攻击框架，能够利用SNN的生物学特性进行针对性攻击，揭示了SNN在对抗攻击下的脆弱性，为未来SNN安全研究提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are energy-efficient and biologically plausible, ideal for embedded and security-critical systems, yet their adversarial robustness remains open. Existing adversarial attacks often overlook SNNs' bio-plausible dynamics. We propose Spike-PTSD, a biologically inspired adversarial attack framework modeled on abnormal neural firing in Post-Traumatic Stress Disorder (PTSD). It localizes decision-critical layers, selects neurons via hyper/hypoactivation signatures, and optimizes adversarial examples with dual objectives. Across six datasets, three encoding types, and four models, Spike-PTSD achieves over 99% success rates, systematically compromising SNN robustness. Code: https://github.com/bluefier/Spike-PTSD.

</details>


### [12] [Combating Data Laundering in LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01904)
*Muxing Li,Zesheng Ye,Sharon Li,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SDR方法通过推断未知的数据清洗变换并合成类似清洗数据的查询，有效对抗数据清洗攻击，增强LLM训练数据滥用检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据清洗攻击通过变换专有数据的风格形式但保留关键信息来模糊数据来源，使得传统基于性能差异的数据滥用检测方法失效，需要新的对抗措施

Method: 提出合成数据还原(SDR)方法：1) 从黑盒访问的目标LLM推断未知清洗变换的高层目标；2) 通过辅助LLM迭代细化具体细节，合成模拟清洗数据的查询；3) 逐步增强从目标LLM获取的检测信号

Result: 在MIMIR基准测试中，针对多种清洗实践和目标LLM家族(Pythia、Llama2、Falcon)，SDR始终增强数据滥用检测效果，提供对抗数据清洗的实用对策

Conclusion: SDR通过推断清洗变换并合成类似查询，有效对抗数据清洗攻击，为数据权利所有者提供了一种实用的检测方法，即使他们只有原始数据也能检测数据滥用

Abstract: Data rights owners can detect unauthorized data use in large language model (LLM) training by querying with proprietary samples. Often, superior performance (e.g., higher confidence or lower loss) on a sample relative to the untrained data implies it was part of the training corpus, as LLMs tend to perform better on data they have seen during training. However, this detection becomes fragile under data laundering, a practice of transforming the stylistic form of proprietary data, while preserving critical information to obfuscate data provenance. When an LLM is trained exclusively on such laundered variants, it no longer performs better on originals, erasing the signals that standard detections rely on. We counter this by inferring the unknown laundering transformation from black-box access to the target LLM and, via an auxiliary LLM, synthesizing queries that mimic the laundered data, even if rights owners have only the originals. As the search space of finding true laundering transformations is infinite, we abstract such a process into a high-level transformation goal (e.g., "lyrical rewriting") and concrete details (e.g., "with vivid imagery"), and introduce synthesis data reversion (SDR) that instantiates this abstraction. SDR first identifies the most probable goal for synthesis to narrow the search; it then iteratively refines details so that synthesized queries gradually elicit stronger detection signals from the target LLM. Evaluated on the MIMIR benchmark against diverse laundering practices and target LLM families (Pythia, Llama2, and Falcon), SDR consistently strengthens data misuse detection, providing a practical countermeasure to data laundering.

</details>


### [13] [From Component Manipulation to System Compromise: Understanding and Detecting Malicious MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01905)
*Yiheng Huang,Zhijia Zhao,Bihuan Chen,Susheng Wu,Zhuotong Zhou,Yiheng Cao,Xin Hu,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出组件中心视角来理解和检测恶意MCP服务器，构建首个组件中心PoC数据集，开发两阶段行为偏差检测器Connor，在检测恶意MCP服务器方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP标准在促进LLM与外部工具和数据源集成的同时引入了新的攻击向量。现有安全研究按可观察效果分类攻击，忽视了攻击在不同MCP服务器组件间的行为模式，且现有防御措施对多组件攻击或未知恶意行为效果有限。

Method: 1) 构建首个包含114个恶意MCP服务器的组件中心PoC数据集，评估攻击在两个MCP主机和五个LLM上的有效性；2) 提出并实现Connor两阶段行为偏差检测器：执行前分析检测恶意shell命令并提取工具功能意图，执行中分析追踪工具行为轨迹并检测与功能意图的偏差。

Result: 1) 发现组件位置影响攻击成功率，多组件组合通常优于单组件攻击；2) Connor在数据集上达到94.6%的F1分数，比现有最佳方法提升8.9%至59.6%；3) 在实际检测中识别出两个恶意服务器。

Conclusion: 组件中心视角能更深入理解MCP服务器攻击行为，Connor两阶段检测方法能有效识别恶意MCP服务器，包括多组件攻击和未知恶意行为，显著提升检测性能。

Abstract: The model context protocol (MCP) standardizes how LLMs connect to external tools and data sources, enabling faster integration but introducing new attack vectors. Despite the growing adoption of MCP, existing MCP security studies classify attacks by their observable effects, obscuring how attacks behave across different MCP server components and overlooking multi-component attack chains. Meanwhile, existing defenses are less effective when facing multi-component attacks or previously unknown malicious behaviors.
  This work presents a component-centric perspective for understanding and detecting malicious MCP servers. First, we build the first component-centric PoC dataset of 114 malicious MCP servers where attacks are achieved as manipulation over MCP components and their compositions. We evaluate these attacks' effectiveness across two MCP hosts and five LLMs, and uncover that (1) component position shapes attack success rate; and (2) multi-component compositions often outperform single-component attacks by distributing malicious logic. Second, we propose and implement Connor, a two-stage behavioral deviation detector for malicious MCP servers. It first performs pre-execution analysis to detect malicious shell commands and extract each tool's function intent, and then conducts step-wise in-execution analysis to trace each tool's behavioral trajectories and detect deviations from its function intent. Evaluation on our curated dataset indicates that Connor achieves an F1-score of 94.6%, outperforming the state of the art by 8.9% to 59.6%. In real-world detection, Connor identifies two malicious servers.

</details>

### [14] [Disclosure or Marketing? Analyzing the Efficacy of Vendor Self-reports for Vetting Public-sector AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01332)
*Blaine Kuehnert,Nari Johnson,Ravit Dotan,Hoda Heidari*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过定性分析发现，AI FactSheets在公共部门采购中承担多重冲突角色，限制了其作为独立评估工具的有效性，但作为建立信任和持续对话的关系性工具仍具价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于文档的披露已成为负责任AI的核心治理策略，但缺乏关于模型卡、数据表和AI FactSheets等工具在实际中如何生产、解释和使用的实证证据。研究旨在填补这一空白，特别关注公共部门采购中广泛采用的GovAI Coalition FactSheet。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，包括对供应商和公共部门从业者的半结构化访谈，以及对已完成的FactSheets进行系统分析，考察FactSheets的使用方式、揭示的信息及其局限性。

Result: 研究发现FactSheets被要求同时服务于多重且冲突的目的：展示供应商产品、支持评估和尽职调查、促进供应商与机构间的早期对话。这些竞争性期望加上自愿和公开自我披露的结构性约束，限制了FactSheets作为独立评估或风险评估工具的功能。然而，当被视为用于建立信任、共享理解和持续对话的关系性工具时，FactSheets能够帮助创造支持更有意义披露和治理的条件。

Conclusion: AI FactSheets无法作为独立的评估工具有效运作，但作为促进供应商与公共部门之间信任建立、共享理解和持续对话的关系性工具具有重要价值。研究强调了理解这些文档作为关系性工具而非单纯信息披露工具的重要性，为改进AI治理透明度实践提供了实证基础。

Abstract: Documentation-based disclosure has become a central governance strategy for responsible AI, particularly in public-sector procurement. Tools such as model cards, datasheets, and AI FactSheets are increasingly expected to support accountability, risk assessment, and informed decision-making across organizational boundaries. Yet there is limited empirical evidence about how these artifacts are produced, interpreted, and used in practice. In this paper, we present a qualitative study of the GovAI Coalition FactSheet, a widely adopted transparency document designed to support AI procurement and governance in government contexts. Drawing on semi-structured interviews with vendors and public-sector practitioners, alongside a systematic analysis of completed FactSheets, we examine how FactSheets are used, what information they surface, and where they fall short. We find that FactSheets are asked to serve multiple and conflicting purposes simultaneously: showcasing vendor offerings, supporting evaluation and due diligence, and facilitating early-stage dialogue between vendors and agencies. These competing expectations, combined with the structural constraints of voluntary and public self-disclosure, limit the ability of FactSheets to function as standalone evaluation or risk-assessment tools. At the same time, our findings suggest that when understood as relational artifacts used to establish trust, shared understanding, and ongoing dialogue, FactSheets can help create conditions that support more meaningful disclosure and governance over time.

</details>


### [15] [The Weak Signal Cultivation Model: A Human-Centric Framework for Frontline Risk Detection, Signal Tracking, and Proactive Organizational Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01495)
*Maurice Codourey,Emmanuel A. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出弱信号培育模型（WSCM），这是一个以人为本的框架，用于检测、结构化和跟踪一线员工观察到的弱风险信号，通过二维坐标场可视化风险轨迹，建立统一组织词汇连接一线经验与管理决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统风险管理系统往往忽视一线员工观察到的微弱风险信号，这些信号虽然初期不明显但可能具有重要风险增长潜力。需要建立连接一线经验与管理决策的桥梁，通过统一组织词汇改善风险沟通。

Method: 创建弱信号培育场，采用连续[0,10]×[0,10]坐标场，横轴表示当前风险强度（Risk Intensity），纵轴表示风险增长潜力（Risk Growth Potential）。每个识别的信号作为节点定位在场上，形成风险轨迹，随时间推移根据新评估移动。节点在四个区域间移动：问号区（Question Marks）、点燃导火索区（Lit Fuses）、睡猫区（Sleeping Cats）和猫头鹰区（Owls）。

Result: 建立了一个图形化框架，将弱风险信号可视化，使一线员工观察到的微弱信号能够被系统化检测、跟踪和分析。该模型创建了统一组织词汇，连接前线经验与管理决策，为AI支持的风险分析提供概念基础。

Conclusion: WSCM模型为从业者提供了实用工具，也为AI支持的风险分析奠定了概念基础，通过可视化弱信号轨迹和统一风险语言，改善了组织风险识别和沟通能力。

Abstract: This white paper introduces the Weak Signal Cultivation Model (WSCM). WSCM is a human-centric framework for detecting, structuring, and tracking weak risk signals as observed by frontline staff. The model centers on a continuous [0,10] x [0,10] coordinate field--the Weak Signal Cultivation Field, in which each identified signal is positioned as a node on two independent dimensions: its current Risk Intensity (x) and its Risk Growth Potential (y). Represented as a risk locus, nodes move across the field over time as new team assessments or measurements arrive. The locus reflects the signal's trajectory across four possible regions: Question Marks, Lit Fuses, Sleeping Cats, and Owls. Through this graphical approach, bridging risk communication from the frontline experience to management decision-making is made through a single organizational vocabulary. The model introduced in this document is designed to serve as a practitioner tool and a conceptual foundation for AI-supported analytics.

</details>


### [16] [Designing for Patient Voice in Interactive Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01558)
*Yuhao Sun*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文探讨了交互式健康研究中"患者声音"的认知地位问题，指出当前研究将患者经验视为待分析数据而非独立知识，并提出了支持患者撰写或主导经验贡献的基础设施路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式健康研究虽然越来越多地采用参与式和以用户为中心的方法，但患者的亲身经验通常仅被视为待分析的数据，而非具有独立认知价值的知识。这既是包容性问题，也是认知论问题，涉及经验叙述如何被认可和传播。

Method: 作者分析了方法学惯例、作者规范、评审标准和出版格式如何将患者定位为参与者而非证据作者。通过考察医学出版中的患者合作实践（如BMJ、JAMA等期刊），提出了支持患者撰写或主导经验贡献的基础设施路径，并将其作为设计探针来揭示假设和权衡。

Result: 识别了当前研究基础设施中患者经验被边缘化的机制，提出了具体的出版基础设施改进方案，为将患者经验证据纳入学术知识体系提供了可行的实施路径。

Conclusion: 交互式健康社区需要反思其知识基础设施如何能够容纳经验证据与既定研究形式并存。作者邀请社区共同思考如何通过基础设施设计来更好地认可和整合患者的认知贡献。

Abstract: Interactive Health (IH) research increasingly engages patients through participatory and user-centred approaches. However, patients' lived experiences are typically treated more as data to be analysed than as knowledge in their own right. In this paper, I argue that 'patient voice' in the field of IH is both an inclusion issue and an epistemic one. More specifically, it concerns how experiential accounts are recognised and circulated. I examine how methodological conventions, authorship norms, review criteria, and publication formats tend to position patients as participants rather than as authors of evidence. Looking to patient-partnered practices in medical publishing, including The BMJ, JAMA, and British Journal of Sports Medicine, I outline a possible infrastructural pathway for supporting patient-authored or patient-led experiential contributions within the field. I present this as a design probe to surface assumptions and trade-offs. I end this paper by inviting the IH community to reflect on how its knowledge infrastructures might accommodate experiential evidence alongside established research forms.

</details>


### [17] [AromaGen: Interactive Generation of Rich Olfactory Experiences with Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01650)
*Yunge Wen,Awu Chen,Jianing Yu,Jas Brooks,Hiroshi Ishii,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AromaGen：基于多模态LLM的实时通用气味生成可穿戴接口，通过文本或视觉输入生成气味混合物，支持自然语言迭代优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气味与食物、记忆和社交体验的深层联系促使研究者将其引入交互系统，但现有嗅觉接口局限于固定气味盒和预定义生成模式，且大规模嗅觉数据集稀缺限制了AI方法发展

Method: 开发AromaGen可穿戴接口，采用多模态LLM利用潜在嗅觉知识将语义输入映射到12种精选基础气味剂的结构化混合物，通过颈戴式分配器释放，支持通过上下文学习的自然语言反馈进行迭代优化

Result: 在26人对照研究中，AromaGen在零样本生成中匹配人工配制混合物，迭代优化后显著超越，达到与真实食物气味8/10的中位数相似度，并将人工感知度降低到与真实食物相当的水平

Conclusion: AromaGen是实现现实世界交互式气味生成的重要一步，为通信、健康和沉浸式技术开辟了新可能性

Abstract: Smell's deep connection with food, memory, and social experience has long motivated researchers to bring olfaction into interactive systems. Yet most olfactory interfaces remain limited to fixed scent cartridges and pre-defined generation patterns, and the scarcity of large-scale olfactory datasets has further constrained AI-based approaches. We present AromaGen, an AI-powered wearable interface capable of real-time, general-purpose aroma generation from free-form text or visual inputs. AromaGen is powered by a multimodal LLM that leverages latent olfactory knowledge to map semantic inputs to structured mixtures of 12 carefully selected base odorants, released through a neck-worn dispenser. Users can iteratively refine generated aromas through natural language feedback via in-context learning. Through a controlled user study ($N = 26$), AromaGen matches human-composed mixtures in zero-shot generation and significantly surpasses them after iterative refinement, achieving a median similarity of 8/10 to real food aromas and reducing perceived artificiality to levels comparable to real food. AromaGen is a step towards real-world interactive aroma generation, opening new possibilities for communication, wellbeing, and immersive technologies.

</details>


### [18] [Eyes Can't Always Tell: Fusing Eye Tracking and User Priors for User Modeling under AI Advice Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01741)
*Xin Sun,Shu Wei,Ting Pan,Yajing Wang,Jos A. Bosch,Isao Echizen,Abdallah El Ali,Saku Sugawara*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过眼动追踪实验探究AI辅助建议（正确/错误建议）如何影响用户的认知状态（认知负荷和决策信心），发现AI建议会改变眼动信号与认知状态之间的映射关系，且结合用户先验信息可提升跨参与者泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关键决策场景中，建模用户的认知状态对于构建自适应AI系统至关重要。虽然眼动追踪提供了与认知努力相关的非侵入性行为信号，但先前研究未系统考察AI辅助情境（特别是建议可靠性和用户异质性）如何改变眼动信号与认知状态之间的映射关系。

Method: 采用被试内实验室眼动追踪研究设计（N=54），在事实验证任务中设置三种条件：无AI辅助、正确AI建议、错误AI建议。分析条件依赖的自我报告和眼动模式变化，评估基于眼动追踪的用户建模鲁棒性。最后融合眼动特征与用户先验信息（人口统计学、AI素养/经验、技术信任倾向）来改进跨参与者泛化。

Result: 1) AI建议相比无AI条件提高了决策信心；2) 正确AI建议与较低的感知认知负荷和更高效的眼动行为相关；3) 预测建模具有情境敏感性：眼动信号与认知状态之间的关系随AI条件变化而改变；4) 融合眼动特征与用户先验信息可改善跨参与者泛化性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，AI辅助建议会显著改变用户认知状态与眼动行为之间的关系，支持构建情境感知和个性化的用户建模方法，以实现认知对齐的自适应AI系统。这强调了在AI辅助决策系统中考虑情境因素和用户异质性的重要性。

Abstract: Modeling users' cognitive states (e.g., cognitive load and decision confidence) is essential for building adaptive AI in high-stakes decision-making. While eye tracking provides non-invasive behavioral signals correlated with cognitive effort, prior work has not systematically examined how AI assistance contexts, specifically varying advice reliability and user heterogeneity, can alter the mapping between gaze signals and cognitive states. We conducted a within-subject lab eye-tracking study (N=54) on factual verification tasks under three conditions: No-AI, Correct-AI advice, and Incorrect-AI advice. We analyze condition-dependent changes in self-reports and eye-tracking patterns and evaluate the robustness of eye-tracking-based user modeling. Results show that AI advice increases decision confidence compared to No-AI, while Correct-AI is associated with lower perceived cognitive load and more efficient gaze behavior. Crucially, predictive modeling is context-sensitive: the relationship between eye-tracking signals and cognitive states shifts across AI conditions. Finally, fusing eye-tracking features with user priors (demographics, AI literacy/experience, and propensity to trust technology) improves cross-participant generalization. These findings support condition-aware and personalized user modeling for cognitively aligned adaptive AI systems.

</details>


### [19] [ProVega: A Grammar to Ease the Prototyping, Creation, and Reproducibility of Progressive Data Analysis and Visualization Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02096)
*Matteo Filosa,Graziano Blasilli,Emilio Martino,Marco Angelini*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ProVega：基于Vega-Lite的渐进式数据分析和可视化语法，简化PDAV实现，配套Pro-Ex编辑器，验证了11个文献案例和多种渐进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大数据分析需要快速响应，渐进式数据分析和可视化(PDAV)能提供快速交互但实现复杂、难以复现，需要降低使用门槛。

Method: 提出ProVega语法（基于Vega-Lite）简化PDAV工具化，配套Pro-Ex编辑器支持渐进式解决方案的创建和分析。验证方法包括：重新实现11个文献案例（39名用户验证保真度）、展示对多种渐进方法的支持（数据分块、过程分块、混合分块）、专家用户研究。

Result: 成功重新实现11个文献案例并验证保真度，支持多种渐进方法，专家研究证实ProVega和Pro-Ex在真实任务中的有效性。所有材料开源可用。

Conclusion: ProVega降低了PDAV的实现门槛，简化了渐进式可视化的创建，配套工具Pro-Ex进一步提升了开发效率，为大规模数据分析提供了实用的渐进式解决方案框架。

Abstract: Modern data analysis requires speed for massive datasets. Progressive Data Analysis and Visualization (PDAV) emerged as a discipline to address this problem, providing fast response times while maintaining interactivity with controlled accuracy. Yet it remains difficult to implement and reproduce. To lower this barrier, we present ProVega, a Vega-Lite-based grammar that simplifies PDAV instrumentation for both simple visualizations and complex visual environments. Alongside it, we introduce Pro-Ex, an editor designed to streamline the creation and analysis of progressive solutions. We validated ProVega by reimplementing 11 exemplars from the literature-verified for fidelity by 39 users-and demonstrating its support for various progressive methods, including data-chunking, process-chunking, and mixed-chunking. An expert user study confirmed the efficacy of ProVega and the Pro-Ex environment in real-world tasks. ProVega, Pro-Ex, and all related materials are available at https://github.com/XAIber-lab/provega

</details>


### [20] [Designing Transformational Games to Support Socio-ethical Reasoning about Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02154)
*Jaemarie Solyst,Ruth Karen Nakigozi,Chloe Fong,R. Benjamin Shapiro*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文探讨了使用变革性游戏来培养青少年对生成式AI及其伦理问题的批判性素养，通过设计两款整合GenAI工具的游戏来研究游戏元素如何支持社会伦理推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前青少年需要培养批判性AI素养，不仅要理解AI的工作原理，还要了解其局限性和伦理复杂性。然而，设计能够使这些复杂严肃话题变得有趣的学习体验仍然是一个挑战。

Method: 研究者设计并实施了两款游戏：Diversity Duel和Secret Agent，这些游戏将生成式AI工具与游戏元素相结合。研究调查了游戏元素（1）同伴评估、（2）基于约束的创造力和（3）社交推理如何支持关于生成式AI的社会伦理推理。

Result: 参与者能够识别并辩论生成式AI输出中的偏见，将这些模式与现实世界的不平等联系起来，并发展出对偏见的细致理解。参与者进一步认识到提示设计如何塑造AI行为。研究结果表明，具有这些元素的基于小组的游戏可以支持培养批判性AI素养。

Conclusion: 变革性游戏是一种有前景的方法，可以支持青少年学习生成式AI和伦理问题。通过整合同伴评估、基于约束的创造力和社交推理等游戏元素，能够有效促进关于AI的社会伦理推理和批判性素养的发展。

Abstract: There is an increasing need for young people to become critically AI literate, understanding not only how AI works but also its limitations and ethical nuances. Yet, designing learning experiences that make such complex, serious topics engaging remains a challenge. This paper explores transformational games as a promising approach for supporting youth learning about generative AI (GenAI) and ethics. We designed and implemented two games, Diversity Duel and Secret Agent, that integrate GenAI tools with gameplay elements. This work investigates how the games' elements: (1) peer evaluation, (2) constraint-based creativity, and (3) social deduction supported socio-ethical reasoning about GenAI. Participants recognized and debated bias in GenAI outputs, connected these patterns to real-world inequities, and developed nuanced understandings of bias. Participants further came to see how prompt design shapes AI behavior. Our findings suggest that group-based games with these elements can support fostering critical AI literacy.

</details>


### [21] [Visual Decoding Operators: Towards a Compositional Theory of Visualization Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02220)
*Sheng Long,Remco Chang,Eugene Wu,Alex Kale,Matthew Kay*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出将可视化解释操作化为可组合的视觉解码算子序列，通过概率密度函数和累积分布函数图验证，并将学习到的算子应用于散点图均值估计任务，展示了方法的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将可视化分解为基本通道（如角度、位置、长度）来建立效能排序，但缺乏计算结构来预测新可视化×任务组合的性能，需要为每个新组合进行实验。作者希望找到一种能够预测新组合性能的分析单元。

Method: 提出将定量可视化解释操作化为可组合的视觉解码算子序列。使用PDF和CDF图表，将图表特定任务分解为可重用的、图表无关的感知操作，并通过分层贝叶斯建模表征其误差分布。然后通过组合学习到的算子来预测结构不同的任务（Moritz等人的散点图均值估计实验）的性能。

Result: 在预注册分析计划下，使用六种候选策略组合算子并对照经验数据评估。其中一种策略能够捕捉观察响应的偏差和方差，而其他五种策略以可区分的方式失败。该方法成功预测了与学习条件不同的图表类型、图表维度和分析目标的任务性能。

Conclusion: 这种基于解码算子的实证可视化研究方法为生成模型奠定了基础，能够预测在不同观看条件、新图表类型和新任务下的可能解释分布，为可视化理论和研究提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Prior work on perceptual effectiveness has decomposed visualizations into smaller common units (e.g., channels such as angle, position, and length) to establish rankings. While useful, these decompositions lack the computational structure to predict performance for new visualization $\times$ task combinations, requiring new experiments for each. We propose an alternative unit of analysis: operationalizing quantitative visualization interpretation as sequences of composable visual decoding operators. Using probability density function (PDF) and cumulative distribution function (CDF) charts, we examine how chart-specific tasks can be decomposed into reusable, chart-agnostic perceptual operations and characterize their error profiles through hierarchical Bayesian modeling. We then test generalizability by composing learned operators to predict performance on a structurally different task: Moritz et al.'s [35] scatterplot mean-estimation experiment, where the chart type, chart dimensions, and analytic goal all differ from the learning conditions. With a pre-registered analysis plan, we compose operators under six candidate strategies and evaluate each against empirical data with no parameters fit to the response data. One strategy captures both bias and variance of observed responses; five alternatives fail in distinguishable ways. We argue that this decoding-operator-oriented approach to empirical visualization research and theory-building lays the groundwork for generative models that can predict a distribution of likely interpretations under different viewing conditions, new chart types, and new tasks. Free copy of this paper and supplemental materials: https://osf.io/prtfq; experiment interface: https://gleaming-dolphin-799fda.netlify.app/vis-decode-slider.

</details>


### [22] [Impact of Multimodal and Conversational AI on Learning Outcomes and Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02221)
*Karan Taneja,Anjali Singh,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究比较了三种基于教育内容的学习方法：带图文响应的对话AI、纯文本响应的对话AI和传统教科书界面。结果显示，带图文响应的对话AI学习效果最好，纯文本对话AI虽然更吸引人但学习效果最差，揭示了学生感知与学习成果之间的脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态大语言模型为多媒体学习提供了机会，但对话AI在视觉丰富的STEM领域对学习的影响尚未充分探索。同时，多模态性和对话性在生成式AI系统中如何共同影响学习也缺乏理解。

Method: 采用随机对照在线研究(N=124)，比较三种从教科书内容学习生物学的方法：(1)MuDoC：基于文档的对话AI，提供图文交替响应；(2)TexDoC：基于文档的对话AI，仅提供文本响应；(3)DocSearch：具有语义搜索和高亮功能的教科书界面。

Result: 使用MuDoC的学习者获得最高的后测分数和最积极的学习体验。TexDoC虽然比DocSearch被评为更吸引人和易用，但导致最低的后测分数，表明学生感知与学习成果之间存在脱节。

Conclusion: 从认知负荷理论解释，对话性减少了外在认知负荷，而多模态诱导的视觉-言语整合增加了相关认知负荷，从而带来更好的学习成果。当对话性没有多模态补充时，减少的认知努力可能反而会夸大感知理解而不改善学习成果。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer an opportunity to support multimedia learning through conversational systems grounded in educational content. However, while conversational AI is known to boost engagement, its impact on learning in visually-rich STEM domains remains under-explored. Moreover, there is limited understanding of how multimodality and conversationality jointly influence learning in generative AI systems. This work reports findings from a randomized controlled online study (N = 124) comparing three approaches to learning biology from textbook content: (1) a document-grounded conversational AI with interleaved text-and-image responses (MuDoC), (2) a document-grounded conversational AI with text-only responses (TexDoC), and (3) a textbook interface with semantic search and highlighting (DocSearch). Learners using MuDoC achieved the highest post-test scores and reported the most positive learning experience. Notably, while TexDoC was rated as significantly more engaging and easier to use than DocSearch, it led to the lowest post-test scores, revealing a disconnect between student perceptions and learning outcomes. Interpreted through the lens of the Cognitive Load Theory, these findings suggest that conversationality reduces extraneous load, while visual-verbal integration induced by multimodality increases germane load, leading to better learning outcomes. When conversationality is not complemented by multimodality, reduced cognitive effort may instead inflate perceived understanding without improving learning outcomes.

</details>


### [23] [Dark Patterns in Indian Quick Commerce Apps: A Student Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02257)
*Tanish Taneja,Arihant Tripathy,Nimmi Rangaswamy*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨印度快速商务平台如何通过欺骗性设计模式操纵大学生消费，发现尽管学生能识别操纵性UI策略，但时间压力和认知负荷仍导致他们屈服于这些设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着印度快速商务平台重新定义城市消费，使用欺骗性设计暗模式来膨胀订单价值已成为系统性担忧。研究旨在调查印度大学生的"意识-行动差距"，这一群体具有高数字素养但面临显著财务约束。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对16名参与者进行深入探索，分析时间压力和便利驱动架构如何超越价格敏感性。

Result: 研究发现，虽然学生能够识别操纵性UI策略，但由于诱导的认知负荷和将欺骗性营销视为资本主义代价的常态化，他们经常屈服于这些策略。

Conclusion: 建议采用价值敏感设计替代方案，使商业激励与全球南方用户自主权保持一致。

Abstract: As quick commerce (Q-Commerce) platforms in India redefine urban consumption, the use of deceptive design dark patterns to inflate order values has become a systemic concern. This paper investigates the 'Awareness-Action Gap' among Indian university students, a demographic characterized by high digital fluency yet significant financial constraints. Using a qualitative approach with 16 participants, we explore how temporal pressures and convenience-driven architectures override price sensitivity. Our findings reveal that while students recognize manipulative UI tactics, they frequently succumb to them due to induced cognitive load and the normalization of deceptive marketing as a price of capitalism. We conclude by suggesting value-sensitive design alternatives to align commercial incentives with user autonomy in the Global South.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>
