### [24] [Identifying Privacy Concerns in Upcoming Software Release: A Peek into the Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01393)
*Aurek Chattopadhyay,Nan Niu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Pre-PI：一种在软件发布前识别新功能隐私问题的方法，通过模拟用户反馈提前发现隐私风险


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖发布后的用户反馈来识别隐私问题，但此时软件已经发布，开发者无法在发布前主动解决隐私问题。需要一种能在功能发布前预测隐私问题的方法。

Method: Pre-PI方法首先将现有功能映射到语义相似的隐私评论，学习功能与隐私评论的关系。然后为候选功能模拟用户反馈，生成隐私问题的简洁摘要。

Result: 在三个真实应用上的评估显示，Pre-PI相比最先进的Hark方法能生成更多有效的隐私问题，并且能更早地识别这些问题，允许在发布前进行主动缓解。

Conclusion: Pre-PI填补了现有文献的空白，为发布经理和开发者提供了在软件发布前识别隐私问题的支持，实现了隐私问题的主动管理。

Abstract: Identifying the features to be released in the next version of software, from a pool of potential candidates, is a challenging problem. User feedback from app stores is frequently used by software vendors for the evolution of apps across releases. Privacy feedback, although smaller in volume, carries a larger impact influencing app's success. Multiple existing work has focused on summarizing privacy concerns at the app level and has also shown that developers utilize feedback to implement security and privacy-related changes in subsequent releases. However, the current literature offers little support for release managers and developers in identifying privacy concerns prior to release. This gap exists as user reviews are typically available in app stores only after new features of a software system is released. In this paper, we introduce Pre-PI, a novel approach that summarizes privacy concerns for to-be-released features. Our method first maps existing features to semantically similar privacy reviews to learn feature-privacy review relations. We then simulate feedback for candidate features and generate concise summaries of privacy concerns. We evaluate Pre-PI across three real-world apps, and compare it with Hark, a state-of-the-art method that relies on post-release user feedback to identify privacy concerns. Results show that Pre-PI generates additional valid privacy concerns and identifies these concerns earlier than Hark, allowing proactive mitigation prior to release.

</details>


### [25] [AI Engineering Blueprint for On-Premises Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01395)
*Nicolas Weeger,Jakob Winkler,Annika Stiehl,Jóakim von Kistowski,Christian Uhl,Stefan Geißelsöder*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出企业级本地化RAG系统实施蓝图，包含参考架构、参考应用和最佳实践，解决云服务合规限制下的企业部署需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业因数据保护法规无法使用云服务，需要本地化RAG部署方案，但现有架构主要面向云部署且缺乏企业级组件，本地化实施框架稀缺

Method: 提供全面的AI工程蓝图：1) 使用4+1视图模型描述端到端参考架构；2) 本地部署参考应用；3) 工具、开发和CI/CD管道最佳实践；通过案例研究和专家访谈评估实用性

Result: 开发了完整的本地化企业RAG解决方案蓝图，包含架构设计、参考实现和最佳实践指南，已在GitHub公开，正在进行案例研究和专家访谈评估

Conclusion: 该蓝图填补了企业本地化RAG部署的空白，为受合规限制的组织提供了可扩展的实施框架，有助于简化RAG与企业基础设施的集成

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are gaining traction in enterprise settings, yet stringent data protection regulations prevent many organizations from using cloud-based services, necessitating on-premises deployments. While existing blueprints and reference architectures focus on cloud deployments and lack enterprise-grade components, comprehensive on-premises implementation frameworks remain scarce.
  This paper aims to address this gap by presenting a comprehensive AI engineering blueprint for scalable on-premises enterprise RAG solutions. It is designed to address common challenges and streamline the integration of RAG into existing enterprise infrastructure. The blueprint provides: (1) an end-to-end reference architecture described using the 4+1 view model, (2) a reference application for on-premises deployment, and (3) best practices for tooling, development, and CI/CD pipelines, all publicly available on GitHub. Ongoing case studies and expert interviews with industry partners will assess its practical benefits.

</details>


### [26] [Reproducible, Explainable, and Effective Evaluations of Agentic AI for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01437)
*Jingyue Li,André Storhaug*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该立场论文分析了Agentic AI在软件工程中的评估实践，识别了现有方法的局限性，并提出了一套指导原则来改进评估的可重复性、可解释性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Agentic AI在软件工程中的应用日益增多，基于大语言模型的智能体通常作为黑盒运行，难以证明其相对于基线的优越性。此外，评估设计描述中信息缺失导致结果难以复现，需要系统分析当前评估实践并提出改进方案。

Method: 分析了18篇来自ICSE 2026、ICSE 2025、FSE 2025、ASE 2025和ISSTA 2025的论文，识别了Agentic AI在软件工程评估中的主流方法和局限性。提出了具体的指导原则，并通过概念验证案例研究展示了如何利用Thought-Action-Result轨迹进行系统分析。

Result: 分析揭示了当前Agentic AI评估实践中的主要问题：黑盒性质导致难以解释优势，信息缺失阻碍结果复现。提出了核心建议：研究人员应公开Thought-Action-Result轨迹和LLM交互数据，以支持后续研究的系统性分析。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一套指导原则，旨在促进Agentic AI在软件工程中可重复、可解释和有效的评估。通过公开TAR轨迹和LLM交互数据，可以更好地分析不同Agentic AI方法的优缺点，推动该领域研究的透明度和可比性。

Abstract: With the advancement of Agentic AI, researchers are increasingly leveraging autonomous agents to address challenges in software engineering (SE). However, the large language models (LLMs) that underpin these agents often function as black boxes, making it difficult to justify the superiority of Agentic AI approaches over baselines. Furthermore, missing information in the evaluation design description frequently renders the reproduction of results infeasible. To synthesize current evaluation practices for Agentic AI in SE, this study analyzes 18 papers on the topic, published or accepted by ICSE 2026, ICSE 2025, FSE 2025, ASE 2025, and ISSTA 2025. The analysis identifies prevailing approaches and their limitations in evaluating Agentic AI for SE, both in current research and potential future studies. To address these shortcomings, this position paper proposes a set of guidelines and recommendations designed to empower reproducible, explainable, and effective evaluations of Agentic AI in software engineering. In particular, we recommend that Agentic AI researchers make their Thought-Action-Result (TAR) trajectories and LLM interaction data, or summarized versions of these artifacts, publicly accessible. Doing so will enable subsequent studies to more effectively analyze the strengths and weaknesses of different Agentic AI approaches. To demonstrate the feasibility of such comparisons, we present a proof-of-concept case study that illustrates how TAR trajectories can support systematic analysis across approaches.

</details>


### [27] [MOVis: A Visual Analytics Tool for Surfacing Missed Patches Across Software Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01494)
*Jorge Gonzalo Delgado Cervantes,John Businge,Daniel Ogenrwot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MOVis是一个可视化工具，用于帮助开发者理解和传播跨软件变体之间的遗漏修复补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克隆开发产生独立演化的相关软件变体，导致重要修复补丁在一个仓库中应用后，常常在其他变体中缺失。虽然PaReco已发现生态系统中存在数千个这样的遗漏机会补丁，但其文本输出对理解修复应如何传播的支持有限。

Method: MOVis是一个轻量级、交互式的桌面工具，可视化源变体和目标变体之间的遗漏机会补丁。它加载PaReco的MO分类，并并排显示已修复和有错误的代码块，高亮对应区域并暴露阻碍重用的结构差异。

Result: 该工具使开发者能够快速定位遗漏的修复，理解所需的适配，并更有效地维护软件变体间的一致性。工具、复制包和演示视频已公开提供。

Conclusion: MOVis通过可视化方法解决了跨软件变体修复传播的理解障碍，提高了维护一致性的效率。

Abstract: Clone-and-own development produces families of related software variants that evolve independently. As variants diverge, important fixes applied in one repository are often missing in others. PaReco has shown that thousands of such missed opportunity (MO) patches exist across real ecosystems, yet its textual output provides limited support for understanding where and how these fixes should be propagated. We present MOVis, a lightweight, interactive desktop tool that visualizes MO patches between a source and target variant. MOVis loads PaReco's MO classifications and presents patched and buggy hunks side-by-side, highlighting corresponding regions and exposing structural differences that hinder reuse. This design enables developers to quickly locate missed fixes, understand required adaptations, and more efficiently maintain consistency across software variants. The tool, replication package, and demonstration video are available at https://zenodo.org/records/18356553 and https://youtu.be/Ac-gjBxHJ3Y.

</details>


### [28] [From SWE-ZERO to SWE-HERO: Execution-free to Execution-based Fine-tuning for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01496)
*Nikolai Ludwig,Wasi Uddin Ahmad,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-ZERO到SWE-HERO的两阶段SFT配方，通过蒸馏开源前沿LLMs在SWE-bench上取得SOTA结果，包含无执行轨迹学习和执行引导精炼两个阶段


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决软件工程任务中现有方法依赖资源密集型执行的问题，通过两阶段蒸馏策略提升开源模型在代码理解和工程工作流上的性能

Method: 1. SWE-ZERO阶段：使用大规模无执行轨迹学习代码语义和仓库级推理；2. SWE-HERO阶段：应用执行引导的精炼策略，将语义直觉转化为严谨的工程工作流

Result: SWE-HERO-32B在SWE-bench Verified上达到62.2%解决率，创下同类规模开源模型新基准；在仅训练Python的情况下，SWE-bench Multilingual上零样本迁移达到44.1%

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段SFT配方有效提升了开源模型在软件工程任务上的性能，展示了跨语言泛化能力，并发布了包含300k SWE-ZERO和13k SWE-HERO轨迹的数据集

Abstract: We introduce SWE-ZERO to SWE-HERO, a two-stage SFT recipe that achieves state-of-the-art results on SWE-bench by distilling open-weight frontier LLMs. Our pipeline replaces resource-heavy dependencies with an evolutionary refinement strategy: (1) SWE-ZERO utilizes large-scale, execution-free trajectories to master code semantics and repository-level reasoning, and (2) SWE-HERO applies targeted, execution-backed refinement to transition these semantic intuitions into rigorous engineering workflows. Our empirical results set a new benchmark for open-source models of comparable size. We release a dataset of 300k SWE-ZERO and 13k SWE-HERO trajectories distilled from Qwen3-Coder-480B, alongside a suite of agents based on the Qwen2.5-Coder series. Notably, SWE-HERO-32B achieves a 62.2% resolution rate on SWE-bench Verified. Furthermore, despite being trained exclusively on Python, our agents demonstrate robust zero-shot transferability on SWE-bench Multilingual, reaching 44.1% and confirming the paradigm's generalizability across diverse languages.

</details>


### [29] [EpiDroid: Dependency-Guided Recomposition for Deep State Discovery in Mobile GUI Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01522)
*Jiahui Song,Jiaxin Zhi,Kangjia Zhao,Chen Zhi,Junxiao Han,Xinkui Zhao,Nan Wang,Shuiguang Deng,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EpiDroid是一个黑盒可插拔框架，通过语义状态依赖感知增强现有GUI探索器，使用重组-重放范式提升移动应用测试的代码覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用规模和复杂性不断增加，自动化GUI探索对软件质量保证至关重要。现有方法常忽略测试片段间的状态依赖，导致冗余探索且无法访问深层应用状态。

Method: EpiDroid将原始跟踪提炼为稳定测试片段以提取底层依赖，采用重组-重放范式：通过LLM进行影响推理并在高价值可变状态元素上执行确定性重放，通过迭代反馈精化状态依赖图以系统化到达深层应用状态。

Result: 在20个真实应用上的评估显示，EpiDroid持续改进所有基线方法，平均代码覆盖率提升10-28%，相比从相同起点继续基线探索，覆盖增益提高3-4倍。

Conclusion: 依赖引导的重组能够解锁正向探索无法访问的深层状态，且不依赖额外预算，为移动应用GUI测试提供了有效的状态依赖感知解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing scale and complexity of mobile applications make automated GUI exploration essential for software quality assurance. However, existing methods often neglect state dependencies between test fragments, which leads to redundant exploration and prevents access to deep application states. We introduce EpiDroid, a black-box, pluggable framework that augments existing explorers through semantic state dependency awareness. EpiDroid distills raw traces into stable test fragments to extract underlying dependencies. It then employs a Recomposition-Replay paradigm to perform impact reasoning via LLM and deterministic replay on high-value mutable state elements. Through iterative feedback, EpiDroid refines the state-dependency graph to systematically reach deep application states. We integrated EpiDroid into both industrial and state-of-the-art research tools and evaluated it on 20 real-world apps. The results show that EpiDroid consistently improves the performance of all baselines, increasing average code coverage by 10--28\% and delivering 3--4$\times$ more coverage gain compared to continuing the baselines alone from the same starting point. This demonstrates that dependency-guided recomposition unlocks deep states that forward exploration cannot access, irrespective of additional budget.

</details>


### [30] [Mitigating Implicit Inconsistencies in Patch Porting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01680)
*Shengyi Pan,Zhongxin Liu,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MIP是一个自动化补丁移植系统，通过结合LLM、编译器和代码分析工具来解决跨代码库移植补丁时的隐式不一致性问题，相比现有方法能成功处理两倍以上的补丁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将补丁从源代码库移植到其变体（如分支和分支）对于减轻传播的缺陷和漏洞至关重要。现有方法主要处理补丁本地上下文中可见的不一致性，难以处理需要代码库间全局映射知识的隐式不一致性问题。

Method: MIP系统通过LLM、编译器和代码分析工具协同工作，采用不同策略处理不同情况：当源标识符存在于目标代码库时，利用编译器诊断；否则，从两个代码库检索匹配的代码段对作为映射知识进行缓解。

Result: 在跨分支和跨分支补丁移植两个代表性场景的实验中，MIP成功解决的补丁数量是性能最佳基线的两倍以上。与行业合作伙伴的用户研究进一步证明了其实用有效性。

Conclusion: MIP通过结合多种工具和策略有效解决了补丁移植中的隐式不一致性问题，显著提高了自动化补丁移植的成功率，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Promptly porting patches from a source codebase to its variants (e.g., forks and branches) is essential for mitigating propagated defects and vulnerabilities. Recent studies have explored automated patch porting to reduce manual effort and delay, but existing approaches mainly handle inconsistencies visible in a patch's local context and struggle with those requiring global mapping knowledge between codebases. We refer to such non-local inconsistencies as implicit inconsistencies. Implicit inconsistencies pose greater challenges for developers to resolve due to their non-local nature. To address them, we propose MIP, which enables collaboration among an LLM, a compiler, and code analysis utilities. MIP adopts different strategies for different cases: when source identifiers exist in the target codebase, it leverages compiler diagnostics; otherwise, it retrieves matched code segment pairs from the two codebases as mapping knowledge for mitigation. Experiments on two representative scenarios, cross-fork and cross-branch patch porting, show that MIP successfully resolves more than twice as many patches as the best-performing baseline in both settings. A user study with our industry partner further demonstrates its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [31] [Fuzzing REST APIs in Industry: Necessary Features and Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01759)
*Andrea Arcuri,Alexander Poth,Olsi Rrjolli,Philip Garrett,Juan P. Galeotti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在Volkswagen AG工业环境中使用EvoMaster工具测试REST API的经验，分享了从2023到2026年的使用教训，并讨论了在工业环境中成功应用所需的功能改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REST API在工业领域广泛应用，但传统测试方法耗时且需要专业测试工程师。学术原型工具如何集成到工业环境并产生实际影响需要更多研究。本文旨在分享EvoMaster在Volkswagen AG工业环境中的实际应用经验。

Method: 在Volkswagen AG从2023到2026年作为EvoMaster用户进行实际应用，收集关于4个API的工业环境价值反馈，并进行了涉及4家公司11名测试专家的用户研究。

Result: 获得了Volkswagen AG关于4个API的工业环境价值反馈，通过11名测试专家的用户研究收集了实践经验，识别了EvoMaster在工业环境中成功应用所需的功能改进。

Conclusion: 学术原型工具在工业环境中的成功应用需要特定功能改进，本文分享了实际经验教训并识别了仍需解决的真实世界研究挑战，为工具在工业环境中的集成提供了实践指导。

Abstract: REST APIs are widely used in industry, in all different kinds of domains. An example is Volkswagen AG, a German automobile manufacturer. Established testing approaches for REST APIs are time consuming, and require expertise from professional test engineers. Due to its cost and importance, in the scientific literature several approaches have been proposed to automatically test REST APIs. The open-source, search-based fuzzer EvoMaster is one of such tools proposed in the academic literature. However, how academic prototypes can be integrated in industry and have real impact to software engineering practice requires more investigation. In this paper, we report on our experience in using EvoMaster at Volkswagen AG, as an EvoMaster user from 2023 to 2026. We share our learnt lessons, and discuss several features needed to be implemented in EvoMaster to make its use in an industrial context successful. Feedback about value in industrial setups of EvoMaster was given from Volkswagen AG about 4 APIs. Additionally, a user study was conducted involving 11 testing specialists from 4 different companies. We further identify several real-world research challenges that still need to be solved.

</details>


### [32] [TestDecision: Sequential Test Suite Generation via Greedy Optimization and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01799)
*Guoqing Wang,Chengran Yang,Xiaoxuan Zhou,Zeyu Sun,Bo Wang,David Lo,Dan Hao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TestDecision：将LLM转化为神经贪婪专家，通过MDP形式化和子模优化解决开源LLM在测试套件生成中的结构短视问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源LLM在自动化测试生成中缺乏套件级视角，存在结构短视问题，无法基于当前覆盖状态生成具有较大边际增益的新测试。虽然专有模型如GPT-4o表现出色，但高部署成本和数据隐私问题使开源LLM成为学术和工业场景的实际需求。

Method: 将测试套件生成形式化为马尔可夫决策过程，证明其目标具有单调子模性，从而将NP难全局优化松弛为可处理的逐步贪婪过程。提出TestDecision框架，包含：(1) 实现逐步贪婪策略的推理框架；(2) 通过强化学习训练管道，使基础LLM具备最大化边际增益的顺序测试生成能力。

Result: 在ULT基准测试中，TestDecision显著优于现有先进方法：分支覆盖率提升38.15-52.37%，执行通过率提升298.22-558.88%。7B参数骨干模型达到与更大专有LLM GPT-5.2相当的性能。相比基础LLM发现58.43-95.45%更多bug，在LiveCodeBench上表现出优越泛化能力。

Conclusion: TestDecision通过将测试套件生成形式化为MDP并利用子模优化，成功将LLM转化为神经贪婪专家，解决了开源LLM的结构短视问题，能够构建高质量的测试套件，在覆盖率和bug发现方面表现优异。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of LLMs, automated software testing is witnessing a paradigm shift. While proprietary models like GPT-4o demonstrate impressive capabilities, their high deployment costs and data privacy concerns make open-source LLMs the practical imperative for many academic and industrial scenarios. In the field of automated test generation, it has evolved to iterative workflows to construct test suites based on LLMs. When utilizing open-source LLMs, we empirically observe they lack a suite-level perspective, suffering from structural myopia-failing to generate new tests with large marginal gain based on the current covered status. In this paper, from the perspective of sequences, we formalize test suite generation as a MDP and demonstrate that its objective exhibits monotone submodularity, which enables an effective relaxation of this NP-hard global optimization into a tractable step-wise greedy procedure. Guided by this insight, we propose TestDecision, which transforms LLMs into neural greedy experts. TestDecision consists of two synergistic components: (1) an inference framework which implements test suite construction following a step-wise greedy strategy; and (2) a training pipeline of reinforcement learning which equips the base LLM with sequential test generation ability to maximize marginal gain. Comprehensive evaluations on the ULT benchmark demonstrate that TestDecision significantly outperforms existing advanced methods. It brings an improvement between 38.15-52.37% in branch coverage and 298.22-558.88% in execution pass rate over all base models, achieving a comparable performance on 7B backbone with a much larger proprietary LLM GPT-5.2. Furthermore, TestDecision can find 58.43-95.45% more bugs than vanilla base LLMs and exhibit superior generalization on LiveCodeBench, proving its capability to construct high-quality test suites.

</details>


### [33] [Can Large Language Models Model Programs Formally?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01851)
*Zhiyong Chen,Jialun Cao,Jiarong Wu,Chang Xu,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Model-Bench：一个用于评估和改进LLMs程序建模能力的基准测试和配套流程，将Python程序转换为可验证的模型检查规范


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字时代，通过形式化验证确保软件的正确性、安全性和可靠性至关重要。模型检查因自动程序建模困难而较少受到关注，需要填补这一研究空白

Method: 提出Model-Bench基准测试和配套流程，包含从三个知名基准（HumanEval、MBPP、LiveCodeBench）衍生的400个Python程序，将其建模为可由配套模型检查器验证的模型检查规范

Result: 广泛实验揭示了LLMs在程序建模方面的显著局限性，并提供了有启发性的研究方向

Conclusion: Model-Bench为评估和改进LLMs的程序建模能力提供了重要工具，填补了模型检查领域的研究空白

Abstract: In the digital age, ensuring the correctness, safety, and reliability of software through formal verification is paramount, particularly as software increasingly underpins critical infrastructure. Formal verification, split into theorem proving and model checking, provides a feasible and reliable path. Unlike theorem proving, which yields notable advances, model checking has been less focused due to the difficulty of automatic program modeling. To fill this gap, we introduce Model-Bench, a benchmark and an accompanying pipeline for evaluating and improving LLMs' program modeling capability by modeling Python programs into verification-ready model checking specifications checkable by its accompanying model checker. Model-Bench comprises 400 Python programs derived from three well-known benchmarks (HumanEval, MBPP, and LiveCodeBench). Our extensive experiments reveal significant limitations in LLMs' program modeling and further provide inspiring directions.

</details>


### [34] [APITestGenie: Generating Web API Tests from Requirements and API Specifications with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02039)
*André Pereira,Bruno Lima,João Pascoal Faria*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: APITestGenie：基于LLM、RAG和提示工程的自动化API集成测试生成工具，能够从业务需求和OpenAPI规范直接生成测试脚本，显著减少人工工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统严重依赖Web API，但创建有意义且可执行的测试脚本仍然主要依赖人工，耗时且容易出错。需要自动化解决方案来从业务需求和API规范直接生成测试。

Method: APITestGenie结合大型语言模型(LLM)、检索增强生成(RAG)和提示工程技术，从业务需求和OpenAPI规范自动生成API集成测试。工具使用RAG检索相关API信息，通过精心设计的提示指导LLM生成测试脚本。

Result: 在10个真实API（包括汽车领域工业伙伴的8个API，约1000个活跃端点）上评估。经过最多三次尝试，工具能为89%的业务需求生成语法和语义有效的测试脚本。部分生成的测试揭示了API中先前未知的缺陷，包括端点间的集成问题。统计分析显示API复杂性和业务需求详细程度是影响成功率的主要因素，API文档详细程度也影响结果。

Conclusion: APITestGenie能有效自动化API测试生成，显著减少编写验收测试的人工工作量，并改善测试与业务需求的对齐。行业从业者反馈显示强烈的采用兴趣，证实了工具的实际价值和潜力。

Abstract: Modern software systems rely heavily on Web APIs, yet creating meaningful and executable test scripts remains a largely manual, time-consuming, and error-prone task. In this paper, we present APITestGenie, a novel tool that leverages Large Language Models (LLMs), Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), and prompt engineering to automatically generate API integration tests directly from business requirements and OpenAPI specifications. We evaluated APITestGenie on 10 real-world APIs, including 8 APIs comprising circa 1,000 live endpoints from an industrial partner in the automotive domain. The tool was able to generate syntactically and semantically valid test scripts for 89\% of the business requirements under test after at most three attempts. Notably, some generated tests revealed previously unknown defects in the APIs, including integration issues between endpoints. Statistical analysis identified API complexity and level of detail in business requirements as primary factors influencing success rates, with the level of detail in API documentation also affecting outcomes. Feedback from industry practitioners confirmed strong interest in adoption, substantially reducing the manual effort in writing acceptance tests, and improving the alignment between tests and business requirements.

</details>


### [35] [A Model-Driven Digital Twin for the Systematic Improvement of DevOps Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02077)
*Achref Samoud,Sara Aissat,Francis Bordeleau*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出基于BPMN的CI/CD管道数字孪生模型，将原始配置转换为结构化流程表示，支持可视化分析、故障追踪和版本比较


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI/CD管道日益复杂，现有工具（执行日志和静态图）对管道行为、故障和版本演化的结构化分析支持有限

Method: 使用BPMN作为建模骨干，构建CI/CD管道数字孪生架构，将原始配置转换为结构化高级流程表示，支持可视化抽象、故障追踪和系统化版本比较

Result: 基于已验证的数字孪生架构原则和先前构建优化与异常检测工作，为软件交付过程提供模块化、可扩展的基础，已在开源CI/CD项目中验证

Conclusion: 该数字孪生框架支持DevOps过程的监控和演化分析，为集成高级分析和规范性服务提供基础，未来工作将扩展至更广泛的DevOps生命周期过程

Abstract: CI/CD pipelines are central to DevOps practices, yet their growing complexity makes them increasingly difficult to interpret, analyze, and systematically evolve. Existing tooling primarily offers execution logs and static graph representations, providing limited support for structured analysis of pipeline behavior, failures, and version-to-version evolution. This paper presents a model-driven Digital Twin (DT) for CI/CD pipelines that leverages BPMN as a model-ing backbone to transform raw CI configurations into structured, higher-level process representations. The proposed DT architecture enables visual abstraction of pipeline structure, failure tracing, and systematic version comparison, supporting both monitoring and evolution analysis of DevOps processes. Building upon validated DT architectural principles and prior work on build optimization and anomaly detection, the framework provides a modular, extensible foundation for integrating advanced analytical and prescriptive services into software delivery processes. The approach is validated using open-source CI/CD projects, and ongoing work targets the integration of additional improvement services and the extension of the DT to broader DevOps lifecycle processes.

</details>


### [36] [Automated Functional Testing for Malleable Mobile Application Driven from User Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02079)
*Yuying Wang,Kaifeng Huang,Hao Deng,Zhiyuan Sun,Jinxuan Zhou,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个基于LLM的用户需求驱动GUI测试生成框架，用于验证个性化可配置移动应用功能的正确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可配置系统、自适应推荐系统和可塑GUI通常针对特定软件设计，缺乏通用性。作者设想实现每个用户可配置的移动应用，让终端用户能够指定需求并通过LLM代码生成自动实现，但需要解决自动化测试生成的挑战来可靠验证用户指定功能的正确性。

Method: 提出一个用户需求驱动的GUI测试生成框架，通过增量式导航UI、触发期望功能，并构建LLM引导的预言机来验证正确性。框架支持验证用户指定功能的存在和正确性。

Result: 构建了一个包含六个流行移动应用的基准测试集，包含正确和错误的用户请求功能。实验表明该框架能有效验证每个用户的功能，并且在实际部署中具有实用性。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了将移动应用开发从产品经理驱动转向终端用户驱动范式的可行性，为实现个性化可配置移动应用提供了关键技术支撑。

Abstract: Software malleability allows applications to be easily changed, configured, and adapted even after deployment. While prior work has explored configurable systems, adaptive recommender systems, and malleable GUIs, these approaches are often tailored to specific software and lack generalizability. In this work, we envision per-user malleable mobile applications, where end-users can specify requirements that are automatically implemented via LLM-based code generation. However, realizing this vision requires overcoming the key challenge of designing automated test generation that can reliably verify both the presence and correctness of user-specified functionalities. We propose \tool, a user-requirement-driven GUI test generation framework that incrementally navigates the UI, triggers desired functionalities, and constructs LLM-guided oracles to validate correctness. We build a benchmark spanning six popular mobile applications with both correct and faulty user-requested functionalities, demonstrating that \tool effectively validates per-user features and is practical for real-world deployment. Our work highlights the feasibility of shifting mobile app development from a product-manager-driven to an end-user-driven paradigm.

</details>


### [37] [A Case For Host Code Guided GPU Data Race Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02106)
*Ajay Nayak,Anubhab Ghosh,Arkaprava Basu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: HGRD是一种新的静态分析技术，通过综合分析CPU和GPU代码来准确检测GPU程序中的数据竞争，显著减少误报


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU程序中的数据竞争威胁GPU加速软件栈的可靠性。现有动态技术需要竞争在测试中显现才能检测，会遗漏关键竞争；静态技术虽然能捕获这些竞争，但由于保守地假设变量/参数值，会产生大量误报

Method: HGRD利用一个关键观察：启动GPU内核的主机（CPU）代码包含关于GPU内核参数在执行期间可能取值的语义信息。通过综合分析CPU和GPU代码，HGRD能够准确检测真实竞争同时最小化误报

Result: 与最先进的动态技术（如iGUARD）相比，HGRD不会遗漏任何真实竞争；与静态技术（如GPUVerify和FaialAA）相比，HGRD不会产生误报，而其他技术会产生数十个误报

Conclusion: 通过利用CPU代码中的语义信息进行综合分析，HGRD能够准确检测GPU程序中的数据竞争，在检测真实竞争和减少误报方面都优于现有技术

Abstract: Data races in GPU programs pose a threat to the reliability of GPU-accelerated software stacks. Prior works proposed various dynamic (runtime) and static (compile-time) techniques to detect races in GPU programs. However, dynamic techniques often miss critical races, as they require the races to manifest during testing. While static ones can catch such races, they often generate numerous false alarms by conservatively assuming values of variables/parameters that cannot ever occur during any execution of the program.
  We make a key observation that the host (CPU) code that launches GPU kernels contains crucial semantic information about the values that the GPU kernel's parameters can take during execution. Harnessing this hitherto overlooked information helps accurately detect data races in GPU kernel code. We create HGRD, a new state-of-the-art static analysis technique that performs a holistic analysis of both CPU and GPU code to accurately detect a broad set of true races while minimizing false alarms. While SOTA dynamic techniques, such as iGUARD, miss many true races, HGRD misses none. On the other hand, static techniques such as GPUVerify and FaialAA raise tens of false alarms, where HGRD raises none.

</details>


### [38] [Semantic Evolution over Populations for LLM-Guided Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02134)
*Cuong Chi Le,Minh Le-Anh,Cuong Duc Van,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EvolRepair：一种基于种群语义进化的LLM程序修复框架，将传统遗传算法与LLM语义能力结合，通过行为一致性分组、结构化失败模式引导和互补修复洞察重组来提升修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的迭代修复方法在保持修复假设多样性、识别语义相关修复族、组合互补部分修复、利用结构化失败信息以及逃离结构缺陷搜索区域等方面存在不足，需要更有效的搜索框架。

Method: 提出EvolRepair框架，将LLM-based APR重新表述为语义进化算法。用LLM和结构化执行反馈驱动的语义感知组件替代传统遗传算法的语法操作符，将候选修复组织成行为一致性分组，通过重组跨种群的互补修复洞察来合成更强候选，利用结构化失败模式引导搜索方向。

Result: 实验表明EvolRepair在修复效果上显著优于现有的基于LLM的APR方法。

Conclusion: EvolRepair通过语义进化框架有效解决了现有LLM-based APR方法的局限性，将遗传算法的搜索范式与LLM的语义能力相结合，实现了更有效的程序修复。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently shown strong potential for automated program repair (APR), particularly through iterative refinement that generates and improves candidate patches. However, state-of-the-art iterative refinement LLM-based APR approaches cannot fully address challenges, including maintaining useful diversity among repair hypotheses, identifying semantically related repair families, composing complementary partial fixes, exploiting structured failure information, and escaping structurally flawed search regions. In this paper, we propose a Population-Based Semantic Evolution framework for APR iterative refinement, called EvolRepair, that formulates LLM-based APR as a semantic evolutionary algorithm. EvolRepair reformulates the search paradigm of classic genetic algorithm for APR, but replaces its syntax-based operators with semantics-aware components powered by LLMs and structured execution feedback. Candidate repairs are organized into behaviorally coherent groups, enabling the algorithm to preserve diversity, reason over repair families, and synthesize stronger candidates by recombining complementary repair insights across the population. By leveraging structured failure patterns to guide search direction, EvolRepair can both refine promising repair strategies and shift toward alternative abstractions when necessary. Our experiments show that EvolRepair substantially improves repair effectiveness over existing LLM-based APR approaches.

</details>


### [39] [LLMs as Idiomatic Decompilers: Recovering High-Level Code from x86-64 Assembly for Dart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02278)
*Raafat Abualazm,Ayman Abo Elhassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 小型专用LLM在Dart和Swift等现代语言反编译中表现良好，4B模型在Dart反编译上达到71.3 CODEBLEU，接近480B代码模型性能，且能生成可读性强的代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前反编译研究主要关注C语言，而现代语言如Dart和Swift的反编译尚未充分探索。需要研究小型专用LLM是否能在这些语言上实现高质量、符合语言习惯的反编译。

Method: 使用小型专用LLM作为Dart和Swift的习惯性反编译器。研究两种数据增强方法：1)使用同语言合成示例；2)使用相关语言(如Swift)的人工编写示例。采用CODEBLEU评估代码可读性，compile@k评估语法正确性。

Result: 在73个Dart函数测试集上，4B专用模型达到71.3 CODEBLEU(95% CI 65.5-77.1)，接近480B代码模型的73.1。在34个自然Dart函数子集上，compile@k5达到79.4%，而基础模型为64.7%。Swift训练数据在8B模型上有帮助，但在4B模型上无效，表明存在跨语言迁移的能力阈值。

Conclusion: 小型专用模型能够以最小计算成本生成可读性强、符合语言习惯的Dart代码，具有有意义的标识符。跨语言迁移需要足够的模型容量，4B模型可能未达到有效迁移的阈值。

Abstract: Translating machine code into human-readable high-level languages is an open research problem in reverse engineering. Despite recent advancements in LLM-based decompilation to C, modern languages like Dart and Swift are unexplored. In this paper, we study the use of small specialized LLMs as an idiomatic decompiler for such languages. Additionally, we investigate the augmentation of training data using synthetic same-language examples, and compare it against adding human-written examples using related-language (Swift -> Dart). We apply CODEBLEU to evaluate the decompiled code readability and compile@k to measure the syntax correctness. Our experimental results show that on a 73-function Dart test dataset (representing diverse complexity levels), our 4B specialized model achieves 71.3 CODEBLEU (95% CI 65.5-77.1), approximately comparable to a ~480B code model (73.1; 67.4-78.8). On a subset of 34 natural Dart functions, it reaches compile@k5 = 79.4% (Wilson 95% CI 63.2-89.7), vs. 64.7% (47.9-78.5) for the base model; the difference is suggestive but not statistically significant at 0.05. Our results indicate that adding Swift training data helps at 8B but not at 4B, suggesting a capacity threshold for effective cross-lingual transfer. Our experimental results show that small specialized models can generate readable, idiomatic Dart with meaningful identifiers while using minimal compute.

</details>

### [40] [Simulating Realistic LiDAR Data Under Adverse Weather for Autonomous Vehicles: A Physics-Informed Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01254)
*Vivek Anand,Bharat Lohani,Rakesh Mishra,Gaurav Pandey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PICWGAN框架，通过物理约束建模信号衰减和几何一致性退化，生成恶劣天气下的真实LiDAR数据，缩小仿真与真实差距


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以捕捉LiDAR信号与大气现象之间的复杂相互作用，导致恶劣天气条件下的LiDAR仿真不真实，需要缩小仿真与真实数据之间的差距

Method: 提出物理信息学习框架PICWGAN，集成物理驱动的约束来建模信号衰减和几何一致性退化，通过物理信息学习流程生成真实的恶劣天气LiDAR数据

Result: 在真实数据集（CADC雪天、Boreas雨天、VoxelScape）上评估，方法能准确模拟真实强度模式；定量指标（MSE、SSIM、KL散度、Wasserstein距离）显示强度分布统计一致；使用该框架增强数据训练的模型在下游3D目标检测中优于基线，性能接近真实数据训练的模型

Conclusion: 提出的方法能有效提高LiDAR数据的真实感，在恶劣天气条件下实现鲁棒的感知性能，缩小了仿真与真实数据之间的差距

Abstract: Accurate LiDAR simulation is crucial for autonomous driving, especially under adverse weather conditions. Existing methods struggle to capture the complex interactions between LiDAR signals and atmospheric phenomena, leading to unrealistic representations. This paper presents a physics-informed learning framework (PICWGAN) for generating realistic LiDAR data under adverse weather conditions. By integrating physicsdriven constraints for modeling signal attenuation and geometryconsistent degradations into a physics-informed learning pipeline, the proposed method reduces the sim-to-real gap. Evaluations on real-world datasets (CADC for snow, Boreas for rain) and the VoxelScape dataset show that our approach closely mimics realworld intensity patterns. Quantitative metrics, including MSE, SSIM, KL divergence, and Wasserstein distance, demonstrate statistically consistent intensity distributions. Additionally, models trained on data enhanced by our framework outperform baselines in downstream 3D object detection, achieving performance comparable to models trained on real-world data. These results highlight the effectiveness of the proposed approach in improving the realism of LiDAR data and enabling robust perception under adverse weather conditions.

</details>


### [41] [Bench2Drive-VL: Benchmarks for Closed-Loop Autonomous Driving with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01259)
*Xiaosong Jia,Yuqian Shao,Zhenjie Yang,Qifeng Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bench2Drive-VL扩展了Bench2Drive，为基于视觉语言模型的自动驾驶引入了首个闭环评估基准，通过自动生成多样化、行为驱动的问答对来评估模型在累积误差和分布外状态下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM4AD基准仅在开环环境下通过静态问答数据集评估模型，无法评估模型在累积误差和分布外输入下的性能，而闭环评估在自动驾驶中已被广泛认为是更可靠的验证方法。

Method: 1) DriveCommenter：闭环生成器，自动为CARLA中所有驾驶情况生成多样化、行为驱动的问答对，包括之前无法评估的严重偏离路线和越野情况；2) 统一协议和接口：允许现代VLM直接接入Bench2Drive闭环环境与传统智能体比较；3) 灵活推理和控制框架：支持多格式视觉输入和可配置的基于图的思维链执行；4) 完整的开发生态系统。

Result: 构建了全面的VLM4AD闭环基准测试平台，所有代码和标注数据集均已开源，为视觉语言模型在自动驾驶中的评估提供了首个闭环评估解决方案。

Conclusion: Bench2Drive-VL填补了VLM4AD领域闭环评估的空白，通过自动生成多样化、行为驱动的评估内容，能够更可靠地评估视觉语言模型在自动驾驶场景中的实际性能，特别是在累积误差和分布外状态下的表现。

Abstract: With the rise of vision-language models (VLM), their application for autonomous driving (VLM4AD) has gained significant attention. Meanwhile, in autonomous driving, closed-loop evaluation has become widely recognized as a more reliable validation method than open-loop evaluation, as it can evaluate the performance of the model under cumulative errors and out-of-distribution inputs. However, existing VLM4AD benchmarks evaluate the model`s scene understanding ability under open-loop, i.e., via static question-answer (QA) dataset. This kind of evaluation fails to assess the VLMs performance under out-of-distribution states rarely appeared in the human collected datasets.To this end, we present Bench2Drive-VL, an extension of Bench2Drive that brings closed-loop evaluation to VLM-based driving, which introduces: (1) DriveCommenter, a closed-loop generator that automatically generates diverse, behavior-grounded question-answer pairs for all driving situations in CARLA,including severe off-route and off-road deviations previously unassessable in simulation. (2) A unified protocol and interface that allows modern VLMs to be directly plugged into the Bench2Drive closed-loop environment to compare with traditional agents. (3) A flexible reasoning and control framework, supporting multi-format visual inputs and configurable graph-based chain-of-thought execution. (4) A complete development ecosystem. Together, these components form a comprehensive closed-loop benchmark for VLM4AD. All codes and annotated datasets are open sourced.

</details>


### [42] [Open-loop POMDP Simplification and Safe Skipping of Replanning with Formal Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01352)
*Da Kong,Vadim Indelman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种具有形式性能保证的自适应开环简化框架，通过拓扑信念树在开环和闭环规划间自适应切换，显著降低POMDP规划复杂度，并提供可计算边界保证最优动作识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP为不确定性下的决策提供了原则性数学框架，但精确解计算不可行。现有方法在计算复杂性和性能保证之间存在权衡，需要既能降低计算复杂度又能提供形式化性能保证的解决方案。

Method: 提出自适应开环简化框架：1）通过拓扑信念树自适应交织开环和闭环规划；2）推导可高效计算的边界，为简化提供形式化保证；3）提出安全跳过重规划的执行框架；4）开发实用在线求解器（采样基求解器和随时求解器）。

Result: 实证结果显示计算速度显著提升，同时保持可证明的性能保证。该框架首次解决了具有形式性能保证的跳过重规划问题，推进了POMDP规划的可处理性和效率。

Conclusion: 该工作通过自适应开环简化框架解决了POMDP计算不可行性问题，提供了形式化性能保证和显著计算加速，为POMDP规划的实际应用提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) provide a principled mathematical framework for decision-making under uncertainty. However, the exact solution to POMDPs is computationally intractable. In this paper, we address the computational intractability by introducing a novel framework for adaptive open-loop simplification with formal performance guarantees. Our method adaptively interleaves open-loop and closed-loop planning via a topology-based belief tree, enabling a significant reduction in planning complexity. The key contribution lies in the derivation of efficiently computable bounds which provide formal guarantees and can be used to ensure that our simplification can identify the immediate optimal action of the original POMDP problem. Our framework therefore provides computationally tractable performance guarantees for macro-actions within POMDPs. Furthermore, we propose a novel framework for safely skipping replanning during execution, supported by theoretical guarantees on multi-step open-loop action sequences. To the best of our knowledge, this framework is the first to address skipping replanning with formal performance guarantees. Practical online solvers for our proposed simplification are developed, including a sampling-based solver and an anytime solver. Empirical results demonstrate substantial computational speedups while maintaining provable performance guarantees, advancing the tractability and efficiency of POMDP planning.

</details>


### [43] [A soft and lightweight fabric-based pneumatic interface for multimodal fingertip tactile feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01390)
*Rui Chen,Daniele Leonardis,Antonio Frisoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于织物的气动驱动指尖触觉设备，通过热封TPU涂层织物制造，重量仅2.1克，支持无线操作，能实现多种触觉模式的精确渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可穿戴指尖触觉设备难以同时实现充分的触觉输出、低质量、简单制造和无束缚便携性，需要一种新的技术方案来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用织物基气动驱动技术，通过计算机数控热封工艺制造热塑性聚氨酯涂层织物的四个气动腔室，形成柔软贴合接口，配合腕戴式控制单元实现无线操作。

Result: 设备重量仅2.1克，机械和动态特性测试确认其能产生足够的力、位移和带宽用于指尖触觉渲染；15名参与者的心理物理研究显示，在接触配置、定向滑动和振动触觉频率三种触觉模式上的分类准确率超过90%。

Conclusion: 织物基气动驱动技术为轻量、低成本、多模态的指尖触觉接口提供了一条可行的技术路线。

Abstract: Wearable fingertip haptic devices are critical for realistic interaction in virtual reality, augmented reality, and teleoperation, yet existing approaches struggle to simultaneously achieve adequate tactile output, low mass, simple fabrication, and untethered portability. Here we show that fabric-based pneumatic actuation can address this gap. Our device comprises four pneumatic chambers fabricated from thermoplastic polyurethane-coated fabric via computer numerical control heat-sealing, yielding a soft, conformable interface weighing 2.1 g that operates untethered with a wrist-mounted control unit. Mechanical and dynamic characterization confirms that the fabric actuators produce sufficient force, displacement, and bandwidth for fingertip tactile rendering. A psychophysical study with 15 participants demonstrates classification accuracy exceeding 90% across three distinct tactile modes -- contact configuration, directional sliding, and vibrotactile frequency. These findings establish fabric-based pneumatic actuation as a viable technology route for lightweight, low-cost, and multimodal fingertip haptic interfaces.

</details>


### [44] [Learning When to See and When to Feel: Adaptive Vision-Torque Fusion for Contact-Aware Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01414)
*Jiuzhou Lei,Chang Liu,Yu She,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文比较了不同力/扭矩-视觉融合策略在基于扩散的机器人操作策略中的表现，并提出了一种自适应融合方法，在非接触阶段忽略力信号，在接触阶段自适应利用视觉和扭矩信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于视觉的策略在机器人操作中表现良好，但在接触密集和力敏感的任务中，纯视觉感知不足，需要力/扭矩信号提供接触动力学、对齐和交互质量的关键信息。现有多种融合策略缺乏系统比较。

Method: 提出自适应融合策略：在非接触阶段忽略力/扭矩信号，在接触阶段自适应利用视觉和扭矩信息。在基于扩散的操纵策略框架内比较不同融合方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法比最强基线在成功率上提高了14%，突出了接触感知多模态融合对机器人操作的重要性。

Conclusion: 自适应力/扭矩-视觉融合策略能有效提升接触密集任务的性能，接触感知的多模态融合对机器人操作至关重要。

Abstract: Vision-based policies have achieved a good performance in robotic manipulation due to the accessibility and richness of visual observations. However, purely visual sensing becomes insufficient in contact-rich and force-sensitive tasks where force/torque (F/T) signals provide critical information about contact dynamics, alignment, and interaction quality. Although various strategies have been proposed to integrate vision and F/T signals, including auxiliary prediction objectives, mixture-of-experts architectures, and contact-aware gating mechanisms, a comparison of these approaches remains lacking. In this work, we provide a comparison study of different F/T-vision integration strategies within diffusion-based manipulation policies. In addition, we propose an adaptive integration strategy that ignores F/T signals during non-contact phases while adaptively leveraging both vision and torque information during contact. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms the strongest baseline by 14% in success rate, highlighting the importance of contact-aware multimodal fusion for robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [45] [PRO-SPECT: Probabilistically Safe Scalable Planning for Energy-Aware Coordinated UAV-UGV Teams in Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02142)
*Roger Fowler,Cahit Ikbal Er,Benjamin Johnsenberg,Yasin Yazicioglu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PRO-SPECT算法，为UAV-UGV团队在随机环境中生成风险约束的能量感知路径规划方案，保证任务失败概率不超过用户指定风险水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设确定性旅行时间或使用固定鲁棒性边界，无法有效处理随机环境中的能量约束问题。需要开发能够量化并约束整个任务失败概率的方法，同时支持在线重规划以适应干扰。

Method: 将问题建模为混合整数规划，提出PRO-SPECT多项式时间算法。该算法将旅行时间建模为随机变量，通过风险约束限制能量耗尽概率，支持离线规划和在线重规划。

Result: 提供了解决方案可行性和时间复杂度的理论分析。通过数值比较和仿真验证了算法性能，证明能够生成满足风险约束的规划方案。

Conclusion: PRO-SPECT算法能够为UAV-UGV团队在随机环境中生成风险有界的能量感知规划，支持在线适应干扰，为实际应用提供了理论保证和实用解决方案。

Abstract: We consider energy-aware planning for an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) and unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) team operating in a stochastic environment. The UAV must visit a set of air points in minimum time while respecting energy constraints, relying on the UGV as a mobile charging station. Unlike prior work that assumed deterministic travel times or used fixed robustness margins, we model travel times as random variables and bound the probability of failure (energy depletion) across the entire mission to a user-specified risk level. We formulate the problem as a Mixed-Integer Program and propose PRO-SPECT, a polynomial-time algorithm that generates risk-bounded plans. The algorithm supports both offline planning and online re-planning, enabling the team to adapt to disturbances while preserving the risk bound. We provide theoretical results on solution feasibility and time complexity. We also demonstrate the performance of our method via numerical comparisons and simulations.

</details>


### [46] [Low-Burden LLM-Based Preference Learning: Personalizing Assistive Robots from Natural Language Feedback for Users with Paralysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01463)
*Keshav Shankar,Dan Ding,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于自然语言反馈的低负担离线框架，将非结构化语言直接转化为确定性机器人控制策略，用于物理辅助机器人个性化行为生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏好学习方法（如穷举成对比较）会给重度运动障碍用户带来严重的身体和认知疲劳，需要一种低负担的方法来个性化物理辅助机器人的行为

Method: 使用基于职业治疗实践框架（OTPF）的大型语言模型，将模糊的人类语音解码为明确的生理和心理需求，然后映射到透明决策树中，并通过"LLM-as-a-Judge"自动验证代码结构安全性

Result: 在10名瘫痪成人的模拟备餐研究中验证，自然语言方法显著降低了用户工作负担，独立临床专家确认生成策略安全且准确反映用户偏好

Conclusion: 提出的框架成功将自然语言反馈转化为安全的机器人控制策略，为物理辅助机器人提供了一种低负担的个性化方法，显著改善了用户体验

Abstract: Physically Assistive Robots (PARs) require personalized behaviors to ensure user safety and comfort. However, traditional preference learning methods, like exhaustive pairwise comparisons, cause severe physical and cognitive fatigue for users with profound motor impairments. To solve this, we propose a low-burden, offline framework that translates unstructured natural language feedback directly into deterministic robotic control policies. To safely bridge the gap between ambiguous human speech and robotic code, our pipeline uses Large Language Models (LLMs) grounded in the Occupational Therapy Practice Framework (OTPF). This clinical reasoning decodes subjective user reactions into explicit physical and psychological needs, which are then mapped into transparent decision trees. Before deployment, an automated "LLM-as-a-Judge" verifies the code's structural safety. We validated this system in a simulated meal preparation study with 10 adults with paralysis. Results show our natural language approach significantly reduces user workload compared to traditional baselines. Additionally, independent clinical experts confirmed the generated policies are safe and accurately reflect user preferences.

</details>


### [47] [Integrated Identification of Collaborative Robots for Robot Assisted 3D Printing Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01991)
*Alessandro Dimauro,Davide Tebaldi,Fabio Pini,Luigi Biagiotti,Francesco Leali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型的集成参数辨识方法，用于提高机器人辅助增材制造系统的精度和控制性能，通过五步法确保参数物理一致性，并在6自由度协作机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助增材制造虽然克服了传统笛卡尔系统的尺寸和运动学限制，但机器人动态复杂性增加带来了精度、控制和误差预测的挑战。协作机器人通常存在传感和编程限制，需要可靠的动态模型来提升性能。

Method: 提出集成参数辨识的五步方法：1) 几何和惯性分析；2) 摩擦参数辨识；3) 控制器参数辨识；4) 剩余参数辨识；5) 确保参数物理一致性。该方法整合了机器人、执行器和控制器的参数辨识。

Result: 在6自由度协作机器人的热塑性挤出工艺案例研究中验证了该方法。实验结果表明，辨识模型与实际机器人的结果匹配良好，证明了该方法能有效提升机器人辅助3D打印过程的精度、控制和误差预测能力。

Conclusion: 提出的集成参数辨识方法能够为机器人辅助增材制造系统建立可靠的动态模型，即使在协作机器人存在传感和编程限制的情况下也能确保参数物理一致性，从而显著提升制造过程的精度和控制性能。

Abstract: In recent years, the integration of additive manufacturing (AM) and industrial robotics has opened new perspectives for the production of complex components, particularly in the automotive sector. Robot-assisted additive manufacturing processes overcome the dimensional and kinematic limitations of traditional Cartesian systems, enabling non-planar deposition and greater geometric flexibility. However, the increasing dynamic complexity of robotic manipulators introduces challenges related to precision, control, and error prediction. This work proposes a model-based approach equipped with an integrated identification procedure of the system's parameters, including the robot, the actuators and the controllers. We show that the integrated modeling procedure allows to obtain a reliable dynamic model even in the presence of sensory and programming limitations typical of collaborative robots. The manipulator's dynamic model is identified through an integrated five step methodology: starting with geometric and inertial analysis, followed by friction and controller parameters identification, all the way to the remaining parameters identification. The proposed procedure intrinsically ensures the physical consistency of the identified parameters. The identification approach is validated on a real world case study involving a 6-Degrees-Of-Freedom (DoFs) collaborative robot used in a thermoplastic extrusion process. The very good matching between the experimental results given by actual robot and those given by the identified model shows the potential enhancement of precision, control, and error prediction in Robot Assisted 3D Printing Processes.

</details>


### [48] [Efficient Equivariant Transformer for Self-Driving Agent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01466)
*Scott Xu,Dian Chen,Kelvin Wong,Chris Zhang,Kion Fallah,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveGATr：一种基于几何代数的SE(2)-等变Transformer架构，用于自动驾驶中的智能体行为建模，避免了传统方法中二次复杂度的相对位置编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的智能体行为建模需要考虑多种对称性，包括智能体顺序的置换等变性和整个场景的SE(2)等变性。传统Transformer方法通过显式的成对相对位置编码实现SE(2)等变性，但这带来了智能体数量的二次计算成本，限制了在大规模场景和批量大小下的可扩展性。

Method: 提出DriveGATr架构，将场景元素编码为2D投影几何代数ℝ*_{2,0,1}中的多向量，使用等变Transformer块堆栈进行处理。关键创新在于使用标准注意力机制在多向量之间建模几何关系，无需显式的成对相对位置编码，从而降低计算复杂度。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上的实验表明，DriveGATr在交通模拟任务上达到与最先进方法相当的性能，并在性能与计算成本之间建立了更优的帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: DriveGATr通过几何代数表示实现了SE(2)-等变的智能体建模，避免了传统方法中二次复杂度的相对位置编码，在保持性能的同时显著提高了计算效率，为大规模自动驾驶场景建模提供了更可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurately modeling agent behaviors is an important task in self-driving. It is also a task with many symmetries, such as equivariance to the order of agents and objects in the scene or equivariance to arbitrary roto-translations of the entire scene as a whole; i.e., SE(2)-equivariance. The transformer architecture is a ubiquitous tool for modeling these symmetries. While standard self-attention is inherently permutation equivariant, explicit pairwise relative positional encodings have been the standard for introducing SE(2)-equivariance. However, this approach introduces an additional cost that is quadratic in the number of agents, limiting its scalability to larger scenes and batch sizes. In this work, we propose DriveGATr, a novel transformer-based architecture for agent modeling that achieves SE(2)-equivariance without the computational cost of existing methods. Inspired by recent advances in geometric deep learning, DriveGATr encodes scene elements as multivectors in the 2D projective geometric algebra $\mathbb{R}^*_{2,0,1}$ and processes them with a stack of equivariant transformer blocks. Crucially, DriveGATr models geometric relationships using standard attention between multivectors, eliminating the need for costly explicit pairwise relative positional encodings. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate that DriveGATr is comparable to the state-of-the-art in traffic simulation and establishes a superior Pareto front for performance vs computational cost.

</details>


### [49] [Distal-Stable Beam for Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01490)
*Ryouichi Saito,Takahiro Koide,Yuya Tanaka,Yasutaka Nakashima,Motoji Yamamoto,Ayato Kanada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种新型结构单元——远端稳定梁，通过纯几何设计实现强刚度梯度，在保持中间段柔顺性的同时确保远端高刚性，为连续体机器人提供同时具备安全性和精度的新设计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人适用于受限环境，但存在远端刚度低的问题，导致在外载荷作用下产生较大姿态误差。现有设计难以同时实现柔顺性和远端稳定性。

Method: 提出远端稳定梁结构单元，由两根平行杆和一根收敛杆通过导向盘约束构成，引入几何耦合机制抑制变形模式并保持远端姿态。该设计通过纯几何方式实现刚度梯度，无需主动刚度调节。

Result: 实验表明，远端刚度比中心刚度高12倍，相比传统悬臂梁提升约100倍。该机制能在保持柔顺性的同时实现远端稳定性，为连续体机器人提供了新的设计途径。

Conclusion: 远端稳定梁通过几何设计实现了强刚度梯度，为需要同时具备安全性和精度的连续体机器人提供了创新的被动解决方案，无需主动刚度调节即可实现柔顺性与远端稳定性的平衡。

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for constrained environments but suffer from low distal stiffness, resulting in large posture errors under external loads. In this paper, we propose a novel structural primitive, the Distal-Stable Beam, which achieves a strong stiffness gradient through purely geometric design, maintaining compliance in the intermediate section while ensuring high distal rigidity. The structure consists of two parallel rods and one convergent rod constrained by guide disks, introducing geometric coupling that suppresses deformation modes and preserves distal posture. Experiments show that the distal stiffness is 12 times higher than at the center, corresponding to an approximately 100-fold improvement over a conventional cantilever beam. The proposed mechanism enables simultaneous compliance and distal stability without active stiffness modulation, providing a new design approach for continuum robots requiring both safety and precision.

</details>


### [50] [AnchorVLA: Anchored Diffusion for Efficient End-to-End Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01567)
*Jia Syuen Lim,Zhizhen Zhang,Peter Bohm,Brendan Tidd,Zi Huang,Yadan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnchorVLA：基于扩散的视觉语言动作策略，通过锚定扩散和轻量级自校正机制，在保持动作多样性的同时实现低延迟闭环控制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作中需要同时保持多种可行的动作模型并在执行时保持反应性。扩散策略虽然能建模多模态动作分布，但完整的迭代去噪在控制时成本高昂；动作分块虽然能分摊推理成本，但会导致部分开环行为和漂移累积

Method: 结合轻量级VLA适配骨干和锚定扩散动作头，在锚定轨迹附近进行局部去噪，使用截断扩散计划；引入测试时自校正机制，通过轻量级残差校正模块在部署时进行高频、每步调整

Result: 在多样化移动操作任务中，AnchorVLA在干扰和分布偏移下提高了成功率和稳定性，同时保持低延迟推理

Conclusion: AnchorVLA通过锚定扩散和自校正机制，在保持扩散策略多模态优势的同时解决了推理延迟和动作分块漂移问题，实现了高效、鲁棒的移动操作控制

Abstract: A central challenge in mobile manipulation is preserving multiple plausible action models while remaining reactive during execution. A bottle in a cluttered scene can often be approached and grasped in multiple valid ways. Robust behavior depends on preserving this action diversity while remaining reactive as the scene evolves. Diffusion policies are appealing because they model multimodal action distributions rather than collapsing to one solution. But in practice, full iterative denoising is costly at control time. Action chunking helps amortize inference, yet it also creates partially open-loop behavior, allowing small mismatches to accumulate into drift. We present AnchorVLA, a diffusion-based VLA policy for mobile manipulation built on the core insight that when sampling begins near a plausible solution manifold, extensive denoising is unnecessary to recover multimodal, valid actions. AnchorVLA combines a lightweight VLA adaptation backbone with an anchored diffusion action head, which denoises locally around anchor trajectories using a truncated diffusion schedule. This retains multimodal action generation while reducing inference cost for closed-loop control. Crucially, to mitigate chunking-induced drift, we introduce a test-time self-correction mechanism via a lightweight residual correction module that makes high-frequency, per-step adjustments during rollout. Across diverse mobile manipulation tasks, AnchorVLA improves success and stability under disturbances and distribution shifts while maintaining low-latency inference. The source code is made available at https://github.com/jason-lim26/AnchorVLA.

</details>


### [51] [Boosting Vision-Language-Action Finetuning with Feasible Action Neighborhood Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01570)
*Haochen Niu,Kanyu Zhang,Shuyu Yin,Qinghai Guo,Peilin Liu,Fei Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAN引导的正则化方法，通过高斯先验使VLA模型输出分布与可行动作邻域几何对齐，提升机器人操作任务的样本效率和泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人操作中，每个状态都存在可行动作邻域而非单一正确动作，但现有VLA训练方法直接从语言设置继承，未利用FAN特性，导致泛化能力差和样本效率低

Method: 引入FAN引导的正则化器，通过高斯先验使模型输出分布与FAN几何对齐，促进在偏好方向和幅度周围的局部平滑单峰预测

Result: 在强化微调(RFT)和监督微调(SFT)实验中，方法显著提升了样本效率和成功率，在分布内和分布外场景均有改善

Conclusion: 通过与物理操作内在动作容差对齐，FAN引导的正则化为样本高效、可泛化的VLA适应提供了原则性和实用方法

Abstract: In real-world robotic manipulation, states typically admit a neighborhood of near-equivalent actions. That is, for each state, there exist a feasible action neighborhood (FAN) rather than a single correct action, within which motions yield indistinguishable progress. However, prevalent VLA training methodologies are directly inherited from linguistic settings and do not exploit the FAN property, thus leading to poor generalization and low sample efficiency. To address this limitation, we introduce a FAN-guided regularizer that shapes the model's output distribution to align with the geometry of FAN. Concretely, we introduce a Gaussian prior that promotes locally smooth and unimodal predictions around the preferred direction and magnitude. In extensive experiments across both reinforced finetuning (RFT) and supervised finetuning (SFT), our method achieves significant improvement in sample efficiency, and success rate in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios. By aligning with the intrinsic action tolerance of physical manipulation, FAN-guided regularization provides a principled and practical method for sample-efficient, and generalizable VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [52] [Smooth Feedback Motion Planning with Reduced Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01614)
*Aref Amiri,Steven M. LaValle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在单元分解上改进反馈运动规划的方法，通过启发式对齐局部向量场和构建最大星形链来生成更直接的轨迹，显著减少路径弯曲和控制能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于单元分解的反馈运动规划算法生成的路径通常存在不必要的弯曲，导致运动速度较慢且控制能耗较高，需要一种计算高效的方法来改善这一问题。

Method: 1) 引入启发式方法系统地对齐和分配局部向量场以产生更直接的轨迹；2) 提出新颖的几何算法构建围绕目标的最大星形链，形成大的"漏斗"区域，在其中可以安全应用最优的直接到目标的控制律。

Result: 仿真显示该方法生成明显更直接的路径，平均减少总弯曲91.40%，平均降低LQR控制能耗45.47%。与基于采样和优化的规划器比较，证实了时间效率和鲁棒性优势。

Conclusion: 该方法在计算上高效，能显著改善基于单元分解的反馈运动规划的质量，虽然适用于任何有限维单纯复形，但实际应用集中在低维配置空间（d≤3），其中单纯分解计算可行。

Abstract: Feedback motion planning over cell decompositions provides a robust method for generating collision-free robot motion with formal guarantees. However, existing algorithms often produce paths with unnecessary bending, leading to slower motion and higher control effort. This paper presents a computationally efficient method to mitigate this issue for a given simplicial decomposition. A heuristic is introduced that systematically aligns and assigns local vector fields to produce more direct trajectories, complemented by a novel geometric algorithm that constructs a maximal star-shaped chain of simplexes around the goal. This creates a large ``funnel'' in which an optimal, direct-to-goal control law can be safely applied. Simulations demonstrate that our method generates measurably more direct paths, reducing total bending by an average of 91.40\% and LQR control effort by an average of 45.47\%. Furthermore, comparative analysis against sampling-based and optimization-based planners confirms the time efficacy and robustness of our approach. While the proposed algorithms work over any finite-dimensional simplicial complex embedded in the collision-free subset of the configuration space, the practical application focuses on low-dimensional ($d\le3$) configuration spaces, where simplicial decomposition is computationally tractable.

</details>


### [53] [AURA: Multimodal Shared Autonomy for Real-World Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01659)
*Yukai Ma,Honglin He,Selina Song,Wayne Wu,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AURA是一个辅助城市机器人自主导航框架，将高层人类指令与低层AI控制解耦，通过空间感知指令编码器对齐多模态信息，显著减少接管频率并提升导航稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂城市环境中的长时程导航依赖持续人工操作，导致疲劳、效率降低和安全问题。现有共享自主方法要求人类与AI在同一动作空间操作，造成高认知负荷。

Method: 提出AURA多模态框架，将城市导航分解为高层人类指令和低层AI控制。包含空间感知指令编码器对齐视觉空间上下文与人类指令。构建MM-CoS大规模数据集用于训练。

Result: 仿真和真实世界实验表明，AURA能有效遵循人类指令，减少手动操作工作量，提高导航稳定性，支持在线适应。在类似接管条件下，接管频率降低超过44%。

Conclusion: AURA通过解耦高层指令与低层控制，显著改善了城市环境中的人机协作导航，降低了认知负荷和接管频率，为共享自主系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon navigation in complex urban environments relies heavily on continuous human operation, which leads to fatigue, reduced efficiency, and safety concerns. Shared autonomy, where a Vision-Language AI agent and a human operator collaborate on maneuvering the mobile machine, presents a promising solution to address these issues. However, existing shared autonomy methods often require humans and AI to operate within the same action space, leading to high cognitive overhead. We present Assistive Urban Robot Autonomy (AURA), a new multi-modal framework that decomposes urban navigation into high-level human instruction and low-level AI control. AURA incorporates a Spatial-Aware Instruction Encoder to align various human instructions with visual and spatial context. To facilitate training, we construct MM-CoS, a large-scale dataset comprising teleoperation and vision-language descriptions. Experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate that AURA effectively follows human instructions, reduces manual operation effort, and improves navigation stability, while enabling online adaptation. Moreover, under similar takeover conditions, our shared autonomy framework reduces the frequency of takeovers by more than 44%. Demo video and more detail are provided in the project page.

</details>


### [54] [Bridging Large-Model Reasoning and Real-Time Control via Agentic Fast-Slow Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01681)
*Jiayi Chen,Shuai Wang,Guangxu Zhu,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Fast-Slow Planning分层框架，通过感知-决策-轨迹生成-控制解耦，结合VLM、LLM和经典规划算法，提升自动驾驶系统在扰动下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在局限性：LLM直接生成轨迹脆弱、难以验证且延迟高；在线调整MPC目标混合了慢速决策与快速控制，接口模糊。需要将语义意图映射到可靠实时控制，同时保持可解释性。

Method: 提出分层框架：1) Perception2Decision：车载VLM检测器将场景压缩为以自我为中心的拓扑结构，云端LLM决策器映射为符号化驾驶指令；2) Decision2Trajectory：Semantic-Guided A*将语言指令嵌入软成本到经典搜索中，Agentic Refinement模块基于反馈和记忆调整规划器超参数；3) MPC实时跟踪轨迹，困难情况下使用云端引导参考。

Result: 在CARLA仿真实验中，相比纯MPC和A*引导MPC基线，Agentic Fast-Slow Planning在扰动下鲁棒性显著提升：横向偏差降低达45%，完成时间减少超过12%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过跨自然时间尺度的感知、推理、规划和控制解耦，实现了语义意图到可靠实时控制的映射，在保持可解释性的同时提升了自动驾驶系统的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Large foundation models enable powerful reasoning for autonomous systems, but mapping semantic intent to reliable real-time control remains challenging. Existing approaches either (i) let Large Language Models (LLMs) generate trajectories directly - brittle, hard to verify, and latency-prone - or (ii) adjust Model Predictive Control (MPC) objectives online - mixing slow deliberation with fast control and blurring interfaces. We propose Agentic Fast-Slow Planning, a hierarchical framework that decouples perception, reasoning, planning, and control across natural timescales. The framework contains two bridges. Perception2Decision compresses scenes into ego-centric topologies using an on-vehicle Vision-Language Model (VLM) detector, then maps them to symbolic driving directives in the cloud with an LLM decision maker - reducing bandwidth and delay while preserving interpretability. Decision2Trajectory converts directives into executable paths: Semantic-Guided A* embeds language-derived soft costs into classical search to bias solutions toward feasible trajectories, while an Agentic Refinement Module adapts planner hyperparameters using feedback and memory. Finally, MPC tracks the trajectories in real time, with optional cloud-guided references for difficult cases. Experiments in CARLA show that Agentic Fast-Slow Planning improves robustness under perturbations, reducing lateral deviation by up to 45% and completion time by over 12% compared to pure MPC and an A*-guided MPC baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/cjychenjiayi/icra2026_AFSP.

</details>


### [55] [3-D Relative Localization for Multi-Robot Systems with Angle and Self-Displacement Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01703)
*Chenyang Liang,Liangming Chen,Baoyi Cui,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于机器人间内角和自位移测量的三维相对定位框架，包含线性定位理论和抗噪声的MAP估计器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决存在测量噪声时基于机器人间局部测量的相对定位挑战

Method: 1) 提出分布式线性相对定位算法和可定位性充分条件；2) 将线性计算过程重构为流形上的加权总体最小二乘优化问题；3) 结合神经密度估计器处理先验概率密度未知问题；4) 设计边缘化机制控制计算成本

Result: 机器人能够以纯线性方式确定邻居的相对位置和方向，WTLS提供更精确的初始值减少局部最优风险，边缘化机制确保计算成本恒定

Conclusion: 提出的三维相对定位框架通过系统方法有效处理测量噪声，实现精确且计算高效的相对定位

Abstract: Realizing relative localization by leveraging inter-robot local measurements is a challenging problem, especially in the presence of measurement noise. Motivated by this challenge, in this paper we propose a novel and systematic 3-D relative localization framework based on inter-robot interior angle and self-displacement measurements. Initially, we propose a linear relative localization theory comprising a distributed linear relative localization algorithm and sufficient conditions for localizability. According to this theory, robots can determine their neighbors' relative positions and orientations in a purely linear manner. Subsequently, in order to deal with measurement noise, we present an advanced Maximum a Posterior (MAP) estimator by addressing three primary challenges existing in the MAP estimator. Firstly, it is common to formulate the MAP problem as an optimization problem, whose inherent non-convexity can result in local optima. To address this issue, we reformulate the linear computation process of the linear relative localization algorithm as a Weighted Total Least Squares (WTLS) optimization problem on manifolds. The optimal solution of the WTLS problem is more accurate, which can then be used as initial values when solving the optimization problem associated with the MAP problem, thereby reducing the risk of falling into local optima. The second challenge is the lack of knowledge of the prior probability density of the robots' relative positions and orientations at the initial time, which is required as an input for the MAP estimator. To deal with it, we combine the WTLS with a Neural Density Estimator (NDE). Thirdly, to prevent the increasing size of the relative positions and orientations to be estimated as the robots continuously move when solving the MAP problem, a marginalization mechanism is designed, which ensures that the computational cost remains constant.

</details>


### [56] [Hi-LOAM: Hierarchical Implicit Neural Fields for LiDAR Odometry and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01720)
*Zhiliu Yang,Jianyuan Zhang,Lianhui Zhao,Jinyu Dai,Zhu Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hi-LOAM是一个多尺度隐式神经激光雷达里程计与建图框架，通过自监督学习实现高保真度的大规模复杂场景重建和姿态估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LOAM框架要么依赖监督信号，要么缺乏重建保真度，难以准确描绘大规模复杂场景的细节。需要克服这些限制，实现高保真度的自监督激光雷达定位与建图。

Method: Hi-LOAM接收激光雷达点云输入，基于八叉树结构在多个层级的哈希表中学习和存储分层潜在特征，通过浅层MLP将这些多尺度特征解码为有符号距离值进行建图。姿态估计采用无对应关系的扫描到隐式匹配范式，将当前扫描注册到子地图中。整个训练过程完全自监督。

Result: 在多个真实世界和合成数据集上的广泛实验表明，Hi-LOAM在有效性和泛化能力方面优于现有最先进方法，展现了卓越的性能。

Conclusion: Hi-LOAM通过多尺度隐式神经表示和自监督学习，实现了高保真度的激光雷达定位与建图，能够准确描绘大规模复杂场景的细节，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: LiDAR Odometry and Mapping (LOAM) is a pivotal technique for embodied-AI applications such as autonomous driving and robot navigation. Most existing LOAM frameworks are either contingent on the supervision signal, or lack of the reconstruction fidelity, which are deficient in depicting details of large-scale complex scenes. To overcome these limitations, we propose a multi-scale implicit neural localization and mapping framework using LiDAR sensor, called Hi-LOAM. Hi-LOAM receives LiDAR point cloud as the input data modality, learns and stores hierarchical latent features in multiple levels of hash tables based on an octree structure, then these multi-scale latent features are decoded into signed distance value through shallow Multilayer Perceptrons (MLPs) in the mapping procedure. For pose estimation procedure, we rely on a correspondence-free, scan-to-implicit matching paradigm to estimate optimal pose and register current scan into the submap. The entire training process is conducted in a self-supervised manner, which waives the model pre-training and manifests its generalizability when applied to diverse environments. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world and synthetic datasets demonstrate the superior performance, in terms of the effectiveness and generalization capabilities, of our Hi-LOAM compared to existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [57] [Causal Scene Narration with Runtime Safety Supervision for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01723)
*Yun Li,Yidu Zhang,Simon Thompson,Ehsan Javanmardi,Manabu Tsukada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CSN方法，通过意图约束对齐、量化基础和结构化分离重构VLA文本输入，结合安全监督和DPO对齐训练，在CARLA评估中显著提升自动驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶的VLA模型需要处理多种文本输入（导航指令、危险警告、交通状态描述），但这些输入通常以不连贯的片段呈现，迫使模型自行发现哪些环境约束与当前操作相关，导致效率低下。

Method: 1. 提出因果场景叙述（CSN）：在推理时通过意图约束对齐、量化基础和结构化分离重构VLA文本输入，零GPU成本；2. 结合基于单纯形的运行时安全监督；3. 使用带负对数似然正则化的Plackett-Luce DPO进行训练时对齐。

Result: 1. 多城镇闭环CARLA评估显示：CSN使原始LMDrive的驾驶评分提升+31.1%，偏好对齐变体提升+24.5%；2. 控制消融实验表明：因果结构贡献了39.1%的性能增益；3. 感知噪声消融确认CSN对现实传感误差具有鲁棒性；4. 语义安全监督提升违规评分，而反应式TTC监控降低性能。

Conclusion: CSN通过结构化文本输入显著提升VLA自动驾驶模型性能，因果结构是重要贡献因素。研究强调了意图感知监控对VLA系统的重要性，而非简单的反应式安全监控。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving must integrate diverse textual inputs, including navigation commands, hazard warnings, and traffic state descriptions, yet current systems often present these as disconnected fragments, forcing the model to discover on its own which environmental constraints are relevant to the current maneuver. We introduce Causal Scene Narration (CSN), which restructures VLA text inputs through intent-constraint alignment, quantitative grounding, and structured separation, at inference time with zero GPU cost. We complement CSN with Simplex-based runtime safety supervision and training-time alignment via Plackett-Luce DPO with negative log-likelihood (NLL) regularization. A multi-town closed-loop CARLA evaluation shows that CSN improves Driving Score by +31.1% on original LMDrive and +24.5% on the preference-aligned variant. A controlled ablation reveals that causal structure accounts for 39.1% of this gain, with the remainder attributable to information content alone. A perception noise ablation confirms that CSN's benefit is robust to realistic sensing errors. Semantic safety supervision improves Infraction Score, while reactive Time-To-Collision monitoring degrades performance, demonstrating that intent-aware monitoring is needed for VLA systems.

</details>


### [58] [Analysis of Efficient Transmission Methods of Grid Maps for Intelligent Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01753)
*Robin Dehler,Dominik Authaler,Aryan Thakur,Thomas Wodtko,Michael Buchholz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于分块的通信管道，利用现有压缩算法高效传输网格地图数据，解决了网格地图在分布式处理和V2X应用中数据量过大的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网格地图是智能车辆环境建模的基础方法，但细胞级别的表示导致数据量庞大。虽然分块网格地图通过调整细胞大小部分解决了这个问题，但其数据量仍然过大，无法满足新型分布式处理设置和车联网(V2X)应用的需求。因此需要从通信角度解决网格地图的尺寸问题。

Method: 基于分块网格地图方法，提出了一种分块通信管道，利用现有压缩算法高效传输网格地图数据。对车辆内部通信和V2X通信进行了全面分析，并通过两个真实世界实验设置验证了这些用例。

Result: 提供了网格地图数据传输的全面分析，验证了分块通信管道在车辆内部和V2X通信中的有效性。通过两个真实世界实验设置证明了该方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 总结了智能交通系统中高效传输网格地图数据的推荐指南，为解决网格地图数据量过大问题提供了实用的通信解决方案。

Abstract: Grid mapping is a fundamental approach to modeling the environment of intelligent vehicles or robots. Compared with object-based environment modeling, grid maps offer the distinct advantage of representing the environment without requiring any assumptions about objects, such as type or shape. For grid-map-based approaches, the environment is divided into cells, each containing information about its respective area, such as occupancy. This representation of the entire environment is crucial for achieving higher levels of autonomy. However, it has the drawback that modeling the scene at the cell level results in inherently large data sizes. Patched grid maps tackle this issue to a certain extent by adapting cell sizes in specific areas. Nevertheless, the data sizes of patched grid maps are still too large for novel distributed processing setups or vehicle-to-everything (V2X) applications. Our work builds on a patch-based grid-map approach and investigates the size problem from a communication perspective. To address this, we propose a patch-based communication pipeline that leverages existing compression algorithms to transmit grid-map data efficiently. We provide a comprehensive analysis of this pipeline for both intra-vehicle and V2X-based communication. The analysis is verified for these use cases with two real-world experiment setups. Finally, we summarize recommended guidelines for the efficient transmission of grid-map data in intelligent transportation systems.

</details>


### [59] [Realistic Lip Motion Generation Based on 3D Dynamic Viseme and Coarticulation Modeling for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01756)
*Sheng Li,Jingcheng Huang,Min Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于3D动态视位和协同发音建模的唇部运动生成框架，用于人形机器人语音驱动唇部同步，通过中文发音理论构建3D动态视位库，开发协同发音机制解决连续语音流中的运动冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现逼真的唇部同步对于人形机器人自然的人机非语言交互至关重要，需要解决连续语音流中的运动冲突问题，为机器人提供轻量级、高效的语音驱动唇部运动生成方案。

Method: 基于中文发音理论构建基于ARKit标准的3D动态视位库，提供连贯的唇部运动先验轨迹；开发协同发音机制，包含声母-韵母解耦和能量调制；设计策略将高维空间唇部运动重定向到14自由度唇部驱动系统。

Result: 通过定量消融实验验证了所提架构的效率和准确性，使用皮尔逊相关系数(PCC)和平均绝对急动度(MAJ)作为评估指标；提供了3D动态视位库和实际部署视频。

Conclusion: 该研究为人形机器人的语音驱动唇部运动生成提供了一个轻量级、高效且高度实用的范式，显著提升了机器人非语言交互的自然性。

Abstract: Realistic lip synchronization is essential for the natural human-robot non-verbal interaction of humanoid robots. Motivated by this need, this paper presents a lip motion generation framework based on 3D dynamic viseme and coarticulation modeling. By analyzing Chinese pronunciation theory, a 3D dynamic viseme library is constructed based on the ARKit standard, which offers coherent prior trajectories of lips. To resolve motion conflicts within continuous speech streams, a coarticulation mechanism is developed by incorporating initial-final (Shengmu-Yunmu) decoupling and energy modulation. After developing a strategy to retarget high-dimensional spatial lip motion to a 14-DOF lip actuation system of a humanoid head platform, the efficiency and accuracy of the proposed architecture is experimentally validated and demonstrated with quantitative ablation experiments using the metrics of the Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) and the Mean Absolute Jerk (MAJ). This research offers a lightweight, efficient, and highly practical paradigm for the speech-driven lip motion generation of humanoid robots. The 3D dynamic viseme library and real-world deployment videos are available at {https://github.com/yuesheng21/Phoneme-to-Lip-14DOF}

</details>


### [60] [Preferential Bayesian Optimization with Crash Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01776)
*Johanna Menn,David Stenger,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrashPBO：一种结合偏好反馈和崩溃报告的贝叶斯优化方法，用于机器人参数学习，减少实验崩溃63%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人硬件系统的参数优化中，传统偏好贝叶斯优化(PBO)无法处理实验崩溃情况，导致频繁的硬件损坏和时间浪费。需要一种能同时接收用户偏好反馈和崩溃报告的方法。

Method: 提出CrashPBO框架，扩展标准PBO方法，允许用户在优化过程中既表达参数偏好（通过成对比较），又报告实验崩溃情况。该方法将崩溃信息整合到贝叶斯优化模型中，避免探索会导致崩溃的参数区域。

Result: 在合成函数基准测试中，CrashPBO将崩溃次数减少63%，并提高了数据效率。在三个机器人平台上实验验证了方法的广泛适用性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: CrashPBO提供了一个灵活、用户友好的框架，用于结合人类对偏好和崩溃的反馈进行参数学习，特别适用于硬件系统优化，能显著减少实验崩溃并提高优化效率。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is a popular black-box optimization method for parameter learning in control and robotics. It typically requires an objective function that reflects the user's optimization goal. However, in practical applications, this objective function is often inaccessible due to complex or unmeasurable performance metrics. Preferential Bayesian optimization (PBO) overcomes this limitation by leveraging human feedback through pairwise comparisons, eliminating the need for explicit performance quantification. When applying PBO to hardware systems, such as in quadcopter control, crashes can cause time-consuming experimental resets, wear and tear, or otherwise undesired outcomes. Standard PBO methods cannot incorporate feedback from such crashed experiments, resulting in the exploration of parameters that frequently lead to experimental crashes. We thus introduce CrashPBO, a user-friendly mechanism that enables users to both express preferences and report crashes during the optimization process. Benchmarking on synthetic functions shows that this mechanism reduces crashes by 63% and increases data efficiency. Through experiments on three robotics platforms, we demonstrate the wide applicability and transferability of CrashPBO, highlighting that it provides a flexible, user-friendly framework for parameter learning with human feedback on preferences and crashes.

</details>


### [61] [Posterior Optimization with Clipped Objective for Bridging Efficiency and Stability in Generative Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01860)
*Yuhui Chen,Haoran Li,Zhennan Jiang,Yuxing Qin,Yuxuan Wan,Weiheng Liu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POCO是一种用于机器人操作的后验优化强化学习框架，通过期望最大化过程将奖励加权的隐式后验提炼到策略中，采用离线到在线范式，无需架构修改即可微调大型VLA模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然表达能力强的生成模型能够捕捉复杂多模态的动作分布，但通过强化学习微调这些策略仍然面临不稳定和样本效率低的问题，需要一种更稳定有效的微调方法

Method: POCO将策略改进形式化为针对时间动作块的后验推断问题，通过期望最大化过程将奖励加权的隐式后验提炼到策略中，无需似然估计，采用离线到在线范式，将在线探索锚定到预训练先验

Result: 在7个模拟基准测试和4个接触丰富的真实世界任务中，POCO防止了灾难性策略崩溃，超越了最先进的基线方法，在真实世界任务中实现了96.7%的成功率

Conclusion: POCO提供了一个原则性的强化学习框架，能够稳定高效地微调表达性生成模型，在模拟和真实世界任务中都表现出优越性能，为机器人操作策略的优化提供了有效解决方案

Abstract: Expressive generative models have advanced robotic manipulation by capturing complex, multi-modal action distributions over temporally extended trajectories. However, fine-tuning these policies via RL remains challenging due to instability and sample inefficiency. We introduce Posterior Optimization with Clipped Objective (POCO), a principled RL framework that formulates policy improvement as a posterior inference problem tailored for temporal action chunks. Through an Expectation-Maximization procedure, POCO distills a reward-weighted implicit posterior into the policy without likelihood estimation. Furthermore, POCO adopts an offline-to-online paradigm that anchors online exploration to pre-trained priors, and its model-agnostic design scales to fine-tune large VLA models without architectural modifications. Evaluations across 7 simulation benchmarks and 4 contact-rich real-world tasks demonstrate that POCO prevents catastrophic policy collapse, outperforms SOTA baselines, and achieves a 96.7% success rate on real-world tasks. Videos are available at our project website https://cccedric.github.io/poco/.

</details>


### [62] [Bridging Discrete Planning and Continuous Execution for Redundant Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02021)
*Teng Yan,Yue Yu,Yihan Liu,Bingzhuo Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个连接离散规划与连续执行的桥梁框架，通过改进规划侧的动作设计和执行侧的逆运动学层，显著提升7自由度冗余机械臂在体素网格强化学习路径规划中的执行质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体素网格强化学习在冗余机械臂路径规划中广泛应用，但通过逐点数值逆运动学直接执行会导致步长抖动、关节突变和奇异配置附近的不稳定性问题。

Method: 提出一个桥梁框架，不修改离散规划器本身。规划侧：引入步长归一化的26邻域笛卡尔动作和几何平局打破机制；执行侧：实现任务优先级阻尼最小二乘逆运动学层，将末端执行器位置作为主要任务，姿态和关节居中作为投影到零空间的从属任务，结合信任区域裁剪和关节速度约束。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂的随机稀疏、中等和密集环境中，该桥梁框架将密集场景的规划成功率从约0.58提升到1.00，代表性路径长度从约1.53米缩短到1.10米，末端执行器误差保持在1毫米以下，同时将峰值关节加速度降低一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 该桥梁框架显著改善了基于体素的强化学习路径在冗余机械臂上的连续执行质量，解决了离散规划与连续执行之间的不匹配问题。

Abstract: Voxel-grid reinforcement learning is widely adopted for path planning in redundant manipulators due to its simplicity and reproducibility. However, direct execution through point-wise numerical inverse kinematics on 7-DoF arms often yields step-size jitter, abrupt joint transitions, and instability near singular configurations. This work proposes a bridging framework between discrete planning and continuous execution without modifying the discrete planner itself. On the planning side, step-normalized 26-neighbor Cartesian actions and a geometric tie-breaking mechanism are introduced to suppress unnecessary turns and eliminate step-size oscillations. On the execution side, a task-priority damped least-squares (TP-DLS) inverse kinematics layer is implemented. This layer treats end-effector position as a primary task, while posture and joint centering are handled as subordinate tasks projected into the null space, combined with trust-region clipping and joint velocity constraints.
