These results highlight the promise of spectral-domain expert adaptation as an effective and parameter-efficient paradigm for LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [102] [Bridging Deep Learning and Integer Linear Programming: A Predictive-to-Prescriptive Framework for Supply Chain Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01775)
*Khai Banh Nghiep,Duc Nguyen Minh,Lan Hoang Thi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个结合预测与运营分析的三步框架，用于零售需求预测和物流优化。通过比较N-BEATS MSTL和N-HiTS两种时间序列模型，选择N-BEATS进行未来4周预测，并基于预测结果构建整数线性规划模型优化配送计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售数据常呈现不可调和的季节性、不规则峰值和噪声，使得精确预测几乎无法实现。供应链规划需要可靠的需求预测来优化运营决策，因此需要开发一个结合预测分析和运营优化的综合框架。

Method: 1. 探索性数据分析：对180,519笔交易数据进行分区，分析长期趋势、季节性和配送相关属性。
2. 模型比较：对比统计时间序列分解模型N-BEATS MSTL和深度学习架构N-HiTS的预测性能。
3. 优化建模：选择预测误差最低的N-BEATS模型预测未来4周需求（1918单位），基于预测结果构建整数线性规划模型，在预算、容量和服务约束下最小化总配送时间。

Result: N-HiTS和N-BEATS均显著优于统计基准模型。N-BEATS被选为最优模型，具有最低的预测误差。基于N-BEATS预测结果构建的整数线性规划模型提供了可行且成本最优的配送计划解决方案。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了精确预测与简单、高度可解释的模型优化在物流实践中的实际影响，为供应链规划提供了一个有效的分析框架，能够处理复杂的零售数据特征并生成优化的运营决策。

Abstract: Although demand forecasting is a critical component of supply chain planning, actual retail data can exhibit irreconcilable seasonality, irregular spikes, and noise, rendering precise projections nearly unattainable. This paper proposes a three-step analytical framework that combines forecasting and operational analytics. The first stage consists of exploratory data analysis, where delivery-tracked data from 180,519 transactions are partitioned, and long-term trends, seasonality, and delivery-related attributes are examined. Secondly, the forecasting performance of a statistical time series decomposition model N-BEATS MSTL and a recent deep learning architecture N-HiTS were compared. N-BEATS and N-HiTS were both statistically, and hence were N-BEATS's and N-HiTS's statistically selected. Most recent time series deep learning models, N-HiTS, N-BEATS. N-HiTS and N-BEATS N-HiTS and N-HiTS outperformed the statistical benchmark to a large extent. N-BEATS was selected to be the most optimized model, as the one with the lowest forecasting error, in the 3rd and final stage forecasting values of the next 4 weeks of 1918 units, and provided those as a model with a set of deterministically integer linear program outcomes that are aimed to minimize the total delivery time with a set of bound budget, capacity, and service constraints. The solution allocation provided a feasible and cost-optimal shipping plan. Overall, the study provides a compelling example of the practical impact of precise forecasting and simple, highly interpretable model optimization in logistics.

</details>


### [103] [Graph Neural Operator Towards Edge Deployability and Portability for Sparse-to-Dense, Real-Time Virtual Sensing on Irregular Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01802)
*William Howes,Jason Yoo,Kazuma Kobayashi,Subhankar Sarkar,Farid Ahmed,Souvik Chakraborty,Syed Bahauddin Alam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VIRSO是一种基于图的神经算子，用于不规则几何上的稀疏到密集重建，通过可变连接算法V-KNN构建网格感知图，在边缘设备上实现低功耗、低延迟的实时虚拟传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集仪器部署成本高且不可行，物理求解器计算延迟和功耗大，无法在资源受限的边缘系统中实时使用。现有神经算子将硬件部署性视为次要考虑，缺乏对边缘约束环境的优化。

Method: 提出VIRSO图神经算子用于不规则几何的稀疏到密集重建，开发V-KNN可变连接算法进行网格感知图构建。将推理重构为测量过程，结合频谱和空间分析，降低延迟和功耗。

Result: 在三个核热工水力学基准测试中，重建比47:1到156:1，平均相对L2误差低于1%，参数更少。10层配置将能量延迟积从206J·ms降至10.1J·ms（H200）。Jetson Orin Nano上功耗低于10W，延迟低于1秒。

Conclusion: VIRSO证明了边缘可行性和硬件可移植性，提出了计算感知算子学习作为资源受限环境中实时传感的新范式。

Abstract: Accurate sensing of spatially distributed physical fields typically requires dense instrumentation, which is often infeasible in real-world systems due to cost, accessibility, and environmental constraints. Physics-based solvers address this through direct numerical integration of governing equations, but their computational latency and power requirements preclude real-time use in resource-constrained monitoring and control systems. Here we introduce VIRSO (Virtual Irregular Real-Time Sparse Operator), a graph-based neural operator for sparse-to-dense reconstruction on irregular geometries, and a variable-connectivity algorithm, Variable KNN (V-KNN), for mesh-informed graph construction. Unlike prior neural operators that treat hardware deployability as secondary, VIRSO reframes inference as measurement: the combination of both spectral and spatial analysis provides accurate reconstruction without the high latency and power consumption of previous graph-based methodologies with poor scalability, presenting VIRSO as a potential candidate for edge-constrained, real-time virtual sensing. We evaluate VIRSO on three nuclear thermal-hydraulic benchmarks of increasing geometric and multiphysics complexity, across reconstruction ratios from 47:1 to 156:1. VIRSO achieves mean relative $L_2$ errors below 1%, outperforming other benchmark operators while using fewer parameters. The full 10-layer configuration reduces the energy-delay product (EDP) from ${\approx}206$ J$\cdot$ms for the graph operator baseline to $10.1$ J$\cdot$ms on an NVIDIA H200. Implemented on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, all configurations of VIRSO provide sub-10 W power consumption and sub-second latency. These results establish the edge-feasibility and hardware-portability of VIRSO and present compute-aware operator learning as a new paradigm for real-time sensing in inaccessible and resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [104] [CANDI: Curated Test-Time Adaptation for Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01845)
*HyunGi Kim,Jisoo Mok,Hyungyu Lee,Juhyeon Shin,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CANDI提出了一种针对多元时间序列异常检测的测试时自适应框架，通过选择性识别和适应潜在误报来应对分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列异常检测在现实应用中面临分布偏移问题，导致预训练模型性能严重下降。测试时自适应能够仅使用未标记测试数据在线更新模型，为解决这一挑战提供了可能。

Method: CANDI框架包含两个核心组件：1) 误报挖掘策略，基于异常分数和潜在相似性筛选适应样本；2) 即插即用的时空感知正态性适应模块，用于结构化的模型更新。

Result: 实验表明CANDI显著提升了分布偏移下多元时间序列异常检测的性能，AUROC最高提升14%，同时使用更少的适应样本。

Conclusion: CANDI通过选择性适应潜在误报并保留预训练知识，有效解决了多元时间序列异常检测中的分布偏移问题，为实际部署提供了鲁棒的测试时自适应解决方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series anomaly detection (MTSAD) aims to identify deviations from normality in multivariate time-series and is critical in real-world applications. However, in real-world deployments, distribution shifts are ubiquitous and cause severe performance degradation in pre-trained anomaly detector. Test-time adaptation (TTA) updates a pre-trained model on-the-fly using only unlabeled test data, making it promising for addressing this challenge. In this study, we propose CANDI (Curated test-time adaptation for multivariate time-series ANomaly detection under DIstribution shift), a novel TTA framework that selectively identifies and adapts to potential false positives while preserving pre-trained knowledge. CANDI introduces a False Positive Mining (FPM) strategy to curate adaptation samples based on anomaly scores and latent similarity, and incorporates a plug-and-play Spatiotemporally-Aware Normality Adaptation (SANA) module for structurally informed model updates. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CANDI significantly improves the performance of MTSAD under distribution shift, improving AUROC up to 14% while using fewer adaptation samples.

</details>


### [105] [Robust Graph Representation Learning via Adaptive Spectral Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01878)
*Zhuolong Li,Boxue Yang,Haopeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASPECT框架通过可靠性感知的频谱门控机制解决谱图对比学习中的频谱困境，在混合图上实现节点级频谱融合，在9个基准测试中8个达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱图对比学习在处理同质性和异质性混合图时面临频谱困境：高频信号对编码异质性至关重要，但理论分析表明它们在频谱集中扰动下具有更高方差。全局频谱融合策略在节点级频谱偏好分离的混合图上存在固有次优性。

Method: 提出ASPECT框架，采用可靠性感知的频谱门控机制。通过极小极大博弈实现，使用节点级门控动态重新加权频谱通道，基于其对抗专门构建的对抗器的稳定性。对抗器通过Rayleigh商惩罚显式针对频谱能量分布，迫使编码器学习既具有结构判别性又具有频谱鲁棒性的表示。

Result: 在9个基准测试中的8个上实现了新的最先进性能，有效解耦了有意义的结构异质性与偶然噪声。

Conclusion: ASPECT通过节点级可靠性感知频谱融合解决了谱图对比学习中的频谱困境，为处理混合同质-异质图提供了理论保证和实证有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Spectral graph contrastive learning has emerged as a unified paradigm for handling both homophilic and heterophilic graphs by leveraging high-frequency components. However, we identify a fundamental spectral dilemma: while high-frequency signals are indispensable for encoding heterophily, our theoretical analysis proves they exhibit significantly higher variance under spectrally concentrated perturbations. We derive a regret lower bound showing that existing global (node-agnostic) spectral fusion is provably sub-optimal: on mixed graphs with separated node-wise frequency preferences, any global fusion strategy incurs non-vanishing regret relative to a node-wise oracle. To escape this bound, we propose ASPECT, a framework that resolves this dilemma through a reliability-aware spectral gating mechanism. Formulated as a minimax game, ASPECT employs a node-wise gate that dynamically re-weights frequency channels based on their stability against a purpose-built adversary, which explicitly targets spectral energy distributions via a Rayleigh quotient penalty. This design forces the encoder to learn representations that are both structurally discriminative and spectrally robust. Empirical results show that ASPECT achieves new state-of-the-art performance on 8 out of 9 benchmarks, effectively decoupling meaningful structural heterophily from incidental noise.

</details>


### [106] [Enhancing the Reliability of Medical AI through Expert-guided Uncertainty Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01898)
*Aleksei Khalin,Ekaterina Zaychenkova,Aleksandr Yugay,Andrey Goncharov,Sergey Korchagin,Alexey Zaytsev,Egor Ershov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种利用专家意见分歧来训练机器学习模型的新方法，通过两集成方法分别估计不确定性，在医疗AI系统中提升不确定性估计质量9%-50%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI系统作为第二意见系统加速医疗流程并提高诊断准确性，但AI错误的不可预测性带来重大挑战，特别是在医疗环境中错误可能造成严重后果。现有不确定性估计方法有限，尤其是在量化数据模糊和噪声引起的偶然不确定性方面。

Method: 提出一种新颖方法，利用专家响应的分歧生成训练机器学习模型的目标。这些目标与标准数据标签结合，通过两集成方法及其轻量变体，根据全方差定律分别估计不确定性的两个组成部分。

Result: 在二值图像分类、二值和多类图像分割以及多项选择题回答任务上验证方法。实验表明，结合专家知识可将不确定性估计质量提升9%到50%，具体取决于任务类型。

Conclusion: 专家知识是构建医疗应用中风险感知AI系统的宝贵信息来源，能够显著提升不确定性估计质量，从而更好地识别高风险案例并优化人工专家的工作流程。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems accelerate medical workflows and improve diagnostic accuracy in healthcare, serving as second-opinion systems. However, the unpredictability of AI errors poses a significant challenge, particularly in healthcare contexts, where mistakes can have severe consequences. A widely adopted safeguard is to pair predictions with uncertainty estimation, enabling human experts to focus on high-risk cases while streamlining routine verification. Current uncertainty estimation methods, however, remain limited, particularly in quantifying aleatoric uncertainty, which arises from data ambiguity and noise. To address this, we propose a novel approach that leverages disagreement in expert responses to generate targets for training machine learning models. These targets are used in conjunction with standard data labels to estimate two components of uncertainty separately, as given by the law of total variance, via a two-ensemble approach, as well as its lightweight variant. We validate our method on binary image classification, binary and multi-class image segmentation, and multiple-choice question answering. Our experiments demonstrate that incorporating expert knowledge can enhance uncertainty estimation quality by $9\%$ to $50\%$ depending on the task, making this source of information invaluable for the construction of risk-aware AI systems in healthcare applications.

</details>


### [107] [annbatch unlocks terabyte-scale training of biological data in anndata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01949)
*Ilan Gold,Felix Fischer,Lucas Arnoldt,F. Alexander Wolf,Fabian J. Theis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: annbatch是一个用于anndata的mini-batch加载器，支持直接在磁盘数据集上进行out-of-core训练，解决了生物数据规模超过系统内存时的数据访问瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物数据集的规模现在经常超过系统内存，使得数据访问而非模型计算成为训练机器学习模型的主要瓶颈。这个瓶颈在生物学中尤为严重，因为广泛使用的社区数据格式必须支持异构元数据、稀疏和密集检测以及现有计算生态系统中的下游分析。

Method: 开发了annbatch，这是一个原生支持anndata的mini-batch加载器，能够直接在磁盘数据集上进行out-of-core训练。它完全兼容scverse生态系统，支持异构元数据、稀疏和密集检测。

Result: 在单细胞转录组学、显微镜和全基因组测序基准测试中，annbatch将加载吞吐量提高了最多一个数量级，将训练时间从几天缩短到几小时，同时保持与scverse生态系统的完全兼容性。

Conclusion: annbatch为可扩展的生物AI建立了实用的数据加载基础设施，允许使用越来越大规模和多样化的数据集，而无需放弃标准的生物数据格式。

Abstract: The scale of biological datasets now routinely exceeds system memory, making data access rather than model computation the primary bottleneck in training machine-learning models. This bottleneck is particularly acute in biology, where widely used community data formats must support heterogeneous metadata, sparse and dense assays, and downstream analysis within established computational ecosystems. Here we present annbatch, a mini-batch loader native to anndata that enables out-of-core training directly on disk-backed datasets. Across single-cell transcriptomics, microscopy and whole-genome sequencing benchmarks, annbatch increases loading throughput by up to an order of magnitude and shortens training from days to hours, while remaining fully compatible with the scverse ecosystem. Annbatch establishes a practical data-loading infrastructure for scalable biological AI, allowing increasingly large and diverse datasets to be used without abandoning standard biological data formats. Github: https://github.com/scverse/annbatch

</details>


### [108] [Learn by Surprise, Commit by Proof](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01951)
*Kang-Sin Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LSCP是一种自门控后训练框架，通过检测异常高损失段落，生成问答链让模型自我验证知识缺口，并基于验证深度自适应调整AdamW的β₂参数，实现仅学习模型未知内容的自适应知识获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型训练通常需要外部监督或标注数据，且容易产生机械记忆而非语义理解。模型需要一种能够自主识别知识缺口、仅学习未知内容、同时保护已有知识不被污染的自适应学习机制。

Method: 1) 检测异常高损失段落；2) 生成问答链让模型自我验证知识缺口；3) 基于验证深度k自适应调整AdamW的β₂参数：β₂ = 0.999·r^k；4) 整个学习强度由单一参数r控制；5) 系统具有自熄灭特性，随着学习进展逐渐收敛到标准AdamW。

Result: 实验在Qwen3-14B等六个模型(8B-32B，四个家族)上进行：标准微调产生机械记忆(扰动间隙11.6±0.2倍基线)，而所有LSCP条件都实现语义学习(2.7-3.0倍)。r=1.0条件证明训练数据格式(而非β₂门控)是防止记忆的主要机制；门控机制保护相邻知识免受污染内容影响(r=0.98时相邻问题准确率93±7% vs 90%基线)。

Conclusion: LSCP框架实现了自主知识获取，仅学习模型未知内容，同时保护已有知识。该框架模拟生物记忆巩固过程，将上下文中的临时信息选择性地巩固到参数权重中。训练数据格式是防止机械记忆的关键，而门控机制则保护知识免受污染。

Abstract: We propose LSCP, a self-gated post-training framework for autonomous knowledge acquisition: learning only what a model does not already know, verified against what it does know, at a strength proportional to conviction, with no external oracle. When a passage produces anomalously high per-token loss, LSCP flags it, generates a Q&A chain that forces the model to articulate its own knowledge and identify gaps, then adjusts AdamW's $β_2$ proportionally to conviction depth k (the number of self-verification steps the passage survives) via $β_2 = 0.999 \cdot r^k$. The entire learning intensity is governed by a single parameter $r$. Beyond new knowledge, this process sharpens weakly encoded existing knowledge, which is a primary source of hallucination. The framework is self-extinguishing: as the model learns, per-token loss on learned passages decreases toward the surprisal threshold and the system progressively converges to standard AdamW. This models biological memory consolidation: temporary information in the context window is selectively consolidated into parametric weights, the model's long-term memory. Experiments on the reference model (Qwen3-14B) and across six models (8B--32B, four families) show that standard fine-tuning produces rote memorization (perturbation gap (the ratio of paraphrase to original perplexity) of 11.6 +- 0.2 x baseline) while all LSCP conditions learn semantically (2.7--3.0x). The r=1.0 condition (identical optimizer, nearly identical data, only Q&A format differs) confirms that the training data format, not $β_2$ gating, is the primary mechanism preventing memorization; gating instead protects neighboring knowledge from contamination by corrupt content (93 +- 7% accuracy on adjacent questions at r=0.98 vs. 90% baseline).

</details>


### [109] [Generalization Bounds and Statistical Guarantees for Multi-Task and Multiple Operator Learning with MNO Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01961)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多算子学习中的泛化理论，针对Multiple Neural Operator架构，通过覆盖数分析推导了可分离模型的泛化误差界，建立了算子采样预算与泛化性能之间的量化关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多算子学习旨在学习由算子描述符α索引的算子族{G[α]:U→V}，现有研究虽然发展了表达能力强的架构和逼近理论缩放律，但定量的统计泛化保证仍然有限。需要为可分离模型提供基于覆盖数的泛化分析，特别是针对Multiple Neural Operator架构。

Method: 采用分层采样策略收集训练数据：先采样算子实例α，然后为每个实例采样输入函数u，最后为每个输入采样评估点x。通过覆盖数分析推导假设类的度量熵界，结合MNO的逼近保证，得到期望测试误差的显式逼近-估计权衡。

Result: 获得了显式的泛化误差界，使分层采样预算(n_α,n_u,n_x)的依赖关系变得透明，并在算子采样预算n_α中提供了显式学习率陈述，为跨算子实例的泛化提供了样本复杂度特征化。

Conclusion: 该研究为多算子学习提供了首个基于覆盖数的泛化理论框架，量化了算子采样预算对泛化性能的影响，将MNO架构视为通用求解器或小型PDE基础模型，其中三元组(α,u,x)是多模态的一种形式。

Abstract: Multiple operator learning concerns learning operator families $\{G[α]:U\to V\}_{α\in W}$ indexed by an operator descriptor $α$. Training data are collected hierarchically by sampling operator instances $α$, then input functions $u$ per instance, and finally evaluation points $x$ per input, yielding noisy observations of $G[α][u](x)$. While recent work has developed expressive multi-task and multiple operator learning architectures and approximation-theoretic scaling laws, quantitative statistical generalization guarantees remain limited. We provide a covering-number-based generalization analysis for separable models, focusing on the Multiple Neural Operator (MNO) architecture: we first derive explicit metric-entropy bounds for hypothesis classes given by linear combinations of products of deep ReLU subnetworks, and then combine these complexity bounds with approximation guarantees for MNO to obtain an explicit approximation-estimation tradeoff for the expected test error on new (unseen) triples $(α,u,x)$. The resulting bound makes the dependence on the hierarchical sampling budgets $(n_α,n_u,n_x)$ transparent and yields an explicit learning-rate statement in the operator-sampling budget $n_α$, providing a sample-complexity characterization for generalization across operator instances. The structure and architecture can also be viewed as a general purpose solver or an example of a "small'' PDE foundation model, where the triples are one form of multi-modality.

</details>


### [110] [Apriel-Reasoner: RL Post-Training for General-Purpose and Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02007)
*Rafael Pardinas,Ehsan Kamalloo,David Vazquez,Alexandre Drouin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Apriel-Reasoner：基于15B参数开源LLM的多领域RL后训练方法，通过自适应领域采样和难度感知长度惩罚，在保持推理准确性的同时显著缩短推理链长度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通用推理模型通常使用RLVR训练但训练配方和领域混合不公开，跨领域联合优化面临挑战：领域间rollout长度、问题难度和样本效率差异大，长推理链增加推理成本和延迟

Method: 1) 在Apriel-Base（15B参数开源LLM）上使用完全可复现的多领域RL后训练配方，覆盖数学、代码生成、指令遵循、逻辑谜题和函数调用五个领域；2) 引入自适应领域采样机制，在异构rollout动态下保持目标领域比例；3) 提出难度感知的标准长度惩罚扩展，无需额外训练开销，鼓励困难问题更长推理、简单问题更短推理

Result: 在严格16K token输出预算下训练，推理时泛化到32K token，在AIME 2025、GPQA、MMLU-Pro和LiveCodeBench上超越Apriel-Base，推理链缩短30-50%；在相似规模的开源模型中达到同等性能但token成本更低，推进了准确性与token预算的帕累托前沿

Conclusion: Apriel-Reasoner通过创新的自适应领域采样和难度感知长度惩罚，实现了多领域推理的高效优化，在保持准确性的同时显著降低推理成本，为实用部署提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Building general-purpose reasoning models using reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) across diverse domains has been widely adopted by frontier open-weight models. However, their training recipes and domain mixtures are often not disclosed. Joint optimization across domains poses significant challenges: domains vary widely in rollout length, problem difficulty and sample efficiency. Further, models with long chain-of-thought traces increase inference cost and latency, making efficiency critical for practical deployment. We present Apriel-Reasoner, trained with a fully reproducible multi-domain RL post-training recipe on Apriel-Base, a 15B-parameter open-weight LLM, across five domains using public datasets: mathematics, code generation, instruction following, logical puzzles and function calling. We introduce an adaptive domain sampling mechanism that preserves target domain ratios despite heterogeneous rollout dynamics, and a difficulty-aware extension of the standard length penalty that, with no additional training overhead, encourages longer reasoning for difficult problems and shorter traces for easy ones. Trained with a strict 16K-token output budget, Apriel-Reasoner generalizes to 32K tokens at inference and improves over Apriel-Base on AIME 2025, GPQA, MMLU-Pro, and LiveCodeBench while producing 30-50% shorter reasoning traces. It matches strong open-weight models of similar size at lower token cost, thereby pushing the Pareto frontier of accuracy versus token budget.

</details>


### [111] [Feature Weighting Improves Pool-Based Sequential Active Learning for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02019)
*Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于特征加权的主动学习回归方法，通过使用岭回归系数对特征进行加权来改进样本选择中的距离计算，从而提升回归模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于池的序列主动学习回归方法在计算样本间距离时未考虑不同特征的重要性，导致样本选择不够优化。代表性（representativeness）和多样性（diversity）是主动学习中的关键考虑因素，但现有方法在距离计算中未进行特征加权，影响了样本选择效果。

Method: 提出了三种特征加权的单任务主动学习回归方法和两种特征加权的多任务主动学习回归方法。核心思想是使用从少量已标记样本训练得到的岭回归系数来加权相应特征在样本间距离计算中的重要性。这种增强方法易于实现，可以应用于现有的主动学习回归方法。

Result: 实验表明，这种特征加权增强几乎总是能提升四种现有主动学习回归方法的性能，无论是在单任务还是多任务回归问题中。该方法简单易实现且效果显著。

Conclusion: 特征加权策略能有效改进主动学习回归中的样本选择，提升回归模型的准确性。该方法还可轻松扩展到基于流的主动学习以及分类算法中，具有较好的通用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Pool-based sequential active learning for regression (ALR) optimally selects a small number of samples sequentially from a large pool of unlabeled samples to label, so that a more accurate regression model can be constructed under a given labeling budget. Representativeness and diversity, which involve computing the distances among different samples, are important considerations in ALR. However, previous ALR approaches do not incorporate the importance of different features in inter-sample distance computation, resulting in sub-optimal sample selection. This paper proposes three feature weighted single-task ALR approaches and two feature weighted multi-task ALR approaches, where the ridge regression coefficients trained from a small amount of previously labeled samples are used to weight the corresponding features in inter-sample distance computation. Experiments showed that this easy-to-implement enhancement almost always improves the performance of four existing ALR approaches, in both single-task and multi-task regression problems. The feature weighting strategy may also be easily extended to stream-based ALR, and classification algorithms.

</details>


### [112] [AA-SVD : Anchored and Adaptive SVD for Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02119)
*Atul Kumar Sinha,François Fleuret*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于低秩分解的快速LLM压缩框架，通过同时考虑原始输入和压缩引起的分布偏移，实现无需重训练的十亿参数模型快速压缩


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分解的压缩方法存在两个主要问题：1) 仅优化原始输入，忽略上游压缩引起的分布偏移，导致误差传播；2) 仅依赖偏移输入，可能偏离原始输出。需要一种能同时考虑两者的方法

Method: 1) 同时考虑原始输入和压缩引起的分布偏移，将压缩层锚定到原始输出；2) 在单个层压缩基础上，端到端细化每个transformer块，最小化块级输出失真；3) 允许压缩层共同补偿累积误差，找到保持功能等效的低秩近似

Result: 在大型语言模型上的实验表明，该方法在各种压缩比下始终优于现有SVD基线，在激进压缩预算下优势更加明显（竞争方法性能大幅下降或完全崩溃）

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种实用的大规模模型部署解决方案，通过同时建模原始输出和输入分布偏移，实现了高效、快速的LLM压缩

Abstract: We introduce a fast low-rank factorization-based framework for compressing large language models that enables rapid compression of billion-parameter models without retraining. Unlike existing factorization-based approaches that optimize only on the original inputs, ignoring distribution shifts from upstream compression and thus propagating errors forward, or those that rely only on shifted inputs and risk drifting away from the original outputs, our approach accounts for both. Beyond individual layer compression, we further refine each transformer block end-to-end, minimizing block-level output distortion and allowing compressed layers to jointly compensate for accumulated errors. By anchoring each compressed layer to the original outputs while explicitly modeling input distribution shifts, our method finds a low-rank approximation that maintains functional equivalence with the original model. Experiments on large language models show that our method consistently outperforms existing SVD-based baselines across compression ratios, with the advantage becoming increasingly pronounced at aggressive compression budgets, where competing methods degrade substantially or collapse entirely, offering a practical solution for efficient, large-scale model deployment.

</details>


### [113] [Auction-Based Online Policy Adaptation for Evolving Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02151)
*Guruprerana Shabadi,Kaushik Mallik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于多目标强化学习的模块化框架，通过拍卖机制协调自私的局部策略，实现运行时目标的动态适应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多目标强化学习中目标可能动态出现或消失的问题，需要设计能够高效适应目标变化的策略

Method: 采用模块化框架：每个目标由自私的局部策略支持，通过拍卖机制协调（策略出价竞争行动执行权，出价反映状态紧急度）。当目标变化时，只需添加或移除相应策略。由于目标来自相同家族，可部署参数化策略的相同副本。将问题转化为一般和博弈，使用PPO并发训练策略

Result: 在Atari Assault和基于网格世界的路径规划任务（动态目标）上评估，相比使用PPO训练的整体策略，本方法性能显著更好

Conclusion: 提出的模块化拍卖框架能有效处理动态多目标强化学习问题，实现运行时适应，性能优于传统整体策略方法

Abstract: We consider multi-objective reinforcement learning problems where objectives come from an identical family -- such as the class of reachability objectives -- and may appear or disappear at runtime. Our goal is to design adaptive policies that can efficiently adjust their behaviors as the set of active objectives changes. To solve this problem, we propose a modular framework where each objective is supported by a selfish local policy, and coordination is achieved through a novel auction-based mechanism: policies bid for the right to execute their actions, with bids reflecting the urgency of the current state. The highest bidder selects the action, enabling a dynamic and interpretable trade-off among objectives. Going back to the original adaptation problem, when objectives change, the system adapts by simply adding or removing the corresponding policies. Moreover, as objectives arise from the same family, identical copies of a parameterized policy can be deployed, facilitating immediate adaptation at runtime. We show how the selfish local policies can be computed by turning the problem into a general-sum game, where the policies compete against each other to fulfill their own objectives. To succeed, each policy must not only optimize its own objective, but also reason about the presence of other goals and learn to produce calibrated bids that reflect relative priority. In our implementation, the policies are trained concurrently using proximal policy optimization (PPO). We evaluate on Atari Assault and a gridworld-based path-planning task with dynamic targets. Our method achieves substantially better performance than monolithic policies trained with PPO.

</details>


### [114] [Smoothing the Landscape: Causal Structure Learning via Diffusion Denoising Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02250)
*Hao Zhu,Di Zhou,Donna Slonim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DDCD利用扩散模型的去噪分数匹配目标平滑梯度，提出自适应k跳无环约束，实现更快速稳定的因果发现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（如NOTEARS、DAG-GNN）在高维数据中面临可扩展性和稳定性问题，特别是在特征-样本不平衡时

Method: 提出Denoising Diffusion Causal Discovery (DDCD)框架：利用扩散模型的去噪分数匹配目标平滑梯度以加速收敛；引入自适应k-hop无环约束避免矩阵求逆，提高运行效率

Result: 在合成基准数据上表现出竞争性性能；在两个真实世界案例中通过定性分析展示了实用价值

Conclusion: DDCD通过扩散模型的梯度平滑机制和自适应无环约束，解决了高维因果发现中的收敛稳定性和计算效率问题

Abstract: Understanding causal dependencies in observational data is critical for informing decision-making. These relationships are often modeled as Bayesian Networks (BNs) and Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs). Existing methods, such as NOTEARS and DAG-GNN, often face issues with scalability and stability in high-dimensional data, especially when there is a feature-sample imbalance. Here, we show that the denoising score matching objective of diffusion models could smooth the gradients for faster, more stable convergence. We also propose an adaptive k-hop acyclicity constraint that improves runtime over existing solutions that require matrix inversion. We name this framework Denoising Diffusion Causal Discovery (DDCD). Unlike generative diffusion models, DDCD utilizes the reverse denoising process to infer a parameterized causal structure rather than to generate data. We demonstrate the competitive performance of DDCDs on synthetic benchmarking data. We also show that our methods are practically useful by conducting qualitative analyses on two real-world examples. Code is available at this url: https://github.com/haozhu233/ddcd.

</details>


### [115] [SKILL0: In-Context Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Skill Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02268)
*Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Jinyang Wu,Chengcheng Han,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SKILL0：一种通过上下文强化学习框架实现技能内化的方法，使LLM智能体无需运行时技能检索即可零样本自主执行任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理时技能增强存在三个根本限制：检索噪声引入无关指导、注入技能内容带来大量token开销、模型仅遵循知识而非真正掌握。需要探索能否将技能内化到模型参数中，实现无需运行时技能检索的零样本自主行为

Method: SKILL0框架包含：1）训练时课程从完整技能上下文开始并逐步撤回；2）技能按类别离线分组并渲染为紧凑视觉上下文，教授工具调用和多轮任务完成；3）动态课程评估每个技能文件对当前策略的有用性，在线性衰减预算内仅保留仍有帮助的技能，直到智能体完全在零样本设置下运行

Result: 在广泛的智能体实验中，SKILL0相比标准RL基线取得显著改进（ALFWorld +9.7%，Search-QA +6.6%），同时保持每步少于0.5k token的高效上下文

Conclusion: SKILL0成功实现了技能内化，使LLM智能体能够零样本自主执行任务，无需运行时技能检索，解决了推理时技能增强的根本限制，同时保持了上下文效率

Abstract: Agent skills, structured packages of procedural knowledge and executable resources that agents dynamically load at inference time, have become a reliable mechanism for augmenting LLM agents. Yet inference-time skill augmentation is fundamentally limited: retrieval noise introduces irrelevant guidance, injected skill content imposes substantial token overhead, and the model never truly acquires the knowledge it merely follows. We ask whether skills can instead be internalized into model parameters, enabling zero-shot autonomous behavior without any runtime skill retrieval. We introduce SKILL0, an in-context reinforcement learning framework designed for skill internalization. SKILL0 introduces a training-time curriculum that begins with full skill context and progressively withdraws it. Skills are grouped offline by category and rendered with interaction history into a compact visual context, teaching he model tool invocation and multi-turn task completion. A Dynamic Curriculum then evaluates each skill file's on-policy helpfulness, retaining only those from which the current policy still benefits within a linearly decaying budget, until the agent operates in a fully zero-shot setting. Extensive agentic experiments demonstrate that SKILL0 achieves substantial improvements over the standard RL baseline (+9.7\% for ALFWorld and +6.6\% for Search-QA), while maintaining a highly efficient context of fewer than 0.5k tokens per step. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/SkillZero.

</details>


### [116] [Unifying Group-Relative and Self-Distillation Policy Optimization via Sample Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02288)
*Gengsheng Li,Tianyu Yang,Junfeng Fang,Mingyang Song,Mao Zheng,Haiyun Guo,Dan Zhang,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SRPO结合GRPO和SDPO优势，通过样本路由机制将正确样本导向GRPO的奖励对齐强化学习，将失败样本导向SDPO的密集对数级监督，解决了SDPO长期训练崩溃问题，实现了快速早期改进和长期稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习验证奖励方法中，GRPO采用粗粒度信用分配，对失败rollout统一惩罚，缺乏针对特定偏差的token级关注；SDPO提供更密集的对数级监督，能实现快速早期改进，但在长期训练中经常崩溃。需要解决SDPO的后期不稳定性问题。

Method: 提出样本路由策略优化(SRPO)：1) 将正确样本路由到GRPO的奖励对齐强化学习；2) 将失败样本路由到SDPO的针对性对数级校正；3) 引入熵感知动态加权机制，抑制高熵不可靠蒸馏目标，强调置信度高的目标。

Result: 在五个基准测试和两种模型规模上，SRPO同时实现了SDPO的快速早期改进和GRPO的长期稳定性。在Qwen3-8B上，五个基准测试平均性能比GRPO提高3.4%，比SDPO提高6.3%，同时产生适度的响应长度，每步计算成本降低高达17.2%。

Conclusion: SRPO通过样本路由机制统一了GRPO和SDPO的优势，解决了SDPO的后期崩溃问题，实现了快速收敛和长期稳定训练，在多个基准测试上超越了两种基线方法的峰值性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard paradigm for post-training large language models. While Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely adopted, its coarse credit assignment uniformly penalizes failed rollouts, lacking the token-level focus needed to efficiently address specific deviations. Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) addresses this by providing denser, more targeted logit-level supervision that facilitates rapid early improvement, yet it frequently collapses during prolonged training. We trace this late-stage instability to two intrinsic flaws: self-distillation on already-correct samples introduces optimization ambiguity, and the self-teacher's signal reliability progressively degrades. To resolve these issues, we propose Sample-Routed Policy Optimization (SRPO), a unified on-policy framework that routes correct samples to GRPO's reward-aligned reinforcement and failed samples to SDPO's targeted logit-level correction. SRPO further incorporates an entropy-aware dynamic weighting mechanism to suppress high-entropy, unreliable distillation targets while emphasizing confident ones. Evaluated across five benchmarks and two model scales, SRPO achieves both the rapid early improvement of SDPO and the long-horizon stability of GRPO. It consistently surpasses the peak performance of both baselines, raising the five-benchmark average on Qwen3-8B by 3.4% over GRPO and 6.3% over SDPO, while simultaneously yielding moderate response lengths and lowering per-step compute cost by up to 17.2%.

</details>


### [117] [Taming the Exponential: A Fast Softmax Surrogate for Integer-Native Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02292)
*Dimitrios Danopoulos,Enrico Lupi,Michael Kagan,Maurizio Pierini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HCCS（Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax）作为softmax的替代方案，针对AMD Versal AI Engines平台优化，使用int8运算显著提升推理速度，同时保持任务精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低精度推理的小型Transformer模型中，softmax成为计算瓶颈，其指数运算和归一化操作带来显著开销。现有AMD平台参考实现使用bfloat16或查找表进行指数运算，限制了平台吞吐量且未能充分利用AI Engine的高吞吐整数向量处理单元。

Method: 提出HCCS方法：使用截断线性映射替代指数函数，生成有界单调的softmax近似。引入轻量级校准参数，基于代表性数据集离线优化，为每个注意力头单独校准以保持统计特性。针对AMD AI Engines的int8乘累加单元进行硬件优化实现。

Result: HCCS在AMD AI Engines上显著超越其他参考实现的速度性能，同时在小规模或重度量化MHA工作负载上，经过量化感知重训练后保持竞争性任务精度。这是首个针对AMD AI Engines的int8优化softmax替代方案。

Conclusion: HCCS有效解决了softmax在低精度推理中的计算瓶颈问题，充分利用硬件特性实现高效推理，为小型或量化Transformer模型提供了实用的加速方案。

Abstract: Softmax can become a computational bottleneck in the Transformer model's Multi-Head Attention (MHA) block, particularly in small models under low-precision inference, where exponentiation and normalization incur significant overhead. As such, we suggest using Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax (HCCS), a bounded, monotone surrogate to the exponential softmax function, which uses a clipped linear mapping of the max centered attention logits. This approximation produces a stable probability distribution, maintains the ordering of the original logits and has non-negative values. HCCS differs from previous softmax surrogates as it includes a set of lightweight calibration parameters that are optimized offline based on a representative dataset and calibrated for each individual attention head to preserve the statistical properties of the individual heads. We describe a hardware-motivated implementation of HCCS for high-throughput scenarios targeting the AMD Versal AI Engines. The current reference implementations from AMD for this platform rely upon either bfloat16 arithmetic or LUTs to perform the exponential operation, which might limit the throughput of the platform and fail to utilize the high-throughput integer vector processing units of the AI Engine. In contrast, HCCS provides a natural mapping to the AI Engines' int8 multiply accumulate (MAC) units. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first int8 optimized softmax surrogate for AMD AI engines that significantly exceeds the speed performance of other reference implementations while maintaining competitive task accuracy on small or heavily quantized MHA workloads after quantization-aware retraining.

</details>


### [118] [go-$m$HC: Direct Parameterization of Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections via Generalized Orthostochastic Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02309)
*Torque Dandachi,Sophia Diggs-Galligan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出go-mHC方法，通过广义正交随机矩阵理论实现对双随机矩阵集合（Birkhoff多面体）的精确高效参数化，在流混合任务中实现理论最小损失且收敛速度提升10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双随机矩阵能够实现残差流之间的学习混合，但现有方法要么计算复杂度随流数量呈阶乘增长（精确方法），要么表达能力有限（Kronecker分解方法），缺乏既精确又高效的参数化方案。

Method: 基于广义正交随机矩阵理论提出新颖的精确参数化方法，计算复杂度为O(d³)，通过超参数s在计算效率边界和完全表达能力之间连续插值。该方法在流形约束超连接(mHC)框架下实例化为go-mHC，可与Kronecker分解方法自然组合，在相似FLOP成本下显著恢复表达能力。

Result: 谱分析表明go-mHC比Kronecker分解基线更完整地填充Birkhoff多面体。在合成流混合任务中，go-mHC达到理论最小损失且收敛速度提升高达10倍。在3000万参数的GPT风格语言模型中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: go-mHC的精确性、高效性和表达能力为将流数量d作为模型容量的新维度进行扩展提供了实用途径，解决了双随机矩阵参数化的核心挑战。

Abstract: Doubly stochastic matrices enable learned mixing across residual streams, but parameterizing the set of doubly stochastic matrices (the Birkhoff polytope) exactly and efficiently remains an open challenge. Existing exact methods scale factorially with the number of streams ($d$), while Kronecker-factorized approaches are efficient but expressivity-limited. We introduce a novel exact parameterization grounded in the theory of generalized orthostochastic matrices, which scales as $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$ and exposes a single hyperparameter $s$ which continuously interpolates between a computationally efficient boundary and the fully expressive Birkhoff polytope. Building on Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections ($m$HC), a framework for learned dynamic layer connectivity, we instantiate this parameterization in go-$m$HC. Our method composes naturally with Kronecker-factorized methods, substantially recovering expressivity at similar FLOP costs. Spectral analysis indicates that go-$m$HC fills the Birkhoff polytope far more completely than Kronecker-factorized baselines. On synthetic stream-mixing tasks, go-$m$HC achieves the minimum theoretical loss while converging up to $10\times$ faster. We validate our approach in a 30M parameter GPT-style language model. The expressivity, efficiency, and exactness of go-$m$HC offer a practical avenue for scaling $d$ as a new dimension of model capacity.

</details>


### [119] [Batched Contextual Reinforcement: A Task-Scaling Law for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02322)
*Bangji Yang,Hongbo Ma,Jiajun Fan,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BCR是一种通过批量上下文强化训练的单阶段范式，让模型在共享上下文窗口中同时解决多个问题，仅基于每个实例的准确性进行奖励，从而在不降低推理质量的情况下显著减少令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型思维链推理方法存在令牌消耗过多、推理成本高昂的问题。现有的效率改进方法如显式长度惩罚、难度估计或多阶段课程学习要么会降低推理质量，要么需要复杂的训练流程。

Method: 提出批量上下文强化（BCR）方法：在训练过程中，让模型在共享上下文窗口中同时解决N个问题，仅基于每个实例的准确性进行奖励。这种结构修改创建了隐式令牌预算，无需显式长度监督。

Result: 1) 发现新的任务缩放规律：推理时并发问题数N增加，每个问题的令牌使用单调减少，准确性下降远小于基线；2) BCR挑战传统准确性-效率权衡，在标准单问题推理中实现"免费午餐"现象，在1.5B和4B模型上减少15.8%-62.6%令牌使用，同时在五个主要数学基准上保持或提高准确性；3) 模型自主消除冗余元认知循环；4) 隐式预算约束成功规避显式长度惩罚的对抗梯度和灾难性优化崩溃。

Conclusion: BCR证明简单的结构激励可以解锁大语言模型中潜在的高密度推理能力，提供了一种高度稳定、基于约束的长度控制替代方案，为高效推理提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models employing Chain-of-Thought reasoning achieve strong performance but suffer from excessive token consumption that inflates inference costs. Existing efficiency methods such as explicit length penalties, difficulty estimators, or multi-stage curricula either degrade reasoning quality or require complex training pipelines. We introduce Batched Contextual Reinforcement, a minimalist, single-stage training paradigm that unlocks efficient reasoning through a simple structural modification: training the model to solve N problems simultaneously within a shared context window, rewarded purely by per-instance accuracy. This formulation creates an implicit token budget that yields several key findings: (1) We identify a novel task-scaling law: as the number of concurrent problems N increases during inference, per-problem token usage decreases monotonically while accuracy degrades far more gracefully than baselines, establishing N as a controllable throughput dimension. (2) BCR challenges the traditional accuracy-efficiency trade-off by demonstrating a "free lunch" phenomenon at standard single-problem inference. Across both 1.5B and 4B model families, BCR reduces token usage by 15.8% to 62.6% while consistently maintaining or improving accuracy across five major mathematical benchmarks. (3) Qualitative analyses reveal emergent self-regulated efficiency, where models autonomously eliminate redundant metacognitive loops without explicit length supervision. (4) Crucially, we empirically demonstrate that implicit budget constraints successfully circumvent the adversarial gradients and catastrophic optimization collapse inherent to explicit length penalties, offering a highly stable, constraint-based alternative for length control. These results prove BCR practical, showing simple structural incentives unlock latent high-density reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Know Your Streams: On the Conceptualization, Characterization, and Generation of Intentional Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01440)
*Andrea Maldonado,Christian Imenkamp,Hendrik Reiter,Thomas Seidl,Wilhelm Hasselbring,Martin Werner,Agnes Koschmider*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文针对流式过程挖掘（SPM）算法评估方法的不足，提出了新的流事件概念框架和原型生成器Stream of Intent，用于生成具有特定特征的流事件数据以支持针对性基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和传感器驱动系统产生了连续实时的流事件数据，但SPM算法评估仍依赖静态日志或人工流化数据，无法反映真实流数据的复杂性（乱序事件、并发活动、不完整案例、概念漂移等）。

Method: 1. 全面回顾数据流文献，识别SPM领域未反映的流特征；2. 基于此扩展流事件的概念基础；3. 开发Stream of Intent原型生成器，生成具有特定特征的流事件数据。

Result: 评估显示Stream of Intent在生成可重复、具有特定意图的流事件数据方面表现优异，能够支持SPM领域的针对性基准测试和自适应算法开发。

Conclusion: 论文填补了SPM算法评估的空白，提供了反映真实世界流数据复杂性的概念框架和生成工具，有助于推动流式过程挖掘算法的发展。

Abstract: The shift toward IoT-enabled, sensor-driven systems has transformed how operational data is generated, favoring continuous, real-time event streams (ES) over static event logs. This evolution presents new challenges for Streaming Process Mining (SPM), which must cope with out-of-order events, concurrent activities, incomplete cases, and concept drifts. Yet, the evaluation of SPM algorithms remains rooted in outdated practices, relying on static logs or artificially streamified data that fail to reflect the complexities of real-world streams. To address this gap, we first perform a comprehensive review of data stream literature to identify stream characteristics currently not reflected in the SPM community. Next, we use this information to extend the conceptual foundation for ES. Finally, we propose Stream of Intent, a prototype generator to produce ES with specific features. Our evaluation shows excellence in producing reproducible, intentional ES for targeted benchmarking and adaptive algorithm development in SPM.

</details>


### [121] [CogPic: A Multimodal Dataset for Early Cognitive Impairment Assessment via Picture Description Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01626)
*Liuyu Wu,Rui Feng,Jie Li,Wentao Xiang,Yi Zhang,Yin Cao,Siyang Song,Xiao Gu,Jianqing Li,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CogPic是一个用于认知障碍检测的多模态基准数据集，包含574名参与者的严格同步音频、视觉和语言数据，通过专家临床评估建立可靠诊断标签，是目前同类数据集中规模最大、模态最丰富、评估最严谨的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于多媒体技术的认知状态自动评估面临主要挑战：缺乏大规模、严格同步且经过临床验证的多模态数据集，这阻碍了认知障碍检测机器学习模型的稳健发展。

Method: 构建CogPic数据库：1）从574名参与者收集自然图片描述任务中的严格同步音频、视觉和语言数据；2）由临床神经心理学家进行详尽评估，通过全面临床共识将参与者分层到不同的认知组别；3）建立高度可靠的诊断基准真值。

Result: CogPic成为目前同类数据集中规模最大、模态最丰富、评估最严谨的数据集。通过在该数据集上进行广泛的基准实验，建立了异常稳健、无偏且具有临床泛化性的基础。

Conclusion: CogPic数据库填补了认知障碍检测领域多模态数据集的空白，为未来多媒体技术在自动认知健康评估领域的研究提供了可靠基础，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: The automated evaluation of cognitive status utilizing multimedia technologies presents a promising frontier in early dementia diagnosis. However, the development of robust machine learning models for cognitive impairment detection is frequently hindered by the scarcity of large-scale, strictly synchronized, and clinically validated multimodal datasets. To bridge this critical gap, we introduce the CogPic database, a comprehensive multimodal benchmark meticulously designed for fine-grained cognitive impairment detection. The dataset comprises strictly synchronized audio, visual, and linguistic data continuously collected from 574 participants during a naturalistic picture description task. To establish highly reliable diagnostic ground truth, expert clinical neuropsychologists conducted exhaustive evaluations, stratifying participants into distinct cognitive groups through a comprehensive clinical consensus. Consequently, CogPic stands as the largest, most modality-rich, and most meticulously evaluated dataset of its kind to date. By conducting extensive benchmark experiments on the CogPic dataset, we establish an exceptionally robust, unbiased, and clinically generalizable foundation to propel future multimedia research in automated cognitive health assessment. Detailed information and access application procedures for our CogPic database are available at https://cogpic.github.io/.

</details>


### [122] [GPU-RMQ: Accelerating Range Minimum Queries on Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01811)
*Lara Kreis,Justus Henneberg,Valentin Henkys,Felix Schuhknecht,Bertil Schmidt*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: GPU-RMQ：一种基于层次结构的新型GPU范围最小值查询方法，通过结合光线追踪核心和CUDA核心，显著提升大数组查询吞吐量，同时降低内存占用和索引构建时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GPU的范围最小值查询方法存在严重内存开销、高索引构建时间和低查询吞吐量问题，特别是对于大数组（如≥2^29），现有方法超出GPU内存容量，限制了实际应用。

Method: 采用分层方法：首先以高度并行方式在原始数组上构建范围最小值摘要的层次结构；查询时仅处理层次结构的相关部分，通过优化的海量并行扫描操作；设计为混合架构，在不同层次上同时利用光线追踪核心和CUDA核心处理查询。

Result: GPU-RMQ在查询吞吐量方面显著优于现有方法，特别是对大数组：比LCA高约8倍，比RTXRMQ高约17倍，比优化CPU方法高约4800倍；内存占用显著降低，索引构建时间快两个数量级。

Conclusion: GPU-RMQ通过创新的分层设计和混合架构，有效解决了现有GPU范围最小值查询方法的内存、构建时间和吞吐量问题，为大规模数组处理提供了实用高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Range minimum queries are frequently used in string processing and database applications including biological sequence analysis, document retrieval, and web search. Hence, various data structures have been proposed for improving their efficiency on both CPUs and GPUs.Recent work has also shown that hardware-accelerated ray tracing on modern NVIDIA RTX graphic cards can be exploited to answer range minimum queries by expressing queries as rays, which are fired into a scene of triangles representing minima of ranges at different granularities. While these approaches are promising, they suffer from at least one of three issues: severe memory overhead, high index construction time, and low query throughput. This renders these methods practically unusable on larger arrays: For example, the state-of-art GPU-based approaches LCA and RTXRMQ exceed the memory capacity of an NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU for input arrays of size >= 2^29. To tackle these problems, in this work, we present a new approach called GPU-RMQ which is based on a hierarchical approach. GPU-RMQ first constructs a hierarchy of range minimum summaries on top of the original array in a highly parallel fashion. For query answering, only the relevant portions of the hierarchy are then processed in an optimized massively-parallel scan operation. Additionally, GPU-RMQ is hybrid in design enabling the use of both ray tracing cores and CUDA cores across different levels of the hierarchy to handle queries. Our experimental evaluation shows that GPU-RMQ outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches in terms of query throughput especially for larger arrays while offering a significantly lower memory footprint and up to two orders-of-magnitude faster index construction. In particular, it achieves up to ~8x higher throughput than LCA, ~17x higher throughput than RTXRMQ, and up to ~4800x higher throughput compared to an optimized CPU-based approach.

</details>


### [123] [BBC: Improving Large-k Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search with a Bucket-based Result Collector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01960)
*Ziqi Yin,Gao Cong,Kai Zeng,Jinwei Zhu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出BBC方法，通过桶式结果收集器和定制重排序算法，提升量化ANN索引在大k值查询中的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻搜索方法在大k值查询（需要检索大量最近邻）时性能显著下降，这一问题在实际应用中普遍存在但研究不足

Method: 提出桶式结果收集器（BBC），包含两个核心组件：1）基于桶的结果缓冲区，按候选点与查询的距离组织桶，降低排序成本并提高缓存效率；2）针对不同类型量化方法的两种重排序算法，通过减少重排序候选数量或缓存缺失来加速重排序过程

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，BBC将现有量化ANN方法在大k值查询中的速度提升最高达3.8倍（recall@k = 0.95）

Conclusion: BBC方法有效解决了量化ANN索引在大k值查询中的性能瓶颈，通过优化候选维护和重排序过程显著提升了查询效率

Abstract: Although Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) search has been extensively studied, large-k ANN queries that aim to retrieve a large number of nearest neighbors remain underexplored, despite their numerous real-world applications. Existing ANN methods face significant performance degradation for such queries. In this work, we first investigate the reasons for the performance degradation of quantization-based ANN indexes: (1) the inefficiency of existing top-k collectors, which incurs significant overhead in candidate maintenance, and (2) the reduced pruning effectiveness of quantization methods, which leads to a costly re-ranking process. To address this, we propose a novel bucket-based result collector (BBC) to enhance the efficiency of existing quantization-based ANN indexes for large-k ANN queries. BBC introduces two key components: (1) a bucket-based result buffer that organizes candidates into buckets by their distances to the query. This design reduces ranking costs and improves cache efficiency, enabling high performance maintenance of a candidate superset and a lightweight final selection of top-k results. (2) two re-ranking algorithms tailored for different types of quantization methods, which accelerate their re-ranking process by reducing either the number of candidate objects to be re-ranked or cache misses. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that BBC accelerates existing quantization-based ANN methods by up to 3.8x at recall@k = 0.95 for large-k ANN queries.

</details>


### [124] [Optimizing Relational Queries over Array-Valued Data in Columnar Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01967)
*Maroua Zeblah,Etienne Couritas,Sarah Chlyah,Pierre Genevès,Nils Gesbert,Nabil Layaïda*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: A3D-RA：支持数组值属性的扩展关系代数，提供代数推理和优化框架，作为模块化、后端独立的优化层集成到现有分析数据库系统中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分析工作负载越来越多地结合关系数据和数组值属性。虽然列式数据库系统能够高效处理此类工作负载，但在优化关系运算符与数组操作交织的查询方面能力有限。

Method: 提出A3D-RA扩展关系代数，支持数组值属性；形式化其数据模型和语义；开发完整的等价保持转换规则，捕捉关系和数组运算符之间的成对交互；提出具有最优性保证的计划枚举策略，在非连接运算符上保持多项式复杂度；设计为模块化、后端独立的优化层。

Result: 在三个高性能引擎上的真实世界工作负载实验表明，所提出的代数优化层能够带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: A3D-RA为处理混合关系-数组工作负载提供了有效的代数优化框架，能够集成到现有分析数据库系统中，显著提升查询性能。

Abstract: Modern analytical workloads increasingly combine relational data with array-valued attributes. While columnar database systems efficiently process such workloads, their ability to optimize queries that interleave relational operators with array manipulations remains limited. This paper introduces A3D-RA, an extended relational algebra supporting array-valued attributes, together with a comprehensive framework for algebraic reasoning and optimization. We formalize its data model and semantics, develop a complete set of equivalence-preserving transformation rules capturing pairwise interactions between relational and array operators, and propose a plan enumeration strategy with an optimality guarantee that remains polynomial in all non-join operators. We design A3D-RA as a modular, backend-independent optimization layer that can be instantiated over existing analytical database systems. Experimental results across three high-performance engines on a real-world workload show consistent performance gains enabled by the proposed algebraic optimization layer.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Semantically Annotated Multimodal Dataset for RF Interpretation and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01433)
*Steve Blandino,Jelena Senic,Raied Caromi,Samuel Berweger,Anuraag Bodi,Camillo Gentile,Nada Golmie*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出新型多模态数据集，结合RF测量与视觉/激光雷达数据，解决无线建模和RF AI中高质量测量数据集缺乏的问题，支持前向（从视觉预测RF热图）和逆向（从RF推断场景语义）任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无线建模和RF AI的主要限制在于缺乏高质量、基于测量的数据集，这些数据集需要将RF信号与其物理环境联系起来。现有的RF热图虽然高维复杂，但缺乏几何和语义上下文，限制了监督机器学习模型的发展。

Method: 提出新型多模态数据集，结合RF测量与辅助模态（高分辨率相机和激光雷达），实现精确的空间和时间配准，创建数字副本进行体素级标注。数据收集涵盖多样室内外环境，包括静态和动态场景，以及从行走到细微手势的人类活动。

Result: 该数据集将支持变革性AI研究，包括两个关键任务：1）前向问题：从视觉数据预测RF热图，革新无线系统设计；2）逆向问题：从RF信号推断场景语义，创建新型RF感知能力。

Conclusion: 通过构建连接RF信号与物理环境的多模态数据集，解决了RF AI发展的关键瓶颈，为无线系统设计和RF感知开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Current limitations in wireless modeling and radio frequency (RF)-based AI are primarily driven by a lack of high-quality, measurement-based datasets that connect RF signals to their physical environments. RF heatmaps, the typical form of such data, are high-dimensional and complex but lack the geometric and semantic context needed for interpretation, constraining the development of supervised machine learning models. To address this bottleneck, we propose a new class of multimodal datasets that combines RF measurements with auxiliary modalities like high-resolution cameras and lidar to bridge the gap between RF signals and their physical causes. The proposed data collection will span diverse indoor and outdoor environments, featuring both static and dynamic scenarios, including human activities ranging from walking to subtle gestures. By achieving precise spatial and temporal co-registration and creating digital replicas for voxel-level annotation, this dataset will enable transformative AI research. Key tasks include the forward problem of predicting RF heatmaps from visual data to revolutionize wireless system design, and the inverse problem of inferring scene semantics from RF signals, creating a new form of RF-based perception.

</details>


### [126] [A Unified Performance-Cost Landscape of Parallel p-bit Ising Machines Based on Update Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01564)
*Naoya Onizawa,Takahiro Hanyu*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 同步与异步p-bit伊辛机在延迟、复用和精度约束下的性能-成本统一框架，提出时间复用同步控制方案，实现硬件成本减半的同时保持解质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行p-bit伊辛机在组合优化中具有潜力，但其可扩展性受到更新同步、硬件延迟和架构成本的限制，需要在实际约束下建立统一的性能-成本分析框架

Method: 建立统一性能-成本框架，分析同步和异步更新方案在有限延迟、时间复用p-bit重用和有限DAC精度下的表现，提出时间复用p-bit重用与结构化同步控制相结合的方法

Result: 同步架构在G-set MaxCut基准测试（800-2000节点）上以不到优化异步设计一半的硬件成本获得相当或更好的解质量；3-4位低分辨率DAC在适当退火时间下可达到接近最优解

Conclusion: 时间复用同步控制方法将统计正确性与物理资源解耦，使p-bit和DAC数量与复用因子成反比缩放，为实际约束下的可扩展概率计算硬件提供了实用设计指南

Abstract: Parallel p-bit Ising machines are a promising platform for fast and energy-efficient combinatorial optimization, but their scalability depends on update synchronization, hardware delay, and architectural cost. In this work, we establish a unified performance-cost framework by analyzing synchronous and asynchronous update schemes under realistic constraints, including finite delay, time-multiplexed p-bit reuse, and limited DAC precision. We show that synchronous updates are not inherently unstable but can exhibit oscillations under excessive simultaneity, while asynchronous updates require slower operation due to hardware delay. To address this trade-off, we introduce time-multiplexed p-bit reuse with structured synchronous control, preserving correct annealing dynamics while reducing hardware requirements. This approach decouples statistical correctness from physical resources, enabling the number of p-bits and DACs to scale inversely with the reuse factor. As a result, synchronous architectures achieve comparable or better solution quality at less than half the hardware cost of optimized asynchronous designs on G-set MaxCut benchmarks (800-2000 nodes). We also show that low-resolution DACs (3-4 bits) are sufficient to reach near-optimal solutions when annealing time is properly adjusted. These findings provide practical design guidelines for scalable probabilistic computing hardware under realistic constraints.

</details>

### [127] [Just Verification of Mutual Exclusion Algorithms with (Non-)Blocking and (Non-)Atomic Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01269)
*Rob van Glabbeek,Bas Luttik,Myrthe Spronck*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 通过模型检验验证多种互斥算法的正确性，关注共享读写寄存器的原子性与非原子性，使用justness作为完整性准则消除伪反例


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证互斥算法的正确性是一个重要问题，特别是在共享读写寄存器通信环境下。现有方法需要合适的完整性准则来消除模型检验中的伪反例，而justness准则及其依赖的并发关系能够更好地建模不同寄存器工作假设

Method: 采用模型检验方法验证互斥算法，考虑原子和非原子共享读写寄存器。使用justness作为完整性准则来验证活性属性，该准则依赖于并发关系。研究了多种并发关系来建模不同的寄存器工作假设。通过执行示例展示算法违反正确性属性的情况，并提出改进建议

Result: 验证了多种互斥算法的正确性，展示了多个算法违反正确性属性的执行示例。在不同并发关系建模下，justness准则能够有效消除伪反例。针对发现的问题，提出了相应的算法改进建议

Conclusion: 模型检验是验证互斥算法正确性的有效方法，justness作为完整性准则能够处理活性属性的验证。不同的并发关系建模对寄存器工作假设有重要影响。研究揭示了现有算法中的缺陷，并提供了改进方向

Abstract: We verify the correctness of a variety of mutual exclusion algorithms through model checking. We look at algorithms where communication is via shared read/write registers, where those registers can be atomic or non-atomic. For the verification of liveness properties, it is necessary to assume a completeness criterion to eliminate spurious counterexamples. We use justness as completeness criterion. Justness depends on a concurrency relation; we consider several such relations, modelling different assumptions on the working of the shared registers. We present executions demonstrating the violation of correctness properties by several algorithms, and in some cases suggest improvements.

</details>


### [128] [Compositional Program Verification with Polynomial Functors in Dependent Type Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01303)
*C. B. Aberlé*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 基于多项式函子的组合式程序验证框架，在依赖类型理论中实现，使用多项式函子作为接口，Kleisli态射作为实现，依赖多项式编码规约，通过接线图实现组合验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个形式化的组合式程序验证框架，能够系统性地处理程序实现、接口和规约的组合，为并发和关系验证等更复杂场景提供理论基础。

Method: 使用多项式函子作为程序接口，Kleisli态射（自由单子单子）作为实现，依赖多项式编码前置/后置条件规约，通过接线图组合实现和验证，Mealy机提供组合式余代数操作语义。

Result: 建立了完整的组合验证框架，识别出底层抽象范畴结构为从规约到接口的幺半群函子，具有兼容的松弛幺半群预层自然变换，该框架已在Agda中形式化实现。

Conclusion: 该框架为组合式程序验证提供了坚实的理论基础，其范畴结构可推广到其他范畴、幺半群积等设置，包括并发和关系验证场景，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We present a framework for compositional program verification based on polynomial functors in dependent type theory. In this framework, polynomial functors serve as program interfaces, Kleisli morphisms for the free monad monad serve as implementations, and dependent polynomials encode pre/postcondition specifications. We show that implementations and their verifications compose via wiring diagrams, and that Mealy machines provide a compositional coalgebraic operational semantics. We identify the abstract categorical structure underlying this compositionality as a monoidal functor from specifications to interfaces with a compatible monoidal natural transformation of lax monoidal presheaves; this opens the door to generalizations to other categories, monoidal products, etc., including settings for concurrency and relational verification, which we sketch. As a proof-of-concept, the entire framework has been formalized in Agda.

</details>

### [129] [Computational Foundations for Strategic Coopetition: Formalizing Sequential Interaction and Reciprocity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01240)
*Vik Pant,Eric Yu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该技术报告扩展了战略竞合的计算基础，引入序列交互动态，将i*概念建模与博弈论互惠分析结合，开发了四种互惠机制，并在参数配置和iOS应用商店生态系统中验证了框架有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多利益相关者系统中战略竞合的持久合作机制，解决在没有约束性合同的情况下，合作如何通过时间持续的问题。将概念建模与博弈论分析结合，为人类利益相关者交互和多智能体计算系统提供理论基础。

Method: 开发了四种核心方法：(1)有界互惠响应函数，将伙伴偏差映射到有限条件响应；(2)记忆窗口历史跟踪，捕捉k个最近时期的认知限制；(3)结构互惠敏感性，源自相互依赖矩阵，其中行为响应被结构依赖性放大；(4)信任门控互惠，信任调节互惠响应。采用单轴处理，智能体在单一连续体上选择合作水平。

Result: 在15,625个参数配置上验证显示稳健的互惠效应，所有六个行为目标均超过阈值：合作涌现(97.5%)、背叛惩罚(100%)、宽恕动态(87.9%)、不对称分化(100%)、信任-互惠交互(100%)和有界响应(100%)。在苹果iOS应用商店生态系统(2008-2024)的实证验证达到84.3%适用点，重现了五个生态系统阶段的合作模式。统计显著性p<0.001，Cohen's d=1.57。

Conclusion: 该报告完成了基础系列(TR-1至TR-4)，证明了战略竞合计算框架在序列交互中的有效性。框架为理解和设计多利益相关者系统中的持久合作提供了理论基础，并已通过大规模参数配置和实际生态系统验证。扩展系列(TR-5至TR-8)将引入双轴处理，其中合作和竞争是独立维度。

Abstract: Strategic coopetition in multi-stakeholder systems requires understanding how cooperation persists through time without binding contracts. This technical report extends computational foundations for strategic coopetition to sequential interaction dynamics, bridging conceptual modeling (i* framework) with game-theoretic reciprocity analysis. We develop: (1) bounded reciprocity response functions mapping partner deviations to finite conditional responses, (2) memory-windowed history tracking capturing cognitive limitations over k recent periods, (3) structural reciprocity sensitivity derived from interdependence matrices where behavioral responses are amplified by structural dependencies, and (4) trust-gated reciprocity where trust modulates reciprocity responses. The framework applies to both human stakeholder interactions and multi-agent computational systems. Comprehensive validation across 15,625 parameter configurations demonstrates robust reciprocity effects, with all six behavioral targets exceeding thresholds: cooperation emergence (97.5%), defection punishment (100%), forgiveness dynamics (87.9%), asymmetric differentiation (100%), trust-reciprocity interaction (100%), and bounded responses (100%). Empirical validation using the Apple iOS App Store ecosystem (2008-2024) achieves 43/51 applicable points (84.3%), reproducing documented cooperation patterns across five ecosystem phases. Statistical significance confirmed at p < 0.001 with Cohen's d = 1.57. This report concludes the Foundations Series (TR-1 through TR-4) adopting uniaxial treatment where agents choose cooperation levels along a single continuum. Companion work on interdependence (arXiv:2510.18802), trust (arXiv:2510.24909), and collective action (arXiv:2601.16237) has been prepublished. Extensions Series (TR-5 through TR-8) introduces biaxial treatment where cooperation and competition are independent dimensions.

</details>

### [130] [CIVIC: Cooperative Immersion Via Intelligent Credit-sharing in DRL-Powered Metaverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02284)
*Amr Aboeleneen,Mohamed Abdallah,Aiman Erbad,Amr Salem*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CIVIC框架通过智能信用共享优化多个Metaverse服务提供商之间的资源分配，提升用户沉浸体验，使用深度强化学习解决NP-hard资源分配问题，显著提高请求完成率和服务质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Metaverse面临复杂的资源分配挑战，包括多样化的虚拟环境、数字孪生、动态用户需求以及严格的沉浸体验要求。现有方法未能有效整合多个服务提供商之间的资源协作，导致资源利用效率低下，用户沉浸体验受限。

Method: 提出CIVIC（Cooperative Immersion Via Intelligent Credit-sharing）框架，整合虚拟环境渲染、数字孪生同步、信用共享和沉浸感知资源供应。将资源分配问题建模为两个NP-hard挑战：非合作设置（MSPs独立运营）和合作设置（使用通用信用池进行动态资源共享）。采用深度强化学习来调优资源和管理合作MSPs。

Result: CIVIC实现了12-36%更高的请求完成率，23-70%更高的履行率，20-60%更多的服务客户，以及高达51%更公平的请求分配，同时保持具有竞争力的成本。实验证明框架在动态负载条件和意外需求激增下具有韧性、适应性和稳健性能。

Conclusion: CIVIC框架通过智能信用共享和深度强化学习优化多MSP协作，有效解决了Metaverse资源分配挑战，显著提升了用户沉浸体验和系统性能，适用于现实世界的分布式Metaverse基础设施。

Abstract: The Metaverse faces complex resource allocation challenges due to diverse Virtual Environments (VEs), Digital Twins (DTs), dynamic user demands, and strict immersion needs. This paper introduces CIVIC (Cooperative Immersion Via Intelligent Credit-sharing), a novel framework optimizing resource sharing among multiple Metaverse Service Providers (MSPs) to enhance user immersion. Unlike existing methods, CIVIC integrates VE rendering, DT synchronization, credit sharing, and immersion-aware provisioning within a cooperative multi-MSP model. The resource allocation problem is formulated as two NP-hard challenges: a non-cooperative setting where MSPs operate independently and a cooperative setting utilizing a General Credit Pool (GCP) for dynamic resource sharing. Using Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for tuning resources and managing cooperating MSPs, CIVIC achieves 12-36% higher request completion, 23-70% higher fulfillment rates, 20-60% more served clients, and up to 51% more fairly distributed requests, all with competitive costs. Extensive experiments demonstrate CIVIC's resilience, adaptability, and robust performance under dynamic load conditions and unexpected demand surges, making it suitable for real-world distributed Metaverse infrastructures.

</details>
